async function sha256(msg){const b=new TextEncoder().encode(msg);const h=await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256",b);return Array.from(new Uint8Array(h)).map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join("");}
async function wompiSig(ref,cents){return sha256(`${ref}${cents}COP${WOMPI_SK}`);}

// ══════════ WOMPI HELPERS ════════════════════════════════════════════
function injectWompiWidget(containerId, opts){
  const c=document.getElementById(containerId);if(!c)return;c.innerHTML="";
  const f=document.createElement("form");const s=document.createElement("script");
  s.src="https://checkout.wompi.co/widget.js";s.setAttribute("data-render","button");
  s.setAttribute("data-public-key",WOMPI_PK);s.setAttribute("data-currency","COP");
  s.setAttribute("data-amount-in-cents",String(opts.cents));
  s.setAttribute("data-reference",opts.ref);s.setAttribute("data-signature:integrity",opts.sig);
  s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:email",opts.email||"");s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:full-name",opts.name||"");
  if(opts.phone)s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:phone-number",opts.phone);
  s.setAttribute("data-redirect-url",opts.redirectUrl||window.location.href);
  f.appendChild(s);c.appendChild(f);
}

function useWompiCheckout(containerRef, deps, opts){
  useEffect(()=>{
    if(!opts.cents||!opts.ref||!opts.sig)return;
    const t=setTimeout(()=>{
      if(containerRef.current){
        containerRef.current.innerHTML="";
        const f=document.createElement("form");const s=document.createElement("script");
        s.src="https://checkout.wompi.co/widget.js";s.setAttribute("data-render","button");
        s.setAttribute("data-public-key",WOMPI_PK);s.setAttribute("data-currency","COP");
        s.setAttribute("data-amount-in-cents",String(opts.cents));
        s.setAttribute("data-reference",opts.ref);s.setAttribute("data-signature:integrity",opts.sig);
        if(opts.email)s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:email",opts.email);
        if(opts.name)s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:full-name",opts.name);
        s.setAttribute("data-redirect-url",window.location.href);
        f.appendChild(s);containerRef.current.appendChild(f);
      }
    },350);
    return()=>clearTimeout(t);
  },deps);
}

// ══════════ CLAUDE AI ═════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function callAI(messages, system="", max=1000){
  try{
    const r=await fetch("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages",{
      method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},
      body:JSON.stringify({model:"claude-sonnet-4-20250514",max_tokens:max,system,messages})
    });
    const d=await r.json();
    return d.content?.map(b=>b.text||"").join("")||"";
  }catch(e){return "Error de conexión con IA.";}
}

// ══════════ STORAGE HOOK ══════════════════════════════════════════════
function useDB(){
  const[db,setDB]=useState(DB0);
  const upd=useCallback((fn)=>setDB(p=>{const n=typeof fn==="function"?fn(p):fn;return n;}),[]);
  return[db,upd];
}

// ══════════ RESPONSIVE ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function useRWD(){
  const[w,setW]=useState(typeof window!=="undefined"?window.innerWidth:1200);
  useEffect(()=>{const h=()=>setW(window.innerWidth);window.addEventListener("resize",h);return()=>window.removeEventListener("resize",h);},[]);
  return{sm:w<640,md:w<1024,lg:w>=1024,w};
}

// ══════════ FORMAT HELPERS ═══════════════════════════════════════════
const fmtBytes=(mb)=>{if(mb>=1024)return`${(mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB`;return`${mb} MB`;};
const fmtCOP=(n)=>n===0?"Gratis":`$${n.toLocaleString("es-CO")} COP`;
const fmtPct=(used,total)=>total?Math.min(Math.round(used/total*100),100):0;
const planColor=(id)=>HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===id)?.color||"#7850ff";
const planName=(id)=>HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===id)?.name||id;

// ══════════ GLOBAL CSS ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function GCss({C}){return(<style>{`
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Plus+Jakarta+Sans:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800;900&family=Clash+Display:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Syne:wght@400;600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500;600&display=swap');

input,select,textarea,button{font-family:'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif}a{text-decoration:none;color:inherit}
@keyframes fu{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(22px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}
@keyframes fi{from{opacity:0}to{opacity:1}}

@keyframes spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
@keyframes float{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0)}50%{transform:translateY(-8px)}}

@keyframes gradShift{0%{background-position:0% 50%}50%{background-position:100% 50%}100%{background-position:0% 50%}}

@keyframes shimmer{0%{background-position:-200% 0}100%{background-position:200% 0}}
.afu{animation:fu .44s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1) both}

// ══════════ UI PRIMITIVES ═════════════════════════════════════════════
function Spin(){
  const C=useC();
  return <span className="spin"style={{display:"inline-block",width:16,height:16,borderRadius:"50%",border:`2px solid ${C.b3}`,borderTopColor:C.p}}/>;
}

function Btn({children,onClick,clr,outline,ghost,sm,xs,full,icon,disabled,sx={},type="button"}){
  const C=useC();
  const col=clr||C.p;
  const pad=xs?"4px 11px":sm?"8px 16px":"11px 24px";
  const fs=xs?10:sm?12:13;
  return(
    <button type={type} onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled}
      style={{display:"inline-flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",gap:6,
        padding:pad,borderRadius:11,fontWeight:700,fontSize:fs,letterSpacing:.2,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",
        border:outline||ghost?`1.5px solid ${outline?col:C.b}`:"none",
        background:outline||ghost?"transparent":col,
        color:outline?col:ghost?C.tm:"#fff",
        cursor:disabled?"not-allowed":"pointer",opacity:disabled?0.45:1,
        width:full?"100%":"auto",
        boxShadow:!outline&&!ghost&&!disabled?`0 3px 16px ${col}45`:"none",
        transition:"all .18s",...sx}}>
      {icon&&<span style={{fontSize:fs+2,flexShrink:0,lineHeight:1}}>{icon}</span>}{children}
    </button>
  );
}

function Inp({label,value,onChange,type="text",placeholder,hint,req,sx={},icon,multi,rows=3,readOnly}){
  const C=useC(),[f,sf]=useState(false);
  const s={background:C.g2,border:`1.5px solid ${f?C.p:C.b2}`,borderRadius:11,
    padding:icon?"10px 14px 10px 40px":"10px 14px",color:C.t,fontSize:13,
    outline:"none",transition:"all .18s",width:"100%"};
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:4,...sx}}>
      {label&&<label style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8}}>{label}{req&&<span style={{color:C.red,marginLeft:2}}>*</span>}</label>}
      <div style={{position:"relative"}}>
        {icon&&<span style={{position:"absolute",left:12,top:"50%",transform:"translateY(-50%)",pointerEvents:"none",fontSize:15}}>{icon}</span>}
        {multi?<textarea value={value} onChange={e=>onChange(e.target.value)} rows={rows} placeholder={placeholder} style={{...s,resize:"vertical"}} onFocus={()=>sf(true)} onBlur={()=>sf(false)} readOnly={readOnly}/>
        :<input type={type} value={value} onChange={e=>onChange(e.target.value)} placeholder={placeholder} style={s} onFocus={()=>sf(true)} onBlur={()=>sf(false)} readOnly={readOnly}/>}
      </div>
      {hint&&<div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,lineHeight:1.5}}>{hint}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

function Sel({label,value,onChange,opts,sx={}}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:4,...sx}}>
      {label&&<label style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8}}>{label}</label>}
      <select value={value} onChange={e=>onChange(e.target.value)}
        style={{background:C.g2,border:`1.5px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:11,padding:"10px 14px",color:C.t,fontSize:13,cursor:"pointer",width:"100%",outline:"none",appearance:"none"}}>
        {opts.map(o=><option key={o.v??o} value={o.v??o}>{o.l??o}</option>)}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

function Bdg({children,clr,dot,sm,sx={}}){
  const C=useC();const col=clr||C.p;
  return(
    <span style={{display:"inline-flex",alignItems:"center",gap:4,fontSize:sm?9:10,fontWeight:700,
      color:col,background:`${col}16`,border:`1px solid ${col}28`,
      padding:sm?"2px 7px":"3px 9px",borderRadius:99,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:.8,whiteSpace:"nowrap",...sx}}>
      {dot&&<span className="pulse"style={{width:5,height:5,borderRadius:"50%",background:col,flexShrink:0}}/>}
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

function Card({children,sx={},onClick,glass}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <div onClick={onClick} style={{background:glass?C.over:C.card,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:18,
      backdropFilter:"blur(8px)",WebkitBackdropFilter:"blur(8px)",
      boxShadow:C.isDark?"0 4px 28px rgba(0,0,0,.32)":C.sh,
      transition:"all .22s",cursor:onClick?"pointer":"default",...sx}}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function Modal({title,sub,onClose,children,w=600}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <div style={{position:"fixed",inset:0,background:C.isDark?"rgba(0,0,0,.88)":"rgba(10,0,50,.7)",
      zIndex:600,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",
      backdropFilter:"blur(14px)",padding:16,overflowY:"auto"}}
      onClick={e=>e.target===e.currentTarget&&onClose()}>
      <div className="asi"style={{background:C.g1,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:22,
        width:"100%",maxWidth:w,maxHeight:"92vh",overflow:"auto",
        boxShadow:"0 32px 90px rgba(0,0,0,.55)"}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"flex-start",justifyContent:"space-between",
          padding:"20px 24px",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,
          position:"sticky",top:0,background:C.g1,zIndex:1,borderRadius:"22px 22px 0 0"}}>
          <div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:17,fontWeight:700,color:C.t}}>{title}</div>
            {sub&&<div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginTop:2}}>{sub}</div>}
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose}style={{width:34,height:34,borderRadius:9,background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,
            color:C.tm,fontSize:17,cursor:"pointer",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",
            transition:"all .15s",flexShrink:0,marginLeft:10}}
            onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.background=`${C.red}22`;e.currentTarget.style.color=C.red;}}
            onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.background=C.g3;e.currentTarget.style.color=C.tm;}}>✕</button>
        </div>
        <div style={{padding:"22px 24px"}}>{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatCard({label,val,sub,icon,clr,trend,pct}){
  const C=useC(),col=clr||C.p;
  return(
    <Card sx={{padding:"18px 20px",flex:1,minWidth:140,position:"relative",overflow:"hidden"}}>
      <div style={{position:"absolute",right:-6,top:-6,fontSize:52,opacity:.06,lineHeight:1}}>{icon}</div>
      <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,marginBottom:6}}>{label}</div>
      <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:26,fontWeight:900,color:col,lineHeight:1,marginBottom:3}}>{val}</div>
      {sub&&<div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{sub}</div>}
      {trend!==undefined&&<div style={{fontSize:10,marginTop:4,fontWeight:700,color:trend>=0?C.lime:C.red}}>{trend>=0?"↑":"↓"}{Math.abs(trend)}% mes</div>}
      {pct!==undefined&&(
        <div style={{marginTop:8}}>
          <div style={{height:3,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}>
            <div style={{height:"100%",width:`${pct}%`,background:pct>80?C.red:pct>60?C.gold:col,borderRadius:99,transition:"width 1s"}}/>
          </div>
          <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,marginTop:3}}>{pct}% usado</div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

function Toggle({on,onChange,label}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <label style={{display:"inline-flex",alignItems:"center",gap:9,cursor:"pointer",userSelect:"none"}}>
      <div onClick={()=>onChange(!on)}
        style={{width:44,height:24,borderRadius:99,background:on?C.p:C.b2,position:"relative",transition:"all .22s",flexShrink:0,boxShadow:on?`0 0 10px ${C.p}60`:"none"}}>
        <div style={{width:18,height:18,borderRadius:"50%",background:"#fff",position:"absolute",top:3,left:on?23:3,transition:"left .22s",boxShadow:"0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.28)"}}/>
      </div>
      {label&&<span style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>{label}</span>}
    </label>
  );
}

function AvatarCircle({name,size=38,clr}){
  const C=useC();const col=clr||C.p;
  const ini=(name||"?").split(" ").slice(0,2).map(w=>w[0]||"").join("").toUpperCase();
  return(
    <div style={{width:size,height:size,borderRadius:size*.28,
      background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${col},${col}aa)`,
      border:`2px solid ${col}50`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",
      fontSize:size*.3,fontWeight:800,color:"#fff",flexShrink:0,userSelect:"none",letterSpacing:-1}}>
      {ini}
    </div>
  );
}

function TBtn({isDark,toggle}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <button onClick={toggle}style={{width:38,height:38,borderRadius:10,background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,
      display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:16,cursor:"pointer",flexShrink:0,transition:"all .18s"}}>
      {isDark?"☀️":"🌙"}
    </button>
  );
}

function Bar({used,total,clr}){
  const C=useC();const pct=fmtPct(used,total);const col=clr||(pct>80?C.red:pct>60?C.gold:C.lime);
  return(
    <div style={{width:"100%"}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:3}}>
        <span>{fmtBytes(used)} / {fmtBytes(total)}</span><span style={{fontWeight:700,color:col}}>{pct}%</span>
      </div>
      <div style={{height:5,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}>
        <div style={{height:"100%",width:`${pct}%`,background:`linear-gradient(90deg,${col},${col}cc)`,borderRadius:99,transition:"width 1.2s",boxShadow:`0 0 8px ${col}80`}}/>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Divider({label}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10}}>
      <div style={{flex:1,height:1,background:C.b}}/>
      {label&&<span style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,fontWeight:700,letterSpacing:1,textTransform:"uppercase",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{label}</span>}
      <div style={{flex:1,height:1,background:C.b}}/>
    </div>
  );
}

function Tabs({tabs,active,onChange}){
  const C=useC();
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:2,background:C.g3,padding:4,borderRadius:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
      {tabs.map(t=>(
        <button key={t.id} onClick={()=>onChange(t.id)}
          style={{flex:1,padding:"8px 12px",borderRadius:9,border:"none",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",
            fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",transition:"all .18s",
            background:active===t.id?C.p:"transparent",
            color:active===t.id?"#fff":C.tm,
            boxShadow:active===t.id?`0 2px 10px ${C.p}50`:"none"}}>
          {t.ic&&<span style={{marginRight:5}}>{t.ic}</span>}{t.l}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

  "pronunciation":"Pronunciación con IA","achievements":"Logros & XP","hosting":"Mi Hosting & Espacio",
  "billing":"Planes & Pagos","profile":"Mi Perfil","youtube":"YouTube & Clases",
  "prof-dash":"Dashboard Docente","prof-classroom":"Mi Aula Virtual","prof-students":"Mis Alumnos",
  "prof-sessions":"Mis Sesiones","prof-materials":"Mis Materiales","prof-earnings":"Mis Ingresos",
  "admin-dash":"Dashboard General","admin-users":"Gestión de Usuarios","admin-hosting":"Panel del Servidor",
  "admin-subjects":"Materias","admin-lessons":"Lecciones","admin-sessions":"Sesiones Live",
  "admin-materials":"Materiales","admin-announcements":"Anuncios","admin-payments":"Pagos Wompi",
  "admin-db":"Base de Datos Interna","admin-apis":"APIs & Servicios","admin-ai":"Generador IA",
  "admin-youtube":"YouTube Studio Admin","admin-stats":"Estadísticas & Analytics",
  "dev-dash":"Dev Dashboard","dev-sdk":"SDK & Code Editor","dev-apis":"APIs & Endpoints",
  "dev-webhooks":"Webhooks & Events","dev-youtube":"YouTube API Studio","dev-logs":"System Logs",
  "dev-wompi":"Wompi Sandbox & Testing",
};

// ══════════ AUTH PAGE ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function AuthPage({db,upd,onLogin,isDark,toggleTheme}){
  const C=useC();
  const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[view,setView]=useState("login");
  const[step,setStep]=useState(1);
  const[busy,setBusy]=useState(null);
  const[err,setErr]=useState("");
  const[msg,setMsg]=useState("");
  // Login
  const[lE,setLE]=useState("");const[lP,setLP]=useState("");const[showPw,setShow]=useState(false);
  // Register
  const[rN,setRN]=useState("");const[rE,setRE]=useState("");const[rPw,setRPw]=useState("");const[rPw2,setRPw2]=useState("");
  const[rRole,setRRole]=useState("student");const[rLevel,setRLevel]=useState("A1");
  const[rPhone,setRPhone]=useState("");const[rGoal,setRGoal]=useState("general");
  const[rPlan,setRPlan]=useState("h_basic");const[regPay,setRegPay]=useState(false);
  const[wompiSigVal,setWompiSig]=useState("");const[wompiRef,setWompiRef]=useState("");

  const OAUTHC={google:"#ea4335",facebook:"#1877f2",outlook:"#0072c6"};

  const doLogin=async(e)=>{
    e?.preventDefault();setBusy("login");setErr("");
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,600));
    const u=db.users.find(x=>x.email.toLowerCase()===lE.toLowerCase()&&x.pw===lP);
    if(u)onLogin(u);else{setErr("Correo o contraseña incorrectos.");setBusy(null);}
  };

  const doOAuth=async(prov)=>{
    setBusy(prov);setErr("");
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,1000));
    const mails={google:"googleuser@gmail.com",facebook:"fbuser@facebook.com",outlook:"outlookuser@outlook.com"};
    const em=mails[prov];
    const ex=db.users.find(u=>u.email===em);
    if(ex){onLogin(ex);return;}
    const id=Math.max(0,...db.users.map(u=>u.id))+1;
    const nu={id,email:em,pw:"",role:"student",name:prov==="google"?"Usuario Google":prov==="facebook"?"Usuario Facebook":"Usuario Outlook",av:prov.slice(0,2).toUpperCase(),bio:"",xp:0,streak:0,level:"A1",plan:"h_free",planExp:"",storage:500,storageUsed:0,bandwidth:1,bwUsed:0,provider:prov,joined:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],phone:"",apiKey:"",webhookUrl:"",subdomains:[],active:true,verified:false};
    upd(p=>({...p,users:[...p.users,nu]}));onLogin(nu);
  };

  const doReg=async()=>{
    if(step===1){
      if(!rN||!rE||!rPw){setErr("Completa todos los campos.");return;}
      if(rPw!==rPw2){setErr("Las contraseñas no coinciden.");return;}
      if(db.users.find(u=>u.email.toLowerCase()===rE.toLowerCase())){setErr("Este correo ya está registrado.");return;}
      setErr("");setStep(2);return;
    }
    if(step===2){setErr("");setStep(3);return;}
    // step 3 = plan
    const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===rPlan);
    if(plan&&plan.price>0){
      // init Wompi
      setBusy("wompi");
      const ref=`SF-reg-${rPlan}-${Date.now()}`;
      const sig=await wompiSig(ref,plan.cents);
      setWompiRef(ref);setWompiSig(sig);setRegPay(true);setBusy(null);
      return;
    }
    await createUser("h_free");
  };

  const createUser=async(planId)=>{
    setBusy("reg");
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,800));
    const id=Math.max(0,...db.users.map(u=>u.id))+1;
    const av=rN.split(" ").map(w=>w[0]).join("").toUpperCase().slice(0,2);
    const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===planId)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];
    const apiKey=`sk-sf-${id}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10)}`;
    const exp=new Date();exp.setMonth(exp.getMonth()+1);
    const nu={id,email:rE,pw:rPw,role:rRole,name:rN,av,bio:"",xp:0,streak:0,level:rRole==="student"?rLevel:"B2",plan:planId,planExp:exp.toISOString().split("T")[0],storage:plan.storage,storageUsed:0,bandwidth:plan.bandwidth,bwUsed:0,provider:"email",joined:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],phone:rPhone,apiKey,webhookUrl:"",subdomains:[`${rE.split("@")[0]}.speakfluently.co`],active:true,verified:false};
    upd(p=>({...p,users:[...p.users,nu],subdomains:[...p.subdomains,{id:Date.now(),domain:nu.subdomains[0],userId:id,status:"active",type:rRole,ssl:true,created:nu.joined}]}));
    setMsg("✅ ¡Cuenta creada! Bienvenido/a a Speak Fluently 🎉");
    setBusy(null);setTimeout(()=>onLogin(nu),1400);
  };

  // Wompi step
  useEffect(()=>{
    if(!regPay||!wompiSigVal)return;
    const t=setTimeout(()=>{
      const c=document.getElementById("reg-wompi");if(!c)return;c.innerHTML="";
      const f=document.createElement("form");
      const s=document.createElement("script");
      s.src="https://checkout.wompi.co/widget.js";
      s.setAttribute("data-render","button");s.setAttribute("data-public-key",WOMPI_PK);
      s.setAttribute("data-currency","COP");
      const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===rPlan);
      s.setAttribute("data-amount-in-cents",String(plan?.cents||0));
      s.setAttribute("data-reference",wompiRef);s.setAttribute("data-signature:integrity",wompiSigVal);
      s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:email",rE);s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:full-name",rN);
      s.setAttribute("data-redirect-url",window.location.href);
      f.appendChild(s);c.appendChild(f);
    },400);
    return()=>clearTimeout(t);
  },[regPay,wompiSigVal]);

  const BG_DARK=`radial-gradient(ellipse at 15% 5%,${C.p}28 0%,transparent 55%),radial-gradient(ellipse at 85% 95%,${C.teal}18 0%,transparent 55%),${C.bg}`;
  const BG_LIGHT=`radial-gradient(ellipse at 15% 5%,${C.p}18 0%,transparent 55%),radial-gradient(ellipse at 85% 95%,${C.teal}12 0%,transparent 55%),${C.bg}`;
  const BG=C.isDark?BG_DARK:BG_LIGHT;

  const STEPS=[{n:1,l:"Datos"},{n:2,l:"Perfil"},{n:3,l:"Plan"}];

  return(
    <div style={{minHeight:"100vh",background:BG,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",overflow:"hidden",position:"relative"}}>
      <GCss C={C}/>
      <div style={{position:"fixed",inset:0,pointerEvents:"none",zIndex:0,overflow:"hidden"}}>
        <div style={{position:"absolute",top:"-20%",left:"-8%",width:550,height:550,borderRadius:"50%",background:`radial-gradient(${C.p}18,transparent 70%)`,filter:"blur(70px)"}}/>
        <div style={{position:"absolute",bottom:"-15%",right:"-5%",width:450,height:450,borderRadius:"50%",background:`radial-gradient(${C.teal}12,transparent 70%)`,filter:"blur(70px)"}}/>
      </div>

      {/* Navbar */}
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",padding:"18px 26px",position:"relative",zIndex:10}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10}}>
          <div className="float"style={{width:40,height:40,borderRadius:11,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:19,boxShadow:`0 4px 18px ${C.p}55`}}>✨</div>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:18,fontWeight:900,color:C.t}}>Speak Fluently</div>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
          {view==="login"&&<Btn sm ghost onClick={()=>{setView("register");setStep(1);setErr("");}}>Crear cuenta →</Btn>}
          {view==="register"&&<Btn sm ghost onClick={()=>{setView("login");setErr("");}}>← Ingresar</Btn>}
          <TBtn isDark={isDark} toggle={toggleTheme}/>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Body */}
      <div style={{flex:1,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",padding:"12px 16px",position:"relative",zIndex:10}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",width:"100%",maxWidth:1000,gap:0,borderRadius:26,overflow:"hidden",
          boxShadow:"0 32px 100px rgba(0,0,0,.45)",border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`}}>

          {/* Left panel */}
          {!sm&&(
            <div style={{flex:1,background:`linear-gradient(155deg,${C.p} 0%,#2d0b6e 55%,#060412 100%)`,padding:"44px 38px",display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",justifyContent:"space-between",minHeight:620,position:"relative",overflow:"hidden"}}>
              <div style={{position:"absolute",top:-50,right:-50,width:240,height:240,borderRadius:"50%",border:"1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06)"}}/>
              <div style={{position:"absolute",bottom:20,left:-70,width:280,height:280,borderRadius:"50%",border:"1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.04)"}}/>
              <div style={{position:"relative"}}>
                <div className="float"style={{width:54,height:54,borderRadius:16,background:"rgba(255,255,255,.12)",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:28,marginBottom:28,boxShadow:"0 0 28px rgba(255,255,255,.2)"}}>✨</div>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:38,fontWeight:900,color:"#fff",lineHeight:1.1,marginBottom:14}}>
                  {view==="register"?"Empieza hoy,\nhabla mañana.":"Bienvenido\nde vuelta."}
                </div>
                <div style={{fontSize:13,color:"rgba(255,255,255,.58)",lineHeight:1.9,marginBottom:32}}>
                  La academia de inglés con IA más<br/>avanzada de Colombia 🇨🇴
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
                  {[["☁️","Hosting en servidor propio"],["🤖","Tutor IA Claude 24/7"],["🎙️","Pronunciación con IA"],["📹","Clases en vivo"],["💳","Wompi — pagos 100% seguros"],["🏆","Certificados CEFR oficiales"],["🔌","APIs y webhooks propios"],["📊","Panel de analytics"]].map(([ic,t])=>(
                    <div key={t}style={{display:"flex",gap:11,alignItems:"center"}}>
                      <div style={{width:32,height:32,borderRadius:9,background:"rgba(255,255,255,.1)",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:15,flexShrink:0}}>{ic}</div>
                      <span style={{fontSize:12,color:"rgba(255,255,255,.75)",fontWeight:500}}>{t}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:"rgba(255,255,255,.25)",marginTop:18}}>
                fundacionstudy@gmail.com · Banco Popular 230560175432<br/>
                Regulado Wompi · Pagos seguros SFC Colombia
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Right form */}
          <div style={{width:sm?"100%":420,background:C.g1,padding:sm?"28px 22px":"40px 36px",display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",overflowY:"auto",maxHeight:"90vh",justifyContent:"center"}}>

            {sm&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,marginBottom:24,alignItems:"center"}}>
                <div style={{width:38,height:38,borderRadius:11,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:18}}>✨</div>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:18,fontWeight:900}}>Speak Fluently</div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Header */}
            <div style={{marginBottom:22}}>
              {view==="login"&&<><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:3}}>Iniciar sesión 👋</div><div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Ingresa a tu cuenta de la academia</div></>}
              {view==="register"&&<>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,marginBottom:14}}>
                  {STEPS.map(s=>(
                    <div key={s.n}style={{flex:1,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:4}}>
                      <div style={{height:3,borderRadius:99,background:step>=s.n?C.p:C.b,transition:"background .3s"}}/>
                      <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:step>=s.n?C.p:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1,textAlign:"center"}}>{s.l}</div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:3}}>
                  {step===1&&"Crear cuenta ✨"}
                  {step===2&&"Tu perfil 🎓"}
                  {step===3&&"Elige tu plan ☁️"}
                </div>
                <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Paso {step} de 3</div>
              </>}
              {view==="forgot"&&<><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:3}}>Recuperar acceso 🔑</div><div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Enviaremos un enlace a tu correo</div></>}
            </div>

            {/* OAuth */}
            {(view==="login"||(view==="register"&&step===1))&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8,marginBottom:18}}>
                {["google","facebook","outlook"].map(p=>(
                  <button key={p} onClick={()=>doOAuth(p)} disabled={!!busy} className="hov"
                    style={{width:"100%",padding:"10px",borderRadius:11,border:`1.5px solid ${C.b2}`,background:C.g2,color:C.t,fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,cursor:"pointer",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",gap:9,transition:"all .18s"}}
                    onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=OAUTHC[p];}}
                    onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.b2;}}>
                    {busy===p?<Spin/>:<>
                      <div style={{width:20,height:20,borderRadius:5,background:OAUTHC[p],display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:11,fontWeight:900,color:"#fff",flexShrink:0}}>
                        {p[0].toUpperCase()}
                      </div>
                      Continuar con {p[0].toUpperCase()+p.slice(1)}
                    </>}
                  </button>
                ))}
                <Divider label="o con correo"/>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Alerts */}
            {err&&<div className="afi"style={{background:`${C.red}10`,border:`1px solid ${C.red}28`,borderRadius:10,padding:"9px 13px",fontSize:12,color:C.red,marginBottom:14}}>{err}</div>}
            {msg&&<div className="afi"style={{background:`${C.lime}10`,border:`1px solid ${C.lime}28`,borderRadius:10,padding:"9px 13px",fontSize:12,color:C.lime,marginBottom:14}}>{msg}</div>}

            {/* LOGIN FORM */}
            {view==="login"&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
                <Inp label="Correo" value={lE} onChange={setLE} type="email" placeholder="tu@correo.com" icon="📧" req/>
                <div>
                  <Inp label="Contraseña" value={lP} onChange={setLP} type={showPw?"text":"password"} placeholder="••••••••" icon="🔒" req/>
                  <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginTop:5}}>
                    <button type="button" onClick={()=>setShow(!showPw)} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer",fontWeight:600,padding:0}}>{showPw?"Ocultar":"Mostrar"}</button>
                    <button type="button" onClick={()=>{setView("forgot");setErr("");}} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer",fontWeight:600,padding:0}}>¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?</button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button type="button" onClick={doLogin} disabled={!!busy} style={{padding:"13px",borderRadius:12,border:"none",background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.p2})`,color:"#fff",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,cursor:busy?"wait":"pointer",boxShadow:`0 4px 20px ${C.p}50`,opacity:busy?0.7:1,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",gap:8}}>
                  {busy==="login"?<><Spin/>&nbsp;Iniciando...</>:"Iniciar sesión →"}
                </button>

                {/* Demo */}
                <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:12,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,marginBottom:9}}>⚡ Acceso demo rápido</div>
                  <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:5}}>
                    {[{l:"👑 Admin",e:"fundacionstudy@gmail.com",p:"admin12345",c:C.gold},{l:"🎓 Estudiante",e:"ana@speakfluently.co",p:"pass123",c:C.p3},{l:"👨‍🏫 Profesor",e:"carlos@speakfluently.co",p:"prof123",c:C.mint},{l:"💻 Dev",e:"dev@speakfluently.co",p:"dev123",c:C.red}].map(a=>(
                      <button key={a.l} onClick={async()=>{setLE(a.e);setLP(a.p);setBusy("login");setErr("");await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,600));const u=db.users.find(x=>x.email.toLowerCase()===a.e.toLowerCase()&&x.pw===a.p);if(u)onLogin(u);else{setErr("Error de demo.");setBusy(null);}}}
                        style={{padding:"6px 8px",borderRadius:8,border:`1px solid ${a.c}30`,background:`${a.c}0e`,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:a.c,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"all .15s",textAlign:"left"}}
                        onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background=`${a.c}1e`}
                        onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background=`${a.c}0e`}>
                        {a.l}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div style={{textAlign:"center",fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>¿No tienes cuenta? <button type="button" onClick={()=>{setView("register");setStep(1);setErr("");}} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:12}}>Registrarse gratis</button></div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* REGISTER STEP 1 */}
            {view==="register"&&step===1&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
                <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:10}}>
                  <Inp label="Nombre completo" value={rN} onChange={setRN} req sx={{gridColumn:"1/-1"}}/>
                </div>
                <Inp label="Correo electrónico" value={rE} onChange={setRE} type="email" placeholder="tu@correo.com" req/>
                <Inp label="Contraseña" value={rPw} onChange={setRPw} type="password" placeholder="Mín. 8 caracteres" req/>
                <Inp label="Confirmar contraseña" value={rPw2} onChange={setRPw2} type="password" placeholder="Repite" req/>
                <Btn full onClick={doReg} clr={C.p}>Continuar →</Btn>
                <div style={{textAlign:"center",fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>¿Ya tienes cuenta? <button onClick={()=>{setView("login");setErr("");}} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:12}}>Ingresar</button></div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* REGISTER STEP 2 */}
            {view==="register"&&step===2&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
                <div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8,marginBottom:10}}>Soy un...</div>
                  <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:8}}>
                    {[{v:"student",l:"🎓 Estudiante",d:"Aprende inglés"},{v:"professor",l:"👨‍🏫 Profesor",d:"Enseña y cobra"}].map(r=>(
                      <div key={r.v} onClick={()=>setRRole(r.v)} style={{padding:"13px",borderRadius:12,border:`2px solid ${rRole===r.v?C.p:C.b}`,background:rRole===r.v?`${C.p}12`:C.g2,cursor:"pointer",transition:"all .18s"}}>
                        <div style={{fontSize:15,marginBottom:3}}>{r.l}</div>
                        <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{r.d}</div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
                {rRole==="student"&&(
                  <Sel label="Nivel de inglés" value={rLevel} onChange={setRLevel} opts={[{v:"A1",l:"A1 — Principiante"},{v:"A2",l:"A2 — Elemental"},{v:"B1",l:"B1 — Intermedio"},{v:"B2",l:"B2 — Intermedio Alto"}]}/>
                )}
                <div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8,marginBottom:8}}>Objetivo principal</div>
                  <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:7}}>
                    {[{v:"general",l:"🌍 Inglés general"},{v:"business",l:"💼 Trabajo"},{v:"ielts",l:"📋 IELTS"},{v:"travel",l:"✈️ Viajes"}].map(g=>(
                      <div key={g.v} onClick={()=>setRGoal(g.v)} style={{padding:"9px 11px",borderRadius:10,border:`1.5px solid ${rGoal===g.v?C.p:C.b}`,background:rGoal===g.v?`${C.p}10`:C.g2,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,fontWeight:500,color:rGoal===g.v?C.p2:C.tm,transition:"all .18s"}}>{g.l}</div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Inp label="Teléfono (opcional)" value={rPhone} onChange={setRPhone} type="tel" placeholder="+57 300 000 0000"/>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9}}>
                  <Btn outline onClick={()=>{setStep(1);setErr("");}}>← Atrás</Btn>
                  <Btn full onClick={doReg} clr={C.p}>Continuar →</Btn>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* REGISTER STEP 3 — PLAN */}
            {view==="register"&&step===3&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
                {!regPay?(
                  <>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:7}}>
                      {HOSTING_PLANS.filter(p=>p.id!=="h_prof"||(rRole==="professor")).filter(p=>!(p.id==="h_prof"&&rRole!=="professor")).map(p=>(
                        <div key={p.id} onClick={()=>setRPlan(p.id)}
                          style={{padding:"13px 15px",borderRadius:13,border:`2px solid ${rPlan===p.id?p.color:C.b}`,background:rPlan===p.id?`${p.color}12`:C.g2,cursor:"pointer",transition:"all .18s",display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>
                          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,alignItems:"center"}}>
                            <span style={{fontSize:20}}>{p.icon}</span>
                            <div>
                              <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,color:rPlan===p.id?p.color:C.t}}>{p.name}</div>
                              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{fmtBytes(p.storage)} · {p.bandwidth}GB BW/mes</div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:900,color:rPlan===p.id?p.color:C.tm,flexShrink:0}}>
                            {p.price===0?"Gratis":`$${(p.price/1000).toFixed(0)}k`}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <div style={{background:`${C.p}0a`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}20`,borderRadius:11,padding:11,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>
                      ☁️ Tu cuenta incluirá espacio real en el servidor de Speak Fluently con tu propio subdominio.
                    </div>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9}}>
                      <Btn outline onClick={()=>{setStep(2);setErr("");}}>← Atrás</Btn>
                      <Btn full onClick={doReg} clr={C.lime} disabled={!!busy}>{busy==="reg"?<><Spin/>&nbsp;Creando...</>:busy==="wompi"?<><Spin/>&nbsp;Cargando pago...</>:"✅ Crear mi cuenta"}</Btn>
                    </div>
                  </>
                ):(
                  <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14,textAlign:"center"}}>
                    <div style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:700}}>💳 Pago seguro con Wompi</div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>
                      Plan: <strong>{HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===rPlan)?.name}</strong> — <strong>${HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===rPlan)?.price.toLocaleString("es-CO")} COP/mes</strong>
                    </div>
                    <div id="reg-wompi"style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"center",minHeight:52}}/>
                    <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>🔒 Cifrado SSL · PCI-DSS · Wompi Colombia</div>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap",justifyContent:"center"}}>
                      {["PSE","Nequi","Bancolombia","Tarjeta","Efecty","Daviplata"].map(m=>(
                        <span key={m}style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,background:C.g3,padding:"3px 8px",borderRadius:5,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>{m}</span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={()=>{setRegPay(false);}} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>← Cambiar plan</button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* FORGOT */}
            {view==="forgot"&&(
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
                <Inp label="Correo electrónico" value={lE} onChange={setLE} type="email" placeholder="tu@correo.com"/>
                <Btn full onClick={async()=>{setBusy("forgot");await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,900));setMsg("Si el correo existe, recibirás el enlace.");setBusy(null);}} clr={C.p} disabled={!!busy}>{busy==="forgot"?<><Spin/>&nbsp;Enviando...</>:"📨 Enviar enlace"}</Btn>
                <button onClick={()=>{setView("login");setErr("");setMsg("");}} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:12,fontWeight:600}}>← Volver al login</button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"14px",fontSize:10,color:C.tf,position:"relative",zIndex:10}}>
        🔒 Pagos 100% seguros · Wompi Colombia · Banco Popular {BANCO.cuenta} · {BANCO.email}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ SIDEBAR ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════
function Sidebar({user,tab,setTab,onLogout,isDark,toggleTheme,open,setOpen}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const items=NAV_ITEMS[user.role]||NAV_ITEMS.student;
  const rc=ROLE_CLR[user.role]||C.p;
  const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===user.plan);

  const inner=(
    <aside style={{width:224,background:C.g1,borderRight:`1px solid ${C.b}`,
      display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",padding:"14px 8px",height:"100vh",overflowY:"auto",
      ...(sm?{position:"fixed",top:0,left:0,zIndex:300,boxShadow:"10px 0 50px rgba(0,0,0,.55)"}:{position:"fixed",top:0,left:0,bottom:0})}}>
      {/* Logo */}
      <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",padding:"6px 8px 14px",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,marginBottom:12}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>
          <div className="glow"style={{width:36,height:36,borderRadius:10,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:17,flexShrink:0}}>✨</div>
          <div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontWeight:900,fontSize:14,lineHeight:1}}>Speak Fluently</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:7,color:C.tf,letterSpacing:2,textTransform:"uppercase",marginTop:1}}>Academia IA · 🇨🇴</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        {sm&&<button onClick={()=>setOpen(false)}style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.tm,fontSize:22,cursor:"pointer",lineHeight:1}}>✕</button>}
      </div>

      {/* User chip */}
      <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:13,padding:"11px 12px",marginBottom:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:8}}>
          <AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={34} clr={rc}/>
          <div style={{minWidth:0,flex:1}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{user.name.split(" ")[0]}</div>
            <Bdg clr={rc} sm>{ROLE_IC[user.role]} {user.role}</Bdg>
          </div>
        </div>
        {/* Storage mini bar */}
        {user.storage>0&&(
          <div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:9,color:C.tf,marginBottom:3}}>
              <span>☁️ {fmtBytes(user.storageUsed||0)}</span>
              <span>{fmtBytes(user.storage)}</span>
            </div>
            <div style={{height:3,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}>
              <div style={{height:"100%",width:`${fmtPct(user.storageUsed||0,user.storage)}%`,background:C.p,borderRadius:99,transition:"width 1s"}}/>
            </div>
            <div style={{fontSize:8,color:C.tf,marginTop:3}}>{plan?.name||"Gratis"} · {fmtPct(user.storageUsed||0,user.storage)}%</div>
          </div>
        )}
        {user.role==="student"&&(
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:4,marginTop:8}}>
            <div style={{flex:1,background:`${C.gold}12`,borderRadius:6,padding:"3px 0",fontSize:9,color:C.gold,fontWeight:700,textAlign:"center"}}>⭐{user.xp}</div>
            <div style={{flex:1,background:`${C.lime}12`,borderRadius:6,padding:"3px 0",fontSize:9,color:C.lime,fontWeight:700,textAlign:"center"}}>🔥{user.streak}d</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Nav items */}
      <nav style={{flex:1,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:1}}>
        {items.map(it=>{
          const a=tab===it.id;
          return(
            <button key={it.id} onClick={()=>{setTab(it.id);if(sm)setOpen(false);}}
              style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8,padding:"8px 10px",borderRadius:9,border:"none",
                fontSize:12,fontWeight:a?700:400,color:a?C.t:C.tm,
                background:a?`${C.p}1e`:"transparent",transition:"all .15s",
                width:"100%",cursor:"pointer",textAlign:"left",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>
              <span style={{fontSize:15,width:20,textAlign:"center",flexShrink:0}}>{it.ic}</span>
              <span style={{flex:1,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{it.l}</span>
              {a&&<div style={{width:4,height:4,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.p,flexShrink:0}}/>}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* Bottom */}
      <div style={{borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`,paddingTop:8,marginTop:6}}>
        <div style={{padding:"4px 8px",marginBottom:4}}><TBtn isDark={isDark} toggle={toggleTheme}/></div>
        <button onClick={onLogout}style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8,padding:"8px 10px",borderRadius:9,border:"none",fontSize:12,color:C.tf,background:"transparent",width:"100%",cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"color .18s"}}
          onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.color=C.red}
          onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.color=C.tf}>
          🚪 <span>Cerrar sesión</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );

  if(sm){
    if(!open)return null;
    return(<><div style={{position:"fixed",inset:0,background:"rgba(0,0,0,.65)",zIndex:299,backdropFilter:"blur(5px)"}} onClick={()=>setOpen(false)}/>{inner}</>);
  }
  return inner;
}

function MobileTopBar({user,tab,setOpen,isDark,toggleTheme}){
  const C=useC();const rc=ROLE_CLR[user.role]||C.p;
  return(
    <header style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",padding:"13px 16px",background:C.g1,borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,position:"sticky",top:0,zIndex:100}}>
      <button onClick={()=>setOpen(true)}style={{width:38,height:38,borderRadius:10,background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:18,cursor:"pointer"}}>☰</button>
      <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:900}}>{TAB_TITLE[tab]||"Speak Fluently"}</div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7,alignItems:"center"}}><TBtn isDark={isDark} toggle={toggleTheme}/><AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={34} clr={rc}/></div>
    </header>
  );
}

function DesktopTopBar({user,tab,isDark,toggleTheme}){

      <div>

      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>

          <><div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,alignItems:"center",background:`${C.gold}10`,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}22`,padding:"6px 12px",borderRadius:99,color:C.gold,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700}}>⭐{user.xp.toLocaleString()}</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,alignItems:"center",background:`${C.lime}10`,border:`1px solid ${C.lime}22`,padding:"6px 12px",borderRadius:99,color:C.lime,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700}}>🔥{user.streak}d</div></>
        )}
        <TBtn isDark={isDark} toggle={toggleTheme}/>
        <AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={38} clr={rc}/>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

function BottomNav({user,tab,setTab}){
  const C=useC();
  const M={student:[{id:"dash",ic:"📊"},{id:"courses",ic:"📚"},{id:"live",ic:"📹"},{id:"ai-tutor",ic:"🤖"},{id:"hosting",ic:"☁️"}],
    admin:[{id:"admin-dash",ic:"🏠"},{id:"admin-users",ic:"👥"},{id:"admin-hosting",ic:"☁️"},{id:"admin-payments",ic:"💰"},{id:"admin-apis",ic:"🔌"}],
    professor:[{id:"prof-dash",ic:"🏠"},{id:"prof-students",ic:"👥"},{id:"prof-sessions",ic:"📹"},{id:"hosting",ic:"☁️"},{id:"billing",ic:"💳"}],
    developer:[{id:"dev-dash",ic:"💻"},{id:"dev-sdk",ic:"🔧"},{id:"dev-logs",ic:"📋"},{id:"dev-wompi",ic:"💳"},{id:"hosting",ic:"☁️"}],
  };
  const items=(M[user.role]||M.student);
  return(
    <nav style={{position:"fixed",bottom:0,left:0,right:0,background:C.g1,borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",zIndex:100,paddingBottom:"env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)"}}>
      {items.map(it=>{const a=tab===it.id;return(
        <button key={it.id} onClick={()=>setTab(it.id)}style={{flex:1,padding:"11px 0 9px",border:"none",background:"transparent",display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",alignItems:"center",gap:2,cursor:"pointer"}}>
          <span style={{fontSize:20,opacity:a?1:.45,transform:a?"scale(1.18)":"scale(1)",transition:"all .18s",display:"block"}}>{it.ic}</span>
          <span style={{width:4,height:4,borderRadius:"50%",background:a?C.p:"transparent",transition:"all .18s"}}/>
        </button>
      );})}
    </nav>
  );
}

// ══════════ HOSTING VIEW (Student/Professor) ══════════════════════════
function HostingView({user,db,upd,setTab}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[addM,setAddM]=useState(false);
  const[selAddon,setSelAddon]=useState(null);
  const[wsig,setWSig]=useState("");const[wref,setWRef]=useState("");
  const subs=db.subdomains.filter(s=>s.userId===user.id);

  const buyAddon=async(addon)=>{
    const ref=`SF-addon-${addon.id}-${Date.now()}`;
    const sig=await wompiSig(ref,addon.cents);
    setWRef(ref);setWSig(sig);setSelAddon(addon);setAddM(true);
  };

  useEffect(()=>{
    if(!addM||!wsig||!selAddon)return;
    const t=setTimeout(()=>{
      const c=document.getElementById("addon-wompi");if(!c)return;c.innerHTML="";
      const f=document.createElement("form");const s=document.createElement("script");
      s.src="https://checkout.wompi.co/widget.js";s.setAttribute("data-render","button");
      s.setAttribute("data-public-key",WOMPI_PK);s.setAttribute("data-currency","COP");
      s.setAttribute("data-amount-in-cents",String(selAddon.cents));
      s.setAttribute("data-reference",wref);s.setAttribute("data-signature:integrity",wsig);
      s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:email",user.email);s.setAttribute("data-customer-data:full-name",user.name);
      s.setAttribute("data-redirect-url",window.location.href);
      f.appendChild(s);c.appendChild(f);
    },350);
    return()=>clearTimeout(t);
  },[addM,wsig]);

  const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===user.plan)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];
  const stoPct=fmtPct(user.storageUsed||0,user.storage);
  const bwPct=fmtPct(user.bwUsed||0,user.bandwidth);

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      {/* Header card */}
      <Card sx={{padding:22,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${plan.color}20,${C.p}12)`,border:`1px solid ${plan.color}35`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"flex-start",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:14}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:14,alignItems:"center"}}>
            <div style={{width:54,height:54,borderRadius:15,background:`${plan.color}22`,border:`1px solid ${plan.color}40`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:26}}>{plan.icon}</div>
            <div>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:900,color:plan.color}}>Plan {plan.name}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginTop:2}}>{fmtBytes(plan.storage)} almacenamiento · {plan.bandwidth} GB BW/mes</div>
              {user.planExp&&<div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginTop:2}}>Vence: {user.planExp}</div>}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8}}>
            <Btn sm onClick={()=>setTab("billing")} clr={plan.color}>Cambiar plan</Btn>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:12,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="Almacenamiento" val={fmtBytes(user.storageUsed||0)} sub={`de ${fmtBytes(user.storage)}`} icon="💾" clr={stoPct>80?C.red:C.teal} pct={stoPct}/>
        <StatCard label="Transferencia" val={`${user.bwUsed||0} GB`} sub={`de ${user.bandwidth} GB/mes`} icon="🌐" clr={bwPct>80?C.red:C.p} pct={bwPct}/>
        <StatCard label="Subdominios" val={subs.length} sub="activos" icon="🔗" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="API Key" val={user.apiKey?"Activa":"—"} sub={user.apiKey?user.apiKey.slice(0,16)+"...":"Sin API"} icon="🔑" clr={C.gold}/>
      </div>

      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:18}}>
        {/* Subdomains */}
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",marginBottom:16}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>🔗 Mis Subdominios</div>
            <Bdg clr={C.teal}>{subs.length}</Bdg>
          </div>
          {subs.length===0&&<div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"20px 0",color:C.tf,fontSize:12}}>Sin subdominios activos</div>}
          {subs.map(s=>(
            <div key={s.id}style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,padding:"10px",background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,marginBottom:7}}>
              <div style={{width:8,height:8,borderRadius:"50%",background:s.status==="active"?C.lime:C.red,boxShadow:`0 0 8px ${s.status==="active"?C.lime:C.red}`,flexShrink:0}}/>
              <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap",fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{s.domain}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,marginTop:1}}>SSL {s.ssl?"✓ Activo":"✗ Pendiente"} · {s.type}</div>
              </div>
              <a href={`https://${s.domain}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{fontSize:11,color:C.p,fontWeight:700,flexShrink:0}}>→</a>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div style={{marginTop:10,padding:"10px",background:`${C.p}0a`,borderRadius:9,border:`1px solid ${C.p}20`,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>
            🔌 Vincula tu dominio propio en Configuración → DNS → CNAME
          </div>
        </Card>

        {/* API & Webhook */}
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:16}}>🔑 API & Webhooks</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:10}}>
            <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:6}}>API Key</div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
                <code style={{flex:1,fontSize:10,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.teal,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{user.apiKey||"Sin API Key — actualiza tu plan"}</code>
                {user.apiKey&&<button onClick={()=>navigator.clipboard?.writeText(user.apiKey)} style={{background:`${C.p}15`,border:"none",borderRadius:6,padding:"4px 8px",color:C.p,fontSize:10,cursor:"pointer",fontWeight:700}}>Copiar</button>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:6}}>Endpoint base</div>
              <code style={{fontSize:10,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.gold}}>https://api.speakfluently.co/v1</code>
            </div>
            <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:6}}>Webhook URL</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:11,color:user.webhookUrl?C.teal:C.tf,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{user.webhookUrl||"No configurado"}</div>
            </div>
            <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:6}}>Ejemplo de uso</div>
              <pre style={{fontSize:9,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.p3,lineHeight:1.7,margin:0,overflowX:"auto"}}>{`fetch('https://api.speakfluently.co/v1/progress', {
  headers: { 'x-api-key': '${user.apiKey?.slice(0,20)||"TU_API_KEY"}...' }
})`}</pre>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Addons */}
      <Card sx={{padding:22}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:5}}>🛒 Comprar Espacio y Complementos</div>
        <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginBottom:18}}>Amplía tu plan sin cambiar de suscripción. Activación inmediata tras el pago.</div>
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(3,1fr)",gap:12}}>
          {ADDON_PLANS.map(a=>(
            <div key={a.id} style={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:13,padding:16,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8,transition:"all .2s"}}
              onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.border=`1px solid ${a.color}50`;e.currentTarget.style.background=`${a.color}08`;}}
              onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.border=`1px solid ${C.b}`;e.currentTarget.style.background=C.g3;}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"flex-start"}}>
                <span style={{fontSize:24}}>{a.icon}</span>
                <Bdg clr={a.color}>${(a.price/1000).toFixed(0)}k</Bdg>
              </div>
              <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,color:a.color}}>{a.name}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.5,flex:1}}>{a.desc}</div>
              <Btn full sm onClick={()=>buyAddon(a)} clr={a.color}>Comprar</Btn>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {addM&&selAddon&&(
        <Modal title={`Comprar: ${selAddon.name}`} sub={fmtCOP(selAddon.price)} onClose={()=>{setAddM(false);setSelAddon(null);}} w={440}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16,textAlign:"center"}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:40}}>{selAddon.icon}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:14,color:C.tm}}>{selAddon.desc}</div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:28,fontWeight:900,color:selAddon.color}}>{fmtCOP(selAddon.price)}</div>
            <div id="addon-wompi"style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"center",minHeight:52}}/>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>🔒 Wompi Colombia · Activación inmediata · Banco Popular {BANCO.cuenta}</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap",justifyContent:"center"}}>
              {["PSE","Nequi","Bancolombia","Tarjeta","Efecty"].map(m=><span key={m}style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,background:C.g3,padding:"2px 7px",borderRadius:4,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>{m}</span>)}
            </div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ BILLING VIEW (Plans + Wompi v6) ═══════════════════════════
function BillingView({user,db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[sel,setSel]=useState(null);const[step,setStep]=useState("plans");
  const[wsig,setWSig]=useState("");const[wref,setWRef]=useState("");
  const[loading,setLoading]=useState(false);
  const[billingTab,setBillingTab]=useState("plans");
  const wompiRef=useRef(null);

  const pay=async(plan)=>{
    if(plan.price===0)return;
    setLoading(true);
    const ref=`SF-${plan.id}-${Date.now()}`;
    const sig=await wompiSig(ref,plan.cents);
    setWRef(ref);setWSig(sig);setSel(plan);setStep("checkout");setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(()=>{
    if(step!=="checkout"||!sel||!wsig)return;
    const t=setTimeout(()=>{
      if(wompiRef.current){
        wompiRef.current.innerHTML="";
        injectWompiWidget("billing-wompi-v6",{cents:sel.cents,ref:wref,sig:wsig,email:user.email,name:user.name,redirectUrl:window.location.href});
      }
    },350);
    return()=>clearTimeout(t);
  },[step,sel,wsig]);

  const myPayments=db.payments.filter(p=>p.userId===user.id);
  const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===user.plan)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];
  const METHODS=["PSE","Nequi","Bancolombia","Tarjeta Crédito","Tarjeta Débito","Efecty","Daviplata","Bancolombia QR"];

  if(step==="checkout"&&sel)return(
    <div style={{maxWidth:560,margin:"0 auto",display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      <button onClick={()=>setStep("plans")}style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:5,padding:0,width:"fit-content"}}>← Volver a planes</button>
      <Card sx={{padding:26}}>
        <div style={{textAlign:"center",marginBottom:22}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:42,marginBottom:10}}>{sel.icon}</div>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:24,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:5}}>Plan {sel.name}</div>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:34,fontWeight:900,color:sel.color}}>{fmtCOP(sel.price)}<span style={{fontSize:13,color:C.tf,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",fontWeight:400}}>/mes</span></div>
          <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginTop:4}}>☁️ {fmtBytes(sel.storage)} · {sel.bandwidth} GB BW/mes</div>
        </div>

        {/* Features in checkout */}
        <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:12,padding:14,marginBottom:18,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:8}}>Incluye</div>
          {sel.features.map((f,i)=><div key={i}style={{display:"flex",gap:8,fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginBottom:6}}><span style={{color:C.lime,flexShrink:0}}>✓</span>{f}</div>)}
        </div>

        {/* Bank info */}
        <div style={{background:`${C.p}08`,borderRadius:12,padding:13,marginBottom:18,border:`1px solid ${C.p}20`}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:8}}>🏦 Cuenta destino pago</div>
          {Object.entries({Banco:BANCO.banco,Cuenta:BANCO.cuenta,Titular:BANCO.titular,Email:BANCO.email}).map(([k,v])=>(
            <div key={k}style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginBottom:4}}>
              <span style={{color:C.tf,fontWeight:700}}>{k}</span>
              <span style={{fontFamily:k==="Cuenta"?"'JetBrains Mono',monospace":"inherit",fontSize:k==="Cuenta"?10:11}}>{v}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Wompi widget */}
        <div style={{textAlign:"center",marginBottom:14}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:10}}>🔒 Pago seguro procesado por Wompi Colombia</div>
          <div ref={wompiRef} id="billing-wompi-v6" style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"center",minHeight:52}}/>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",justifyContent:"center",marginBottom:10}}>
          {METHODS.map(m=><span key={m}style={{fontSize:9,background:C.g3,color:C.tf,padding:"3px 8px",borderRadius:5,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>{m}</span>)}
        </div>
        <div style={{textAlign:"center",fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>🔒 SSL · PCI-DSS · Regulado SFC Colombia</div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:22}}>
      <Tabs tabs={[{id:"plans",l:"Planes",ic:"☁️"},{id:"current",l:"Mi Suscripción",ic:"🔁"},{id:"history",l:"Historial",ic:"📋"},{id:"addons",l:"Complementos",ic:"➕"}]} active={billingTab} onChange={setBillingTab}/>

      {/* PLANS TAB */}
      {billingTab==="plans"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:20}}>
          <div style={{textAlign:"center"}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?22:28,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:5}}>☁️ Elige tu Plan</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Espacio real en servidor · Subdominio propio · Cancela cuando quieras</div>
          </div>
          <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(5,1fr)",gap:12}}>
            {HOSTING_PLANS.map(p=>{
              const curr=user.plan===p.id;
              return(
                <Card key={p.id}sx={{padding:sm?14:18,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",position:"relative",
                  border:`${curr||p.tag?2:1}px solid ${curr?p.color:p.tag?C.b2:C.b}`,
                  background:curr?`${p.color}06`:C.card,
                  boxShadow:curr?`0 0 30px ${p.color}20`:p.tag==="Más popular"?`0 0 28px ${C.p}15`:""}}>
                  {curr&&<div style={{position:"absolute",top:-12,left:"50%",transform:"translateX(-50%)",background:p.color,color:"#111",fontSize:9,fontWeight:800,padding:"3px 12px",borderRadius:99,whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>✓ TU PLAN</div>}
                  {!curr&&p.tag&&<div style={{position:"absolute",top:-12,left:"50%",transform:"translateX(-50%)",background:C.p,color:"#fff",fontSize:9,fontWeight:800,padding:"3px 12px",borderRadius:99,whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{p.tag}</div>}
                  <div style={{fontSize:26,marginBottom:7,marginTop:curr||p.tag?8:0}}>{p.icon}</div>
                  <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:13,fontWeight:800,marginBottom:5,color:curr?p.color:C.t}}>{p.name}</div>
                  <div style={{marginBottom:10}}>
                    <span style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?16:18,fontWeight:900,color:p.color}}>{p.price===0?"Gratis":fmtCOP(p.price)}</span>
                    {p.price>0&&<span style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>/mes</span>}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,marginBottom:8}}>💾 {fmtBytes(p.storage)} · 🌐{p.bandwidth}GB</div>
                  <div style={{flex:1,marginBottom:10}}>
                    {p.features.slice(0,sm?3:6).map((f,i)=><div key={i}style={{display:"flex",gap:5,fontSize:sm?9:10,color:C.tm,marginBottom:4}}><span style={{color:C.lime,flexShrink:0}}>✓</span><span style={{lineHeight:1.3}}>{f}</span></div>)}
                    {sm&&p.features.length>3&&<div style={{fontSize:8,color:C.tf,marginTop:3}}>+{p.features.length-3} más...</div>}
                  </div>
                  <Btn full sm onClick={()=>pay(p)} clr={curr?C.g4:p.color} disabled={loading||curr}>
                    {loading&&sel?.id===p.id?<Spin/>:curr?"✅ Activo":"Suscribirme"}
                  </Btn>
                </Card>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          <Card sx={{padding:18,textAlign:"center"}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,marginBottom:10}}>Pagos 100% seguros · Wompi Colombia</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"center",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:10}}>
              {METHODS.map(m=><span key={m}style={{padding:"5px 10px",borderRadius:99,background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,fontSize:10,color:C.tm}}>{m}</span>)}
            </div>
            <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf}}>🏦 {BANCO.banco} · Cta <strong style={{fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{BANCO.cuenta}</strong> · {BANCO.email}</div>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CURRENT PLAN TAB */}
      {billingTab==="current"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16,maxWidth:600}}>
          <Card sx={{padding:24,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${plan.color}14,${C.p}06)`,border:`2px solid ${plan.color}35`}}>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:14,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:18,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              <div style={{width:58,height:58,borderRadius:16,background:`${plan.color}20`,border:`1px solid ${plan.color}40`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:28}}>{plan.icon}</div>
              <div style={{flex:1}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,color:plan.color,fontWeight:700,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:3}}>Plan Activo</div>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900}}>{plan.name}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Vence: {user.planExp||"Sin vencimiento"}</div>
              </div>
              <div style={{textAlign:"right"}}>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:26,fontWeight:900,color:plan.color}}>{fmtCOP(plan.price)}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>/mes</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:10,marginBottom:14}}>
              <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,fontWeight:700,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:5}}>Almacenamiento</div>
                <Bar used={user.storageUsed||0} total={user.storage}/>
              </div>
              <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,fontWeight:700,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:5}}>Transferencia</div>
                <Bar used={user.bwUsed||0} total={user.bandwidth}/>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              <Btn sm onClick={()=>setBillingTab("plans")} clr={plan.color}>🔄 Cambiar Plan</Btn>
              <Btn sm ghost onClick={()=>setBillingTab("addons")}>➕ Agregar Complementos</Btn>
            </div>
          </Card>

          {/* Features of current plan */}
          <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>✅ Incluido en tu plan</div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:6}}>
              {plan.features.map((f,i)=>(
                <div key={i} style={{display:"flex",gap:8,fontSize:12,color:C.tm,padding:"6px 10px",background:C.g3,borderRadius:8,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                  <span style={{color:C.lime,flexShrink:0}}>✓</span>{f}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            {plan.no?.length>0&&<div style={{marginTop:12}}><div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:8}}>No incluye</div>
              {plan.no.map((f,i)=><div key={i} style={{display:"flex",gap:8,fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:4}}><span style={{flexShrink:0}}>✗</span>{f}</div>)}
            </div>}
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* HISTORY TAB */}
      {billingTab==="history"&&(
        <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
          {myPayments.length===0
            ?<div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"40px 20px",color:C.tf}}>No tienes pagos registrados aún.</div>
            :(
              <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
                <table>
                  <thead><tr><th>Ref</th><th>Plan</th><th>Monto</th><th>Método</th><th>Estado</th><th>Fecha</th></tr></thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {myPayments.map(p=>(
                      <tr key={p.id}>
                        <td style={{fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:9,color:C.teal}}>{p.id}</td>
                        <td><Bdg clr={planColor(p.plan)} sm>{p.planName}</Bdg></td>
                        <td style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.gold}}>${p.amount.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</td>
                        <td style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tm}}>{p.method}</td>
                        <td><Bdg clr={p.status==="APPROVED"?C.lime:p.status==="PENDING"?C.gold:C.red} sm>{p.status}</Bdg></td>
                        <td style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{p.date}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )
          }
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* ADDONS TAB */}
      {billingTab==="addons"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700}}>➕ Complementos disponibles</div>
          <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Amplía sin cambiar plan. Activación inmediata tras el pago Wompi.</div>
          <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(3,1fr)",gap:12}}>
            {ADDON_PLANS.map(a=>(
              <Card key={a.id} sx={{padding:18,border:`1px solid ${a.color}22`,transition:"all .2s"}}
                onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=`${a.color}55`;e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow=`0 0 20px ${a.color}15`;}}
                onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=`${a.color}22`;e.currentTarget.style.boxShadow="";}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:10}}>
                  <span style={{fontSize:26}}>{a.icon}</span>
                  <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:18,fontWeight:900,color:a.color}}>{fmtCOP(a.price)}</div>
                </div>
                <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,color:a.color,marginBottom:6}}>{a.name}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginBottom:14,lineHeight:1.5}}>{a.desc}</div>
                <Btn full sm onClick={async()=>{
                  const ref=`SF-addon-${a.id}-${Date.now()}`;
                  const sig=await wompiSig(ref,a.cents);
                  setSel(a);setWRef(ref);setWSig(sig);setStep("checkout");
                }} clr={a.color}>Comprar</Btn>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN DASHBOARD ═══════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminDash({db,setTab}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const rev=db.payments.filter(p=>p.status==="APPROVED").reduce((a,p)=>a+p.amount,0);
  const live=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.status==="live");
  const stor=db.serverStats;
  const stoUsedPct=fmtPct(stor.usedStorage,stor.totalStorage);

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:20}}>
      {live.length>0&&(
        <div style={{background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.red}18,${C.p}0a)`,border:`1px solid ${C.red}45`,borderRadius:16,padding:"14px 20px",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:10}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}><span className="pulse"style={{width:9,height:9,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.red,boxShadow:`0 0 10px ${C.red}`,display:"inline-block"}}/><span style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:700}}>{live[0].title} — <span style={{color:C.red}}>EN VIVO</span></span></div>
          <Btn sm onClick={()=>setTab("admin-sessions")} clr={C.red}>Gestionar →</Btn>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KPI Stats */}
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="Usuarios" val={db.users.length} sub={`${db.users.filter(u=>u.role==="student").length} estudiantes`} icon="👥" clr={C.p3} trend={8}/>
        <StatCard label="Ingresos" val={`$${(rev/1000000).toFixed(1)}M`} sub="COP mes actual" icon="💰" clr={C.gold} trend={15}/>
        <StatCard label="Servidor" val={`${stoUsedPct}%`} sub={`${fmtBytes(stor.usedStorage)} / ${fmtBytes(stor.totalStorage)}`} icon="☁️" clr={stoUsedPct>70?C.red:C.teal} pct={stoUsedPct}/>
        <StatCard label="Uptime" val={`${stor.uptime}%`} sub="Últimos 30 días" icon="⚡" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="API Requests" val={stor.totalRequests.toLocaleString()} sub="Total plataforma" icon="🔌" clr={C.amber}/>
      </div>

      {/* Server overview */}
      <Card sx={{padding:20,border:`1px solid ${C.teal}30`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",marginBottom:16,flexWrap:"wrap",gap:10}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700}}>🖥️ Estado del Servidor</div>
          <Btn sm onClick={()=>setTab("admin-hosting")} ghost>Ver detalle →</Btn>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(4,1fr)",gap:12}}>
          {[["CPU",stor.cpu,"#3cffa0"],["RAM",stor.ram,"#ffd04d"],["Disco",stor.disk,"#c4b0ff"],["BW",Math.round(stor.usedBandwidth/stor.totalBandwidth*100),"#22d3ee"]].map(([l,v,col])=>(
            <div key={l}style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:11,padding:13,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:5}}>{l}</div>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,color:col,marginBottom:6}}>{v}%</div>
              <div style={{height:3,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${v}%`,background:col,borderRadius:99}}/></div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap",marginTop:12}}>
          {stor.regions.map(r=><Bdg key={r} clr={C.teal}>{r}</Bdg>)}
          <Bdg clr={C.lime} dot>Online</Bdg>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1.5fr 1fr",gap:18}}>
        {/* Payments table */}
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:14,flexWrap:"wrap",gap:8}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>💳 Últimas Transacciones</div>
            <Btn sm ghost onClick={()=>setTab("admin-payments")}>Ver todas →</Btn>
          </div>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Usuario</th><th>Plan</th><th>Monto</th><th>Estado</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {db.payments.slice(0,6).map(p=>(
                  <tr key={p.id}>
                    <td style={{color:C.teal,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:9}}>{p.id}</td>
                    <td style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>{p.userName}</td>
                    <td><Bdg clr={planColor(p.plan)} sm>{p.planName}</Bdg></td>
                    <td style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.gold,fontSize:11}}>${p.amount.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</td>
                    <td><Bdg clr={p.status==="APPROVED"?C.lime:p.status==="PENDING"?C.gold:C.red} sm dot={p.status==="PENDING"}>{p.status}</Bdg></td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>

        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
