          {/* Quick actions */}
          <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>⚡ Acciones Rápidas</div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:7}}>
              {[["📹 Live","admin-sessions",C.red],["📝 Lección","admin-lessons",C.lime],["📢 Anuncio","admin-announcements",C.gold],["👥 Usuarios","admin-users",C.p3],["🔌 APIs","admin-apis",C.teal],["🗄️ BD","admin-db",C.amber],["✨ IA","admin-ai",C.p],["📈 Stats","admin-stats",C.mint]].map(([l,t,col])=>(
                <button key={l} onClick={()=>setTab(t)}style={{padding:"10px 6px",borderRadius:10,border:`1px solid ${col}22`,background:`${col}0a`,color:col,fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"all .15s",textAlign:"center"}}
                  onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background=`${col}1e`}
                  onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background=`${col}0a`}>{l}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Card>
          {/* API Services quick */}
          <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>🔌 Estado APIs</div>
            {db.apiServices.map(s=>(
              <div key={s.id}style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:9,marginBottom:8}}>
                <div style={{width:7,height:7,borderRadius:"50%",background:s.status==="connected"?C.lime:C.red,boxShadow:`0 0 7px ${s.status==="connected"?C.lime:C.red}`,flexShrink:0}}/>
                <span style={{flex:1,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{s.name}</span>
                <span style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{s.requests}req</span>
              </div>
            ))}
            <Btn full sm ghost onClick={()=>setTab("admin-apis")} sx={{marginTop:8}}>Configurar →</Btn>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN HOSTING / SERVER PANEL ══════════════════════════════
function AdminHostingPanel({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const stor=db.serverStats;
  const[tab2,setTab2]=useState("overview");

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <Tabs tabs={[{id:"overview",l:"Resumen",ic:"📊"},{id:"users",l:"Usuarios",ic:"👥"},{id:"domains",l:"Dominios",ic:"🔗"},{id:"usage",l:"Uso",ic:"📈"}]} active={tab2} onChange={setTab2}/>

      {tab2==="overview"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
            <StatCard label="Almacenamiento Total" val={fmtBytes(stor.totalStorage)} sub={`${fmtBytes(stor.usedStorage)} usado`} icon="💾" clr={C.teal} pct={fmtPct(stor.usedStorage,stor.totalStorage)}/>
            <StatCard label="Bandwidth Total" val={`${stor.totalBandwidth} GB`} sub={`${stor.usedBandwidth} GB usado`} icon="🌐" clr={C.p} pct={fmtPct(stor.usedBandwidth,stor.totalBandwidth)}/>
            <StatCard label="Usuarios Activos" val={stor.activeUsers} icon="👥" clr={C.lime}/>
            <StatCard label="Uptime" val={`${stor.uptime}%`} icon="⚡" clr={C.lime}/>
          </div>
          <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:16}}>
            <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>🖥️ Recursos del Servidor</div>
              {[["CPU","cpu","#3cffa0"],["RAM","ram","#ffd04d"],["Disco","disk","#c4b0ff"],["Red","12","#22d3ee"]].map(([l,k,col])=>(
                <div key={l}style={{marginBottom:12}}>
                  <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:11,marginBottom:4}}>
                    <span style={{color:C.tm,fontWeight:600}}>{l}</span>
                    <span style={{color:col,fontWeight:700}}>{stor[k]||k}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{height:5,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${stor[k]||k}%`,background:`linear-gradient(90deg,${col},${col}aa)`,borderRadius:99,boxShadow:`0 0 8px ${col}70`}}/></div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </Card>
            <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>🌍 Regiones Activas</div>
              {stor.regions.map(r=>(
                <div key={r}style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center",padding:"10px",background:C.g3,borderRadius:9,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,marginBottom:7}}>
                  <div style={{width:7,height:7,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.lime,boxShadow:`0 0 7px ${C.lime}`,flexShrink:0}}/>
                  <span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,flex:1}}>{r}</span>
                  <Bdg clr={C.lime} sm dot>Online</Bdg>
                </div>
              ))}
              <div style={{marginTop:10,padding:10,background:`${C.p}0a`,borderRadius:9,border:`1px solid ${C.p}18`,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>CDN Activo · Latencia media: 12ms</div>
            </Card>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab2==="users"&&(
        <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
          <div style={{padding:"16px 20px",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>Espacio por usuario</div>
          </div>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Usuario</th><th>Plan</th><th>Almacenamiento</th><th>Bandwidth</th><th>Subdominios</th><th>API</th><th>Vence</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {db.users.map(u=>{
                  const pl=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===u.plan);const sp=db.subdomains.filter(s=>s.userId===u.id);
                  return(
                    <tr key={u.id}>
                      <td><div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}><AvatarCircle name={u.name} size={28} clr={ROLE_CLR[u.role]}/><div><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700}}>{u.name}</div><div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{u.email}</div></div></div></td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={pl?.color||C.p}>{pl?.icon} {pl?.name||"—"}</Bdg></td>
                      <td><Bar used={u.storageUsed||0} total={u.storage}/></td>
                      <td style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{u.bwUsed||0}/{u.bandwidth} GB</td>
                      <td style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.teal}}>{sp.length}</td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={u.apiKey?C.lime:C.tf} sm>{u.apiKey?"Activa":"—"}</Bdg></td>
                      <td style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{u.planExp||"—"}</td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {tab2==="domains"&&(
        <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
          <div style={{padding:"16px 20px",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center"}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>Todos los subdominios ({db.subdomains.length})</div>
            <Btn sm clr={C.lime} onClick={()=>{const nm=prompt("Nuevo subdominio (ej: nuevo.speakfluently.co):");if(nm)upd(p=>({...p,subdomains:[...p.subdomains,{id:Date.now(),domain:nm,userId:1,status:"pending",type:"custom",ssl:false,created:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]}]}));}}>+ Agregar</Btn>
          </div>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Dominio</th><th>Usuario</th><th>Tipo</th><th>SSL</th><th>Estado</th><th>Creado</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {db.subdomains.map(s=>{
                  const u=db.users.find(x=>x.id===s.userId);
                  return(
                    <tr key={s.id}>
                      <td style={{fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:11,color:C.teal}}>{s.domain}</td>
                      <td style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{u?.name||"—"}</td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={C.p} sm>{s.type}</Bdg></td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={s.ssl?C.lime:C.gold} sm>{s.ssl?"✓ SSL":"Pendiente"}</Bdg></td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={s.status==="active"?C.lime:C.gold} sm dot={s.status==="active"}>{s.status}</Bdg></td>
                      <td style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{s.created}</td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {tab2==="usage"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
          <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>📈 Resumen de uso mensual</div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              {[["Almacenamiento total",`${fmtBytes(stor.usedStorage)} / ${fmtBytes(stor.totalStorage)}`,fmtPct(stor.usedStorage,stor.totalStorage),C.teal],["Transferencia total",`${stor.usedBandwidth} / ${stor.totalBandwidth} GB`,fmtPct(stor.usedBandwidth,stor.totalBandwidth),C.p],["Requests API",`${stor.totalRequests.toLocaleString()}`,85,C.amber],["Uptime SLA",`${stor.uptime}%`,stor.uptime,C.lime]].map(([l,v,pct,col])=>(
                <div key={l}style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:11,padding:14,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:5}}>{l}</div>
                  <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:18,fontWeight:800,color:col,marginBottom:8}}>{v}</div>
                  <div style={{height:4,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${Math.min(pct,100)}%`,background:col,borderRadius:99}}/></div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN DB ══════════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminDB({db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[selTable,setSelTable]=useState(null);
  const[query,setQuery]=useState("");
  const[qRes,setQRes]=useState(null);const[qErr,setQErr]=useState("");

  const execQuery=()=>{
    setQErr("");setQRes(null);
    const q=query.toLowerCase().trim();
    if(q.includes("drop")||q.includes("delete")||q.includes("truncate")){setQErr("⚠️ Operaciones de destrucción no permitidas desde la UI.");return;}
    const m=q.match(/^select .+ from (\w+)/);
    if(m){
      const tname=m[1];
      const tdata=db[tname];
      if(!tdata){setQErr(`Tabla '${tname}' no encontrada.`);return;}
      setQRes(Array.isArray(tdata)?tdata.slice(0,20):Object.entries(tdata).slice(0,20).map(([k,v])=>({key:k,value:JSON.stringify(v)})));
    }else{setQErr("Solo se admiten SELECT statements. Ej: SELECT * FROM users");}
  };

  const getTableData=(name)=>{
    const d=db[name];
    if(!d)return[];
    return Array.isArray(d)?d:Object.entries(d).map(([k,v])=>({key:k,value:JSON.stringify(v).slice(0,80)}));
  };

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      {/* Header stats */}
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        {db.dbTables.map(t=>(
          <Card key={t.name}sx={{padding:"12px 16px",flex:1,minWidth:100,cursor:"pointer",border:`1px solid ${selTable===t.name?C.p:C.b}`,background:selTable===t.name?`${C.p}10`:C.card}}
            onClick={()=>setSelTable(selTable===t.name?null:t.name)}>
            <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:4,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{t.name}</div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,color:C.p3}}>{t.rows}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,marginTop:2}}>filas · {t.size}</div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* SQL Console */}
      <Card sx={{padding:20,border:`1px solid ${C.amber}30`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:14}}>
          <span style={{fontSize:18}}>🔍</span>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>Consola SQL (Solo lectura)</div>
          <Bdg clr={C.gold}>READ ONLY</Bdg>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,marginBottom:12}}>
          <input value={query} onChange={e=>setQuery(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e=>e.key==="Enter"&&execQuery()}
            placeholder="SELECT * FROM users" style={{flex:1,background:C.g2,border:`1.5px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:10,padding:"10px 14px",color:C.t,fontSize:12,outline:"none",fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}/>
          <Btn onClick={execQuery} clr={C.amber}>Ejecutar</Btn>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:10}}>
          {["SELECT * FROM users","SELECT * FROM payments","SELECT * FROM sessions","SELECT * FROM lessons"].map(q=>(
            <button key={q} onClick={()=>setQuery(q)} style={{fontSize:9,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.teal,background:`${C.teal}0a`,border:`1px solid ${C.teal}22`,padding:"3px 8px",borderRadius:5,cursor:"pointer"}}>{q}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {qErr&&<div style={{color:C.red,fontSize:11,padding:"8px 12px",background:`${C.red}0a`,borderRadius:8,marginBottom:10}}>{qErr}</div>}
        {qRes&&(
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr>{qRes[0]&&Object.keys(qRes[0]).map(k=><th key={k}>{k}</th>)}</tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {qRes.map((row,i)=>(
                  <tr key={i}>{Object.values(row).map((v,j)=>(
                    <td key={j}style={{fontSize:10,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",maxWidth:160,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>
                      {typeof v==="boolean"?v?"✓":"✗":String(v??"")}
                    </td>
                  ))}</tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginTop:8}}>{qRes.length} filas</div>
          </div>
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Table explorer */}
      {selTable&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:14,flexWrap:"wrap",gap:8}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>📋 {selTable} ({db.dbTables.find(t=>t.name===selTable)?.rows} filas)</div>
            <Bdg clr={C.teal}>{db.dbTables.find(t=>t.name===selTable)?.size}</Bdg>
          </div>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>{getTableData(selTable)[0]&&Object.keys(getTableData(selTable)[0]).map(k=><th key={k}>{k}</th>)}</tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {getTableData(selTable).slice(0,15).map((row,i)=>(
                  <tr key={i}>{Object.values(row).map((v,j)=>(
                    <td key={j}style={{fontSize:10,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",maxWidth:180,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>
                      {typeof v==="boolean"?v?"✓":"✗":Array.isArray(v)?`[${v.length}]`:String(v??"")}
                    </td>
                  ))}</tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Schema info */}
      <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>🗂️ Esquema de la Base de Datos</div>
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:10}}>
          {db.dbTables.map(t=>(
            <div key={t.name}style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:11,padding:13,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:6}}>
                <code style={{fontSize:12,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.teal,fontWeight:700}}>{t.name}</code>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5}}><Bdg clr={C.p} sm>{t.rows} rows</Bdg><Bdg clr={C.tf} sm>{t.size}</Bdg></div>
              </div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:3}}>{t.indexes} índices · Última escritura:</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:9,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.tm}}>{t.lastWrite}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN APIs ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminAPIs({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[sel,setSel]=useState(null);
  const[form,setForm]=useState({key:"",endpoint:"",webhookUrl:""});
  const[testing,setTesting]=useState(null);
  const[testRes,setTestRes]=useState({});

  const testConn=async(svc)=>{
    setTesting(svc.id);setTestRes(r=>({...r,[svc.id]:null}));
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,1200));
    const ok=["claude","wompi","googlemeet"].includes(svc.id)||Math.random()>.4;
    setTestRes(r=>({...r,[svc.id]:ok}));
    upd(p=>({...p,apiServices:p.apiServices.map(s=>s.id===svc.id?{...s,status:ok?"connected":"error",lastPing:new Date().toISOString()}:s)}));
    setTesting(null);
  };

  const save=()=>{
    if(!sel)return;
    upd(p=>({...p,apiServices:p.apiServices.map(s=>s.id===sel.id?{...s,...form,status:form.key?"configured":"disconnected"}:s)}));
    setSel(null);
  };

  const SICONS={claude:"🤖",wompi:"💳",sendgrid:"📧",twilio:"📱",firebase:"🔥",googlemeet:"📹",s3:"☁️",stripe:"💳"};

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="Conectadas" val={db.apiServices.filter(s=>s.status==="connected").length} icon="✅" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="Total Requests" val={db.apiServices.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.requests,0).toLocaleString()} icon="🔌" clr={C.amber}/>
        <StatCard label="Errores" val={db.apiServices.reduce((a,s)=>a+s.errors,0)} icon="⚠️" clr={C.red}/>
      </div>

      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
        {db.apiServices.map(svc=>{
          const tr=testRes[svc.id];
          const col=svc.status==="connected"?C.lime:svc.status==="configured"?C.gold:C.red;
          return(
            <Card key={svc.id}sx={{padding:18}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:12,gap:8}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}>
                  <div style={{width:40,height:40,borderRadius:11,background:`${col}14`,border:`1px solid ${col}25`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:18,flexShrink:0}}>{SICONS[svc.id]||"🔌"}</div>
                  <div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700}}>{svc.name}</div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{svc.endpoint.replace("https://","")}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Bdg clr={col} dot={svc.status==="connected"}>{svc.status}</Bdg>
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:12}}>
                <span>📥 {svc.requests} req</span>
                {svc.errors>0&&<span style={{color:C.red}}>⚠️ {svc.errors} err</span>}
                {svc.lastPing&&<span>🕐 {svc.lastPing.split("T")[1]?.slice(0,5)}</span>}
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7}}>
                <Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setSel(svc);setForm({key:svc.key==="CONFIGURED"?"":svc.key||"",endpoint:svc.endpoint,webhookUrl:svc.webhookUrl||""});}}>⚙️ Config</Btn>
                <Btn xs clr={testing===svc.id?C.g3:C.teal} disabled={testing===svc.id} onClick={()=>testConn(svc)}>
                  {testing===svc.id?<Spin/>:tr===true?"✅":tr===false?"❌":"🔍 Test"}
                </Btn>
              </div>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Webhooks & Endpoints docs */}
      <Card sx={{padding:20,border:`1px solid ${C.p}22`}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>📖 API de Speak Fluently</div>
        <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:12,padding:14,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:11,lineHeight:2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          {[["GET","/v1/users","Lista todos los usuarios","teal"],["GET","/v1/users/:id","Usuario por ID","teal"],["POST","/v1/progress","Guardar progreso","lime"],["GET","/v1/lessons","Lista lecciones","teal"],["POST","/v1/payments/webhook","Webhook de pagos","amber"],["GET","/v1/sessions","Sesiones live","teal"],["GET","/v1/stats","Estadísticas globales","gold"]].map(([m,e,d,col])=>(
            <div key={e}style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:3}}>
              <Bdg clr={C[col]||C.p} sm>{m}</Bdg>
              <span style={{color:C.teal}}>{e}</span>
              <span style={{color:C.tf,fontSize:10}}>{d}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div style={{marginTop:12,padding:"10px 14px",background:`${C.teal}0a`,borderRadius:9,border:`1px solid ${C.teal}20`,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>
          Header requerido: <code style={{fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.teal}}>x-api-key: sk-sf-XXXXXX</code>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {sel&&(
        <Modal title={`Configurar: ${sel.name}`} onClose={()=>setSel(null)} w={500}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
            <div style={{background:`${C.gold}0a`,borderRadius:10,padding:11,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}22`,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>
              ⚠️ Las API keys son sensibles. No las compartas. Se guardan cifradas en la base de datos.
            </div>
            <Inp label="API Key / Token" value={form.key} onChange={v=>setForm(x=>({...x,key:v}))} type="password" placeholder="sk-... o Bearer ..."/>
            <Inp label="Endpoint base" value={form.endpoint} onChange={v=>setForm(x=>({...x,endpoint:v}))} placeholder="https://api.ejemplo.com/v1"/>
            <Inp label="Webhook URL (opcional)" value={form.webhookUrl} onChange={v=>setForm(x=>({...x,webhookUrl:v}))} placeholder="https://tuservidor.com/webhook"/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}>
              <Btn outline onClick={()=>setSel(null)}>Cancelar</Btn>
              <Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>Guardar</Btn>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN STATS ═══════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminStats({db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const rev=db.payments.filter(p=>p.status==="APPROVED").reduce((a,p)=>a+p.amount,0);
  const byPlan=HOSTING_PLANS.map(p=>({...p,count:db.users.filter(u=>u.plan===p.id).length,rev:db.payments.filter(x=>x.plan===p.id&&x.status==="APPROVED").reduce((a,x)=>a+x.amount,0)}));
  const byRole=["student","professor","admin","developer"].map(r=>({r,count:db.users.filter(u=>u.role===r).length}));

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="MRR" val={`$${(rev/1000000).toFixed(2)}M`} sub="COP mes actual" icon="📈" clr={C.gold} trend={15}/>
        <StatCard label="Usuarios" val={db.users.length} sub={`${db.users.filter(u=>u.active).length} activos`} icon="👥" clr={C.p3}/>
        <StatCard label="Conversión" val={`${Math.round(db.payments.filter(p=>p.status==="APPROVED").length/Math.max(db.users.length,1)*100)}%`} icon="💡" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="Uptime" val="99.97%" icon="⚡" clr={C.teal}/>
      </div>

      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:18}}>
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:16}}>📊 Usuarios por Plan</div>
          {byPlan.map(p=>(
            <div key={p.id}style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,marginBottom:10}}>
              <span style={{fontSize:18,flexShrink:0}}>{p.icon}</span>
              <div style={{flex:1}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:3}}><span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600}}>{p.name}</span><span style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:p.color}}>{p.count}</span></div>
                <div style={{height:4,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${db.users.length?p.count/db.users.length*100:0}%`,background:p.color,borderRadius:99}}/></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Card>
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:16}}>💰 Ingresos por Plan</div>
          {byPlan.filter(p=>p.rev>0).sort((a,b)=>b.rev-a.rev).map(p=>(
            <div key={p.id}style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",padding:"10px 0",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}><span>{p.icon}</span><span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600}}>{p.name}</span></div>
              <span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,color:p.color}}>${p.rev.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginTop:12,paddingTop:12,borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
            <span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>TOTAL</span>
            <span style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:18,fontWeight:900,color:C.gold}}>${rev.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</span>
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>👥 Distribución de Roles</div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          {byRole.map(r=>(
            <div key={r.r}style={{flex:1,minWidth:100,background:C.g3,borderRadius:12,padding:"16px",border:`1px solid ${ROLE_CLR[r.r]||C.b}28`,textAlign:"center"}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:22,marginBottom:6}}>{ROLE_IC[r.r]}</div>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:26,fontWeight:900,color:ROLE_CLR[r.r]||C.p}}>{r.count}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1,fontWeight:700,marginTop:2}}>{r.r}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ PROFESSOR CLASSROOM ═══════════════════════════════════════
function ProfClassroom({user,db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const mySubs=db.subdomains.filter(s=>s.userId===user.id);
  const myMats=db.materials.filter(m=>m.uploadedBy===user.id||m.ownerId===user.id);
  const mySessions=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.professorId===user.id);
  const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===user.plan)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <Card sx={{padding:22,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${plan.color}18,${C.p}0a)`,border:`1px solid ${plan.color}30`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:14}}>
          <div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:4}}>🎓 Tu Aula Virtual</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginBottom:10}}>Gestiona tu espacio de enseñanza · Plan {plan.name}</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              {mySubs.map(s=><a key={s.id} href={`https://${s.domain}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{display:"inline-flex",alignItems:"center",gap:5,padding:"5px 12px",background:`${C.teal}14`,border:`1px solid ${C.teal}28`,borderRadius:99,fontSize:11,color:C.teal,fontWeight:600}}><span>🔗</span>{s.domain}</a>)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
            <div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"14px 20px",background:C.g3,borderRadius:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:24,fontWeight:900,color:plan.color}}>{myMats.length}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>Materiales</div>
            </div>
            <div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"14px 20px",background:C.g3,borderRadius:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:24,fontWeight:900,color:C.red}}>{mySessions.filter(s=>s.status==="live").length}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>En Vivo</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:16}}>
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>📁 Mis Materiales</div>
          <Bar used={user.storageUsed||0} total={user.storage}/>
          <div style={{marginTop:12,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:7}}>
            {myMats.slice(0,6).map(m=>(
              <div key={m.id}style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center",padding:"8px",background:C.g3,borderRadius:9,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                <span style={{fontSize:18,flexShrink:0}}>{{pdf:"📄",doc:"📝",audio:"🎵",video:"🎬"}[m.type]||"📎"}</span>
                <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{m.title}</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{m.size}{m.sizeUnit} · ⬇️{m.downloads}</div>
                </div>
                <Bdg clr={m.public?C.lime:C.tf} sm>{m.public?"Público":"Privado"}</Bdg>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Card>
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>📅 Mis Sesiones</div>
          {mySessions.slice(0,6).map(s=>(
            <div key={s.id}style={{display:"flex",gap:10,padding:"10px 0",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <span className={s.status==="live"?"pulse":""}style={{width:7,height:7,borderRadius:"50%",background:s.status==="live"?C.red:s.status==="upcoming"?C.teal:C.lime,marginTop:4,flexShrink:0,display:"inline-block"}}/>
              <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{s.title}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{s.date} {s.time} · {s.duration}min · 👥{s.enrolled.length}/{s.maxStudents}</div>
              </div>
              <Bdg clr={s.status==="live"?C.red:s.status==="upcoming"?C.teal:C.lime} sm dot={s.status==="live"}>{s.status}</Bdg>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ PROFESSOR EARNINGS ════════════════════════════════════════
function ProfEarnings({user,db}){
  const C=useC();
  const sessions=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.professorId===user.id&&s.price>0&&s.status==="completed");
  const total=sessions.reduce((a,s)=>a+(s.price*s.enrolled.length),0);
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:12,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="Ingresos Totales" val={`$${total.toLocaleString("es-CO")}`} sub="COP" icon="💰" clr={C.gold}/>
        <StatCard label="Sesiones Pagas" val={sessions.length} icon="📹" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="Estudiantes atendidos" val={db.sessions.filter(s=>s.professorId===user.id).reduce((a,s)=>a+s.enrolled.length,0)} icon="👥" clr={C.p3}/>
      </div>
      <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>📋 Detalle de Ingresos</div>
        {sessions.length===0?<div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"24px 0",color:C.tf}}>Sin sesiones de pago registradas aún.</div>
        :<table>
          <thead><tr><th>Sesión</th><th>Precio</th><th>Inscritos</th><th>Total</th><th>Fecha</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {sessions.map(s=>(
              <tr key={s.id}>
                <td style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>{s.title}</td>
                <td style={{color:C.gold,fontWeight:700}}>${s.price.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</td>
                <td style={{color:C.p3,fontWeight:700}}>{s.enrolled.length}</td>
                <td style={{color:C.lime,fontWeight:800}}>${(s.price*s.enrolled.length).toLocaleString("es-CO")}</td>
                <td style={{color:C.tf,fontSize:10}}>{s.date}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ STUDENT DASHBOARD ═════════════════════════════════════════
function StudentDash({user,db,upd,setTab}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const prog=db.progress[user.id]||{};
  const done=Object.values(prog).flat().length;
  const total=db.lessons.filter(l=>l.active).length;
  const live=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.status==="live"&&s.enrolled.includes(user.id));
  const pinned=db.announcements.filter(a=>a.pinned);
  const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===user.plan)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      {live.length>0&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:"14px 18px",background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.red}18,${C.p}0a)`,border:`1px solid ${C.red}45`}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:12}}>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}>
              <span className="pulse"style={{width:10,height:10,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.red,boxShadow:`0 0 12px ${C.red}`,display:"inline-block",flexShrink:0}}/>
              <div><div style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:700}}>🔴 {live[0].title}</div><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{live[0].professorName}</div></div>
            </div>
            <a href={live[0].link||"#"} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{padding:"9px 20px",borderRadius:10,background:C.red,color:"#fff",fontWeight:700,fontSize:12,boxShadow:`0 3px 16px ${C.red}55`}}>Entrar ahora →</a>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
      {pinned.map(a=>(
        <Card key={a.id}sx={{padding:"12px 16px",border:`1px solid ${C.p}28`,background:`${C.p}08`}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10}}><span style={{fontSize:18,flexShrink:0}}>📢</span><div><div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:3}}>{a.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.6}}>{a.body}</div></div></div>
        </Card>
      ))}
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="XP Total" val={user.xp.toLocaleString()} icon="⭐" clr={C.gold} trend={12}/>
        <StatCard label="Racha" val={`${user.streak}d`} icon="🔥" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="Completadas" val={`${done}/${total}`} icon="📚" clr={C.teal}/>
        <StatCard label="Almac." val={fmtBytes(user.storageUsed||0)} sub={`de ${fmtBytes(user.storage)}`} icon="☁️" clr={plan.color} pct={fmtPct(user.storageUsed||0,user.storage)}/>
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1.4fr 1fr",gap:16}}>
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:16}}>🗺️ Tu Ruta de Aprendizaje</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:0,marginBottom:18,position:"relative"}}>
            {db.levels.map((lv,i)=>{const un=user.xp>=lv.xpReq,curr=lv.code===user.level;return(
              <div key={lv.id}style={{flex:1,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",alignItems:"center",gap:3,position:"relative"}}>
                {i>0&&<div style={{position:"absolute",right:"50%",top:9,width:"100%",height:2,background:un?lv.color:C.b,zIndex:0}}/>}
                <div style={{width:20,height:20,borderRadius:"50%",background:curr?lv.color:un?`${lv.color}25`:C.g3,border:`2px solid ${un?lv.color:C.b}`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:6,fontWeight:800,color:curr?"#fff":un?lv.color:C.tf,zIndex:1,boxShadow:curr?`0 0 14px ${lv.color}`:"",transition:"all .5s"}}>{lv.code}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:7,color:curr?lv.color:C.tf,textAlign:"center",fontWeight:curr?700:400}}>{lv.name}</div>
              </div>
            );})}
          </div>
          <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:11,padding:13,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
            <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:C.tm,marginBottom:6}}><span>Hacia A2</span><span>{user.xp}/500 XP</span></div>
            <div style={{height:6,background:C.b,borderRadius:99,overflow:"hidden"}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${Math.min(user.xp/5,100)}%`,background:`linear-gradient(90deg,${C.teal},${C.p})`,borderRadius:99,transition:"width 1.2s ease"}}/></div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginTop:5,textAlign:"center"}}>{Math.max(0,500-user.xp)} XP para desbloquear A2</div>
          </div>
        </Card>
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>☁️ Mi Hosting</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:14,padding:12,background:C.g3,borderRadius:11,border:`1px solid ${plan.color}28`}}>
            <span style={{fontSize:22}}>{plan.icon}</span>
            <div><div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,color:plan.color}}>{plan.name}</div><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{fmtBytes(plan.storage)} · {plan.bandwidth}GB BW</div></div>
          </div>
          <Bar used={user.storageUsed||0} total={user.storage}/>
          <Btn full sm onClick={()=>setTab("hosting")} sx={{marginTop:12}} clr={plan.color}>Gestionar hosting →</Btn>
        </Card>
      </div>
      <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:14,flexWrap:"wrap",gap:8}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700}}>⚡ Continúa aprendiendo</div>
          <Btn sm ghost onClick={()=>setTab("courses")}>Ver todo →</Btn>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(3,1fr)",gap:10}}>
          {db.lessons.filter(l=>l.active).slice(0,3).map(l=>{
            const isDone=(prog[l.levelCode]||[]).includes(l.id);
            const tC={vocabulary:C.teal,grammar:C.p3,conversation:C.lime,exam:C.red,listening:C.gold,writing:C.amber}[l.type]||C.p;
            return(
              <div key={l.id}style={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${isDone?C.lime+"40":C.b}`,borderRadius:13,padding:14,cursor:"pointer",transition:"all .2s"}}
                onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=tC;}}
                onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=isDone?C.lime+"40":C.b;}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:7}}><Bdg clr={tC}>{l.type}</Bdg>{isDone&&<span>✅</span>}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:4,lineHeight:1.3}}>{l.title}</div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginTop:8}}><span>⏱{l.duration}</span><span style={{color:C.gold,fontWeight:700}}>+{l.xp}XP</span></div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ AI TUTOR ══════════════════════════════════════════════════
function AiTutor({user}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[msgs,setM]=useState([{r:"ai",t:"¡Hola! 👋 Soy tu tutor de inglés con IA — Claude AI. Puedo corregirte, explicarte gramática, practicar conversación contigo y más. ¿Empezamos? 🚀"}]);
  const[inp,setI]=useState("");const[load,setL]=useState(false);const[xp,setX]=useState(0);const[lang,setLang]=useState("both");
  const bot=useRef(null);
  useEffect(()=>bot.current?.scrollIntoView({behavior:"smooth"}),[msgs]);

  const send=async()=>{
    if(!inp.trim()||load)return;
    const t=inp.trim();setI("");setM(m=>[...m,{r:"user",t}]);setL(true);
    const h=msgs.map(m=>({role:m.r==="ai"?"assistant":"user",content:m.t}));h.push({role:"user",content:t});
    const rep=await callAI(h,`Eres un tutor experto de inglés para estudiantes colombianos en la plataforma "Speak Fluently". Modo de idioma: ${lang} (both=mezcla pedagógica, english=inglés principalmente, spanish=español principalmente). Cuando el usuario escribe en inglés, corrige sus errores entre [corchetes]. Sé motivador, claro y conciso (máx 100 palabras).`);
    setM(m=>[...m,{r:"ai",t:rep}]);setX(x=>x+5);setL(false);
  };

  const SUGS=["Correct my sentence: I go to school yesterday","¿Cómo se dice 'tengo que irme'?","Top 5 phrasal verbs for work","Explain Present Perfect","Pronunciation tip for 'th'"];

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",height:"calc(100vh - 180px)",minHeight:420}}>
      <Card sx={{borderRadius:"15px 15px 0 0",padding:"12px 18px",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:10}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:11,alignItems:"center"}}>
          <div className="float"style={{width:44,height:44,borderRadius:12,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:20,boxShadow:`0 0 20px ${C.p}60`,flexShrink:0}}>🤖</div>
          <div><div style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:700}}>Tutor IA · Claude AI</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.lime,display:"flex",gap:5,alignItems:"center"}}><span className="pulse"style={{width:5,height:5,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.lime,display:"inline-block"}}/>Activo 24/7</div></div>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
          {xp>0&&<Bdg clr={C.gold}>+{xp} XP</Bdg>}
          <select value={lang} onChange={e=>setLang(e.target.value)} style={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:8,padding:"5px 9px",color:C.t,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer"}}>
            <option value="both">🌐 Bilingüe</option><option value="english">🇺🇸 Inglés</option><option value="spanish">🇨🇴 Español</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </Card>
      <div style={{flex:1,overflowY:"auto",padding:"16px 18px",background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderTop:"none",display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
        {msgs.map((m,i)=>(
          <div key={i}className="afi"style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:m.r==="user"?"flex-end":"flex-start",gap:8}}>
            {m.r==="ai"&&<div style={{width:30,height:30,borderRadius:8,background:`${C.p}18`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}28`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:14,flexShrink:0}}>🤖</div>}
            <div style={{maxWidth:"76%",padding:"10px 15px",borderRadius:m.r==="user"?"17px 4px 17px 17px":"4px 17px 17px 17px",background:m.r==="user"?C.p:C.g1,border:m.r==="user"?"none":`1px solid ${C.b}`,fontSize:13,color:m.r==="user"?"#fff":C.t,lineHeight:1.75,boxShadow:m.r==="user"?`0 3px 14px ${C.p}40`:"none",whiteSpace:"pre-wrap"}}>{m.t}</div>
            {m.r==="user"&&<AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={30}/>}
          </div>
        ))}
        {load&&<div style={{display:"flex",gap:8}}><div style={{width:30,height:30,borderRadius:8,background:`${C.p}18`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:14}}>🤖</div><div style={{padding:"10px 14px",borderRadius:"4px 14px 14px 14px",background:C.g1,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}><div style={{display:"flex",gap:4}}>{[0,1,2].map(i=><div key={i}className="pulse"style={{width:6,height:6,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.p2,animationDelay:`${i*.2}s`}}/>)}</div></div></div>}
        <div ref={bot}/>
      </div>
      <div style={{background:C.g1,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderTop:"none",padding:"7px 10px",display:"flex",gap:5,overflowX:"auto"}}className="noscroll">
        {SUGS.map((s,i)=><button key={i} onClick={()=>setI(s)} style={{padding:"4px 11px",borderRadius:99,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,background:"transparent",color:C.tm,fontSize:10,whiteSpace:"nowrap",cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",flexShrink:0,transition:"all .15s"}} onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.p;e.currentTarget.style.color=C.t;}} onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.b2;e.currentTarget.style.color=C.tm;}}>{s}</button>)}
      </div>
      <Card sx={{borderRadius:"0 0 15px 15px",padding:"12px 16px",display:"flex",gap:9,borderTop:"none"}}>
        <input value={inp} onChange={e=>setI(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e=>e.key==="Enter"&&!e.shiftKey&&send()} placeholder="Escribe en inglés o español..."
          style={{flex:1,background:C.g3,border:`1.5px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:10,padding:"10px 14px",color:C.t,fontSize:13,outline:"none",transition:"border-color .2s"}}
          onFocus={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=C.p} onBlur={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=C.b}/>
        <button onClick={send} disabled={!inp.trim()||load} style={{width:44,height:44,borderRadius:10,background:inp.trim()&&!load?C.p:C.g3,border:"none",color:"#fff",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:18,cursor:inp.trim()&&!load?"pointer":"default",transition:"all .2s",flexShrink:0}}>
          {load?<Spin/>:"➤"}
        </button>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN USERS ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminUsers({db,upd,initNew}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const blank={name:"",email:"",pw:"",role:"student",level:"A1",plan:"h_free",xp:0,streak:0,joined:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],provider:"email",bio:"",phone:"",storage:500,storageUsed:0,bandwidth:1,bwUsed:0,apiKey:"",webhookUrl:"",subdomains:[],active:true,verified:false,planExp:""};
  const[m,setM]=useState(initNew?"new":null);const[f,setF]=useState(blank);const[q,setQ]=useState("");
  const filtered=db.users.filter(u=>u.name.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())||u.email.includes(q));
  const save=()=>{
    if(!f.name||!f.email)return;
    const av=(f.av||f.name).split(" ").map(w=>w[0]).join("").toUpperCase().slice(0,2);
    const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===f.plan)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];
    const apiKey=f.apiKey||`sk-sf-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    upd(p=>{
      if(m==="new"){const id=Math.max(0,...p.users.map(u=>u.id))+1;return{...p,users:[...p.users,{...f,id,av,apiKey,storage:plan.storage,bandwidth:plan.bandwidth}]};}
      return{...p,users:p.users.map(u=>u.id===m.id?{...f,id:u.id,av,apiKey}:u)};
    });setM(null);
  };
  const del=(id)=>{if(id===1)return alert("No puedes eliminar al admin principal.");if(window.confirm("¿Eliminar?"))upd(p=>({...p,users:p.users.filter(u=>u.id!==id)}));};
  const RC=ROLE_CLR;

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <Inp sx={{flex:1,minWidth:160}} value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="🔍 Buscar..." icon="🔍"/>
        <Btn onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM("new");}} clr={C.lime} icon="＋">Nuevo Usuario</Btn>
      </div>

      {m&&(
        <Modal title={m==="new"?"Nuevo Usuario":`Editar: ${m.name||""}`} onClose={()=>setM(null)} w={700}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="Nombre" value={f.name} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,name:v}))} req/>
              <Inp label="Correo" value={f.email} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,email:v}))} type="email" req/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="Contraseña" value={f.pw} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,pw:v}))} type="password"/>
              <Inp label="Teléfono" value={f.phone||""} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,phone:v}))} type="tel"/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Rol" value={f.role} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,role:v}))} opts={[{v:"student",l:"🎓 Estudiante"},{v:"professor",l:"👨‍🏫 Profesor"},{v:"admin",l:"👑 Admin"},{v:"developer",l:"💻 Dev"}]}/>
              <Sel label="Nivel" value={f.level} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,level:v}))} opts={["A1","A2","B1","B2","C1","C2","Admin"].map(l=>({v:l,l}))}/>
              <Sel label="Plan" value={f.plan} onChange={v=>{const pl=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===v);setF(x=>({...x,plan:v,storage:pl?.storage||500,bandwidth:pl?.bandwidth||1}));}} opts={HOSTING_PLANS.map(p=>({v:p.id,l:`${p.icon} ${p.name}`}))}/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="XP" value={String(f.xp)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,xp:parseInt(v)||0}))} type="number"/>
              <Inp label="Racha (días)" value={String(f.streak||0)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,streak:parseInt(v)||0}))} type="number"/>
              <Inp label="Plan vence" value={f.planExp||""} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,planExp:v}))} type="date"/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="Storage usado (MB)" value={String(f.storageUsed||0)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,storageUsed:parseInt(v)||0}))} type="number"/>
              <Inp label="Webhook URL" value={f.webhookUrl||""} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,webhookUrl:v}))} placeholder="https://..."/>
            </div>
            <Inp label="Bio" value={f.bio||""} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,bio:v}))} multi rows={2}/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10}}>
              <Toggle on={f.active} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,active:v}))} label="Cuenta activa"/>
              <Toggle on={f.verified} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,verified:v}))} label="Email verificado"/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}>
              <Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(null)}>Cancelar</Btn>
              <Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>{m==="new"?"Crear":"Guardar"}</Btn>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {sm?(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:9}}>
          {filtered.map(u=>{const pl=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===u.plan);return(
            <Card key={u.id}sx={{padding:14}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:8}}>
                <AvatarCircle name={u.name} size={38} clr={RC[u.role]}/>
                <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700}}>{u.name}</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{u.email}</div>
                </div>
                <Bdg clr={RC[u.role]||C.p}>{u.role}</Bdg>
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:8}}>
                <Bdg clr={pl?.color||C.p} sm>{pl?.icon} {pl?.name}</Bdg>
                <Bdg clr={C.teal} sm>💾{fmtBytes(u.storageUsed||0)}</Bdg>
                {u.apiKey&&<Bdg clr={C.lime} sm>API ✓</Bdg>}
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}><Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...u});setM(u);}}>✏️</Btn>{u.id!==1&&<Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(u.id)}>🗑️</Btn>}</div>
            </Card>
          );})}
        </div>
      ):(
        <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Usuario</th><th>Rol</th><th>Plan</th><th>Almacenamiento</th><th>API</th><th>Estado</th><th>Acciones</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {filtered.map(u=>{const pl=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===u.plan);return(
                  <tr key={u.id}>
                    <td><div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,alignItems:"center"}}><AvatarCircle name={u.name} size={32} clr={RC[u.role]}/><div><div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>{u.name}</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{u.email}</div></div></div></td>
                    <td><Bdg clr={RC[u.role]||C.p}>{ROLE_IC[u.role]} {u.role}</Bdg></td>
                    <td><Bdg clr={pl?.color||C.p}>{pl?.icon} {pl?.name}</Bdg></td>
                    <td style={{minWidth:120}}><Bar used={u.storageUsed||0} total={u.storage}/></td>
                    <td><Bdg clr={u.apiKey?C.lime:C.tf}>{u.apiKey?"✓ Activa":"—"}</Bdg></td>
                    <td><Bdg clr={u.active?C.lime:C.red} dot={u.active}>{u.active?"Activo":"Inactivo"}</Bdg></td>
                    <td><div style={{display:"flex",gap:5}}><Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...u});setM(u);}}>✏️</Btn>{u.id!==1&&<Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(u.id)}>🗑️</Btn>}</div></td>
                  </tr>
                );})}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN PAYMENTS ════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminPayments({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[m,setM]=useState(false);
  const[selTxn,setSelTxn]=useState(null);
  const[filterStatus,setFilterStatus]=useState("all");
  const[filterMethod,setFilterMethod]=useState("all");
  const[tab2,setTab2]=useState("transactions");
  const blank={userId:"",plan:"h_pro",planName:"Profesional",amount:89900,status:"APPROVED",method:"PSE",date:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],type:"plan"};
  const[f,setF]=useState(blank);

  const approved=db.payments.filter(p=>p.status==="APPROVED");
  const rev=approved.reduce((a,p)=>a+p.amount,0);
  const pending=db.payments.filter(p=>p.status==="PENDING");
  const declined=db.payments.filter(p=>p.status==="DECLINED");

  // Revenue by method
  const byMethod=["PSE","NEQUI","BANCOLOMBIA","CREDIT_CARD","DAVIPLATA","EFECTY"].map(m=>({
    m,count:approved.filter(p=>p.method===m).length,
    rev:approved.filter(p=>p.method===m).reduce((a,p)=>a+p.amount,0)
  })).filter(x=>x.count>0).sort((a,b)=>b.rev-a.rev);

  // Revenue by plan
  const byPlan=HOSTING_PLANS.map(p=>({
    ...p,count:approved.filter(x=>x.plan===p.id).length,
    rev:approved.filter(x=>x.plan===p.id).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.amount,0)
  })).filter(x=>x.count>0);

  // MRR simulation (last 6 months)
  const months=["Oct","Nov","Dic","Ene","Feb","Mar"];
  const mrrData=months.map((m,i)=>({m,v:Math.round(rev*(0.4+i*0.12))}));
  const maxMRR=Math.max(...mrrData.map(d=>d.v));

  const save=()=>{
    const usr=db.users.find(u=>u.id===parseInt(f.userId));
    const n=db.payments.length+1;
    upd(p=>({...p,payments:[{...f,id:`TXN-${String(n).padStart(3,"0")}`,userId:parseInt(f.userId),userName:usr?.name||"—",ref:`SF-${f.plan}-${Date.now()}`,email:usr?.email||"",gateway:"wompi"},...p.payments]}));setM(false);
  };

  const approvePayment=(id)=>upd(p=>({...p,payments:p.payments.map(x=>x.id===id?{...x,status:"APPROVED"}:x)}));
  const refundPayment=(id)=>upd(p=>({...p,payments:p.payments.map(x=>x.id===id?{...x,status:"REFUNDED"}:x)}));

  const filtered=db.payments.filter(p=>(filterStatus==="all"||p.status===filterStatus)&&(filterMethod==="all"||p.method===filterMethod));

  const MICONS={PSE:"🏦",NEQUI:"📱",BANCOLOMBIA:"🔵",CREDIT_CARD:"💳",DAVIPLATA:"📲",EFECTY:"💵"};

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      {/* KPI Row */}
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="Ingresos Totales" val={`$${(rev/1000000).toFixed(2)}M`} sub={`COP · ${BANCO.cuenta}`} icon="💰" clr={C.gold} trend={12}/>
        <StatCard label="Aprobados" val={approved.length} sub={`${Math.round(approved.length/Math.max(db.payments.length,1)*100)}% conversión`} icon="✅" clr={C.lime}/>
        <StatCard label="Pendientes" val={pending.length} sub="Requieren atención" icon="⏳" clr={C.gold}/>
        <StatCard label="Declinados" val={declined.length} sub={`${Math.round(declined.length/Math.max(db.payments.length,1)*100)}% tasa`} icon="❌" clr={C.red}/>
      </div>

      {/* Bank info prominent */}
      <Card sx={{padding:18,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.gold}12,${C.p}08)`,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}28`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:12}}>
          <div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.gold,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:6}}>💳 Cuenta Destino Wompi Colombia</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:16,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              {[["Banco",BANCO.banco],[`Cuenta`,BANCO.cuenta],["Titular",BANCO.titular],["Email",BANCO.email]].map(([k,v])=>(
                <div key={k}><div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,fontWeight:700,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1}}>{k}</div><div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,fontFamily:k==="Cuenta"?"'JetBrains Mono',monospace":"inherit",color:k==="Cuenta"?C.teal:C.t}}>{v}</div></div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7,alignItems:"center"}}>
            <Bdg clr={C.lime} dot>Wompi Activo</Bdg>
            <Btn sm onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM(true);}} clr={C.lime}>+ Registrar</Btn>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <Tabs tabs={[{id:"transactions",l:"Transacciones",ic:"📋"},{id:"analytics",l:"Analíticas",ic:"📊"},{id:"plans-rev",l:"Por Plan",ic:"🏷️"},{id:"methods",l:"Métodos",ic:"💳"}]} active={tab2} onChange={setTab2}/>

      {/* TRANSACTIONS TAB */}
      {tab2==="transactions"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
          {/* Filters */}
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5}}>
              {["all","APPROVED","PENDING","DECLINED"].map(s=>(
                <button key={s} onClick={()=>setFilterStatus(s)} style={{padding:"6px 12px",borderRadius:8,border:`1px solid ${filterStatus===s?C.p:C.b}`,background:filterStatus===s?`${C.p}18`:"transparent",color:filterStatus===s?C.p:C.tm,fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>
                  {s==="all"?"Todos":s}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <select value={filterMethod} onChange={e=>setFilterMethod(e.target.value)} style={{background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:8,padding:"5px 10px",color:C.t,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer",outline:"none"}}>
              <option value="all">Todos los métodos</option>
              {["PSE","NEQUI","BANCOLOMBIA","CREDIT_CARD","DAVIPLATA","EFECTY"].map(m=><option key={m} value={m}>{m}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Pending alerts */}
          {pending.length>0&&(
            <div style={{background:`${C.gold}0e`,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}28`,borderRadius:12,padding:14}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.gold,marginBottom:8}}>⏳ {pending.length} pagos pendientes de confirmación</div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:6}}>
                {pending.map(p=>(
                  <div key={p.id} style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:8}}>
                    <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{p.id} · {p.userName} · <span style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.gold}}>${p.amount.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</span></div>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}><Btn xs clr={C.lime} onClick={()=>approvePayment(p.id)}>✅ Aprobar</Btn><Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>refundPayment(p.id)}>❌ Rechazar</Btn></div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
            <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
              <table>
                <thead><tr><th>Referencia</th><th>Usuario</th><th>Plan/Addon</th><th>Monto COP</th><th>Método</th><th>Estado</th><th>Fecha</th><th>Acciones</th></tr></thead>
                <tbody>
                  {filtered.map(p=>{
                    const statusCol=p.status==="APPROVED"?C.lime:p.status==="PENDING"?C.gold:p.status==="REFUNDED"?C.teal:C.red;
                    return(
                      <tr key={p.id} style={{cursor:"pointer"}} onClick={()=>setSelTxn(selTxn?.id===p.id?null:p)}>
                        <td style={{color:C.teal,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:9}}>{p.id}</td>
                        <td><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>{p.userName}</div>{p.email&&<div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{p.email}</div>}</td>
                        <td><div style={{display:"flex",gap:4,flexWrap:"wrap"}}><Bdg clr={planColor(p.plan)} sm>{p.planName}</Bdg><Bdg clr={p.type==="addon"?C.amber:C.p} sm>{p.type}</Bdg></div></td>
                        <td style={{fontWeight:800,color:C.gold,fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif"}}>${p.amount.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</td>
                        <td><div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:4,fontSize:10,color:C.tm}}><span>{MICONS[p.method]||"💳"}</span>{p.method}</div></td>
                        <td><Bdg clr={statusCol} sm dot={p.status==="PENDING"}>{p.status}</Bdg></td>
                        <td style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{p.date}</td>
                        <td>
                          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:4}} onClick={e=>e.stopPropagation()}>
                            {p.status==="PENDING"&&<Btn xs clr={C.lime} onClick={()=>approvePayment(p.id)}>✅</Btn>}
                            {p.status==="APPROVED"&&<Btn xs outline clr={C.gold} onClick={()=>refundPayment(p.id)}>↩</Btn>}
                          </div>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </Card>

          {/* Transaction detail */}
          {selTxn&&(
            <Card sx={{padding:20,border:`1px solid ${C.p}30`}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:14}}>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700}}>🧾 Detalle: {selTxn.id}</div>
                <button onClick={()=>setSelTxn(null)} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.tf,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:16}}>✕</button>
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:10}}>
                {[["Referencia",selTxn.ref||selTxn.id,"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"],["Usuario",selTxn.userName,"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"],["Email",selTxn.email,"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"],["Plan",selTxn.planName,"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"],["Método",selTxn.method,"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"],["Gateway",selTxn.gateway||"wompi","'JetBrains Mono',monospace"]].map(([l,v,ff])=>(
                  <div key={l} style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:9,padding:"10px 13px",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                    <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:4}}>{l}</div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:11,fontFamily:ff,color:C.t,wordBreak:"break-all"}}>{v||"—"}</div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <div style={{marginTop:12,display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center",padding:12,background:`${C.p}08`,borderRadius:10,border:`1px solid ${C.p}18`}}>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:24,fontWeight:900,color:planColor(selTxn.plan)}}>${selTxn.amount.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</div>
                <div style={{flex:1,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>COP · {selTxn.date}</div>
                <Bdg clr={selTxn.status==="APPROVED"?C.lime:selTxn.status==="PENDING"?C.gold:C.red} dot={selTxn.status==="PENDING"}>{selTxn.status}</Bdg>
              </div>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ANALYTICS TAB */}
      {tab2==="analytics"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
          <Card sx={{padding:22}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:16}}>📈 MRR — Tendencia 6 Meses</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"flex-end",gap:8,height:160,padding:"0 8px"}}>
              {mrrData.map((d,i)=>{
                const pct=Math.round(d.v/maxMRR*100);const isLast=i===mrrData.length-1;
                return(
                  <div key={d.m} style={{flex:1,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",alignItems:"center",gap:4}}>
                    <div style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:isLast?C.gold:C.tm}}>${(d.v/1000).toFixed(0)}k</div>
                    <div style={{width:"100%",borderRadius:"6px 6px 0 0",height:`${pct}%`,minHeight:8,
                      background:isLast?`linear-gradient(180deg,${C.gold},${C.amber})`:`linear-gradient(180deg,${C.p}99,${C.p}44)`,
                      boxShadow:isLast?`0 0 18px ${C.gold}50`:"none",transition:"height 1s"}}/>
                    <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,fontWeight:700}}>{d.m}</div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <div style={{marginTop:12,display:"flex",gap:12,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>Crecimiento: <span style={{color:C.lime,fontWeight:700}}>+12% vs mes anterior</span></div>
              <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>LTV promedio: <span style={{color:C.gold,fontWeight:700}}>${Math.round(rev/Math.max(db.users.length,1)).toLocaleString("es-CO")} COP</span></div>
            </div>
          </Card>
          <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:14}}>
            <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>📊 Conversión por Estado</div>
              {[["APPROVED",approved.length,C.lime],["PENDING",pending.length,C.gold],["DECLINED",declined.length,C.red]].map(([s,n,col])=>(
                <div key={s} style={{marginBottom:10}}>
                  <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:11,marginBottom:3}}><span style={{color:C.tm}}>{s}</span><span style={{fontWeight:700,color:col}}>{n} ({Math.round(n/Math.max(db.payments.length,1)*100)}%)</span></div>
                  <div style={{height:6,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${Math.round(n/Math.max(db.payments.length,1)*100)}%`,background:col,borderRadius:99}}/></div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </Card>
            <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>🔁 Suscripciones Activas</div>
              {db.users.filter(u=>u.plan!=="h_free"&&u.planExp).slice(0,5).map(u=>{
                const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===u.plan);
                const daysLeft=Math.max(0,Math.round((new Date(u.planExp)-new Date())/(1000*60*60*24)));
                return(
                  <div key={u.id} style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",padding:"7px 0",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
                      <AvatarCircle name={u.name} size={24} clr={ROLE_CLR[u.role]}/>
                      <div><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>{u.name}</div><div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{plan?.name}</div></div>
                    </div>
                    <Bdg clr={daysLeft<7?C.red:daysLeft<15?C.gold:C.lime} sm>{daysLeft}d</Bdg>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </Card>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* BY PLAN TAB */}
      {tab2==="plans-rev"&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:16}}>💰 Ingresos por Plan</div>
          {byPlan.sort((a,b)=>b.rev-a.rev).map(p=>{
            const pct=Math.round(p.rev/Math.max(rev,1)*100);
            return(
              <div key={p.id} style={{marginBottom:14}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",marginBottom:5}}>
                  <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
                    <span style={{fontSize:18}}>{p.icon}</span>
                    <div>
                      <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>{p.name}</div>
                      <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{p.count} transacciones</div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div style={{textAlign:"right"}}>
                    <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:800,color:p.color,fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif"}}>${p.rev.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{pct}% del total</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div style={{height:7,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${pct}%`,background:`linear-gradient(90deg,${p.color},${p.color}aa)`,borderRadius:99,boxShadow:`0 0 10px ${p.color}60`}}/></div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          <div style={{borderTop:`2px solid ${C.b}`,paddingTop:12,marginTop:4,display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between"}}>
            <span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>TOTAL</span>
            <span style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:900,color:C.gold}}>${rev.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</span>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* METHODS TAB */}
      {tab2==="methods"&&(
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
          {byMethod.map(x=>(
            <Card key={x.m} sx={{padding:18}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:28,marginBottom:8}}>{MICONS[x.m]||"💳"}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:3}}>{x.m}</div>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:900,color:C.gold,marginBottom:4}}>${(x.rev/1000).toFixed(0)}k</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{x.count} transacciones · {Math.round(x.rev/Math.max(rev,1)*100)}% del total</div>
              <div style={{marginTop:10,height:4,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${Math.round(x.rev/Math.max(rev,1)*100)}%`,background:C.p,borderRadius:99}}/></div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Manual register modal */}
      {m&&(
        <Modal title="Registrar Pago Manual" onClose={()=>setM(false)} w={520}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <div style={{background:`${C.gold}0a`,borderRadius:10,padding:10,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}20`,fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>
              ⚠️ Usa esto solo para registrar pagos que llegaron directamente al banco sin pasar por Wompi.
            </div>
            <Sel label="Usuario" value={String(f.userId)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,userId:v}))} opts={[{v:"",l:"— Seleccionar —"},...db.users.map(u=>({v:String(u.id),l:`${u.name} (${u.email})`}))]}/>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Plan" value={f.plan} onChange={v=>{const p=HOSTING_PLANS.find(x=>x.id===v)||ADDON_PLANS.find(x=>x.id===v);setF(x=>({...x,plan:v,planName:p?.name||v,amount:p?.price||0}));}} opts={[...HOSTING_PLANS.map(p=>({v:p.id,l:`${p.icon} ${p.name}`})),...ADDON_PLANS.map(p=>({v:p.id,l:`${p.icon} ${p.name} (Addon)`}))]}/>
              <Inp label="Monto COP" value={String(f.amount)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,amount:parseInt(v)||0}))} type="number"/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Estado" value={f.status} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,status:v}))} opts={["APPROVED","PENDING","DECLINED"].map(s=>({v:s,l:s}))}/>
              <Sel label="Método" value={f.method} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,method:v}))} opts={["PSE","NEQUI","BANCOLOMBIA","CREDIT_CARD","EFECTY","DAVIPLATA","TRANSFERENCIA"].map(s=>({v:s,l:s}))}/>
            </div>
            <Inp label="Fecha" value={f.date} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,date:v}))} type="date"/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}><Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(false)}>Cancelar</Btn><Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>💾 Registrar</Btn></div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ PROFILE VIEW ══════════════════════════════════════════════
function ProfileView({user,db,upd,setUser}){
  const C=useC();
  const[f,setF]=useState({name:user.name,email:user.email,phone:user.phone||"",pw:"",bio:user.bio||"",webhookUrl:user.webhookUrl||""});
  const[saved,setSaved]=useState(false);
  const save=()=>{
    const av=f.name.split(" ").map(w=>w[0]).join("").toUpperCase().slice(0,2);
    const updated={...user,...f,av,pw:f.pw||user.pw};
    upd(p=>({...p,users:p.users.map(u=>u.id===user.id?updated:u)}));
    setUser(updated);setSaved(true);setTimeout(()=>setSaved(false),2500);
  };
  const plan=HOSTING_PLANS.find(p=>p.id===user.plan)||HOSTING_PLANS[0];
  const rc=ROLE_CLR[user.role]||C.p;

  return(
    <div style={{maxWidth:640,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      <Card sx={{padding:26}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:18,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:22,paddingBottom:22,borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          <AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={68} clr={rc}/>
          <div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:3}}>{user.name}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginBottom:8}}>{user.email}</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              <Bdg clr={rc}>{ROLE_IC[user.role]} {user.role}</Bdg>
              <Bdg clr={C.teal}>{user.level}</Bdg>
              <Bdg clr={plan.color}>{plan.icon} {plan.name}</Bdg>
              {user.verified&&<Bdg clr={C.lime}>✓ Verificado</Bdg>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
          <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
            <Inp label="Nombre" value={f.name} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,name:v}))}/>
            <Inp label="Teléfono" value={f.phone} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,phone:v}))} type="tel"/>
          </div>
          <Inp label="Correo" value={f.email} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,email:v}))} type="email"/>
          <Inp label="Bio" value={f.bio} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,bio:v}))} multi rows={2} placeholder="Cuéntanos sobre ti..."/>
          <Inp label="Webhook URL" value={f.webhookUrl} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,webhookUrl:v}))} placeholder="https://tusitio.com/webhook"/>
          <Inp label="Nueva contraseña" value={f.pw} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,pw:v}))} type="password" placeholder="Dejar en blanco para no cambiar"/>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}>
            <Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>💾 Guardar</Btn>
            {saved&&<Bdg clr={C.lime}>✓ Guardado</Bdg>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
      <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:14}}>🔑 Mis Credenciales API</div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8}}>
          {[["API Key",user.apiKey,"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",C.teal],["Plan activo",plan.name,"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",plan.color],["Almacenamiento",`${fmtBytes(user.storageUsed||0)} / ${fmtBytes(user.storage)}`,"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",C.p3]].map(([l,v,ff,col])=>(
            <div key={l}style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:"10px 13px",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}>
              <span style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5}}>{l}</span>
              <code style={{fontSize:11,fontFamily:ff,color:col,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap",maxWidth:260}}>{v||"—"}</code>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN SESSIONS ════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminSessions({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const blank={title:"",professorId:3,professorName:"Prof. Carlos Ruiz",subjectId:1,levelCode:"B1",date:"",time:"18:00",duration:60,maxStudents:12,link:"",status:"upcoming",description:"",price:0};
  const[m,setM]=useState(null);const[f,setF]=useState(blank);
  const save=()=>{
    if(!f.title||!f.date)return;
    upd(p=>{
      if(m==="new"){const id=Math.max(0,...p.sessions.map(s=>s.id))+1;return{...p,sessions:[...p.sessions,{...f,id,professorId:parseInt(f.professorId),subjectId:parseInt(f.subjectId),enrolled:[],materials:[],recording:""}]};}
      return{...p,sessions:p.sessions.map(s=>s.id===m.id?{...f,id:s.id,enrolled:s.enrolled,materials:s.materials,recording:s.recording||""}:s)};
    });setM(null);
  };
  const del=(id)=>{if(window.confirm("¿Eliminar sesión?"))upd(p=>({...p,sessions:p.sessions.filter(s=>s.id!==id)}));};
  const setStatus=(id,st)=>upd(p=>({...p,sessions:p.sessions.map(s=>s.id===id?{...s,status:st}:s)}));
  const SC={live:C.red,upcoming:C.teal,completed:C.lime,cancelled:C.tf};

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:10}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700}}>Sesiones Live ({db.sessions.length})</div>
        <Btn onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM("new");}} clr={C.red}>+ Nueva Sesión</Btn>
      </div>
      {m&&(
        <Modal title={m==="new"?"Nueva Sesión":"Editar Sesión"} onClose={()=>setM(null)} w={600}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <Inp label="Título" value={f.title} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,title:v}))} req/>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Profesor" value={String(f.professorId)} onChange={v=>{const u=db.users.find(x=>x.id===parseInt(v));setF(x=>({...x,professorId:parseInt(v),professorName:u?.name||""}));}} opts={db.users.filter(u=>u.role==="professor"||u.role==="admin").map(u=>({v:String(u.id),l:u.name}))}/>
              <Sel label="Materia" value={String(f.subjectId)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,subjectId:parseInt(v)}))} opts={db.subjects.map(s=>({v:String(s.id),l:`${s.icon} ${s.name}`}))}/>
            </div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Nivel" value={f.levelCode} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,levelCode:v}))} opts={["A1","A2","B1","B2","C1","C2"].map(l=>({v:l,l}))}/>
              <Inp label="Fecha" value={f.date} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,date:v}))} type="date" req/>
              <Inp label="Hora" value={f.time} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,time:v}))} type="time"/>
            </div>
