            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="Duración (min)" value={String(f.duration)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,duration:parseInt(v)||60}))} type="number"/>
              <Inp label="Máx. Estudiantes" value={String(f.maxStudents)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,maxStudents:parseInt(v)||12}))} type="number"/>
              <Inp label="Precio COP (0=gratis)" value={String(f.price)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,price:parseInt(v)||0}))} type="number"/>
            </div>
            <Inp label="Enlace Google Meet" value={f.link} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,link:v}))} placeholder="https://meet.google.com/..."/>
            <Inp label="Descripción" value={f.description} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,description:v}))} multi rows={2}/>
            <Sel label="Estado" value={f.status} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,status:v}))} opts={[{v:"upcoming",l:"📅 Próxima"},{v:"live",l:"🔴 En Vivo"},{v:"completed",l:"✅ Completada"},{v:"cancelled",l:"❌ Cancelada"}]}/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}><Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(null)}>Cancelar</Btn><Btn onClick={save} clr={C.red}>{m==="new"?"Crear":"Guardar"}</Btn></div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {sm?(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:9}}>
          {db.sessions.map(s=>{
            const sub=db.subjects.find(x=>x.id===s.subjectId);
            return(
              <Card key={s.id} sx={{padding:16}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:8}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,flex:1,marginRight:8}}>{s.title}</div>
                  <Bdg clr={SC[s.status]||C.p} dot={s.status==="live"}>{s.status}</Bdg>
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:8}}>
                  {sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon}</Bdg>}
                  <Bdg clr={C.p3} sm>{s.levelCode}</Bdg>
                  <span style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{s.date} {s.time} · {s.duration}min · 👥{s.enrolled.length}/{s.maxStudents}</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}>
                  <Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...s,subjectId:String(s.subjectId),professorId:String(s.professorId)});setM(s);}}>✏️</Btn>
                  {s.status!=="live"&&<Btn xs clr={C.red} onClick={()=>setStatus(s.id,"live")}>🔴 Live</Btn>}
                  {s.status==="live"&&<Btn xs clr={C.lime} onClick={()=>setStatus(s.id,"completed")}>✅ Fin</Btn>}
                  <Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(s.id)}>🗑️</Btn>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ):(
        <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Sesión</th><th>Materia</th><th>Nivel</th><th>Fecha/Hora</th><th>Inscritos</th><th>Precio</th><th>Estado</th><th>Acciones</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {db.sessions.map(s=>{
                  const sub=db.subjects.find(x=>x.id===s.subjectId);
                  return(
                    <tr key={s.id}>
                      <td><div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>{s.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{s.professorName}</div></td>
                      <td>{sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon} {sub.name}</Bdg>}</td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={C.p3} sm>{s.levelCode}</Bdg></td>
                      <td style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{s.date}<br/>{s.time} · {s.duration}min</td>
                      <td style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.teal}}>{s.enrolled.length}/{s.maxStudents}</td>
                      <td style={{color:s.price>0?C.gold:C.tf,fontWeight:700}}>{s.price>0?`$${s.price.toLocaleString("es-CO")}`:"Gratis"}</td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={SC[s.status]||C.p} dot={s.status==="live"}>{s.status}</Bdg></td>
                      <td>
                        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5}}>
                          <Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...s,subjectId:String(s.subjectId),professorId:String(s.professorId)});setM(s);}}>✏️</Btn>
                          {s.status!=="live"&&s.status!=="completed"&&<Btn xs clr={C.red} onClick={()=>setStatus(s.id,"live")}>🔴</Btn>}
                          {s.status==="live"&&<Btn xs clr={C.lime} onClick={()=>setStatus(s.id,"completed")}>✅</Btn>}
                          <Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(s.id)}>🗑️</Btn>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN LESSONS ═════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminLessons({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const blank={title:"",content:"",levelCode:"A1",subjectId:1,type:"vocabulary",xp:70,duration:"20 min",order:1,active:true};
  const[m,setM]=useState(null);const[f,setF]=useState(blank);const[q,setQ]=useState("");
  const filtered=db.lessons.filter(l=>l.title.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())||l.levelCode.includes(q.toUpperCase()));
  const save=()=>{
    if(!f.title)return;
    upd(p=>{
      if(m==="new"){const id=Math.max(0,...p.lessons.map(l=>l.id))+1;return{...p,lessons:[...p.lessons,{...f,id,subjectId:parseInt(f.subjectId),xp:parseInt(f.xp)||70,order:parseInt(f.order)||1,materials:[],aiGen:false}]};}
      return{...p,lessons:p.lessons.map(l=>l.id===m.id?{...f,id:l.id,subjectId:parseInt(f.subjectId),xp:parseInt(f.xp)||70}:l)};
    });setM(null);
  };
  const del=(id)=>{if(window.confirm("¿Eliminar lección?"))upd(p=>({...p,lessons:p.lessons.filter(l=>l.id!==id)}));};
  const tog=(id)=>upd(p=>({...p,lessons:p.lessons.map(l=>l.id===id?{...l,active:!l.active}:l)}));
  const TC={vocabulary:C.teal,grammar:C.p3,conversation:C.lime,exam:C.red,listening:C.gold,writing:C.amber};
  const TI={vocabulary:"📖",grammar:"✏️",conversation:"💬",exam:"📝",listening:"🎧",writing:"✍️"};

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <Inp sx={{flex:1,minWidth:160}} value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="🔍 Buscar lección..." icon="🔍"/>
        <Btn onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM("new");}} clr={C.lime}>+ Nueva Lección</Btn>
      </div>
      {m&&(
        <Modal title={m==="new"?"Nueva Lección":"Editar Lección"} onClose={()=>setM(null)} w={620}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <Inp label="Título" value={f.title} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,title:v}))} req/>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Nivel" value={f.levelCode} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,levelCode:v}))} opts={["A1","A2","B1","B2","C1","C2"].map(l=>({v:l,l}))}/>
              <Sel label="Materia" value={String(f.subjectId)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,subjectId:v}))} opts={db.subjects.map(s=>({v:String(s.id),l:`${s.icon} ${s.name}`}))}/>
              <Sel label="Tipo" value={f.type} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,type:v}))} opts={Object.keys(TI).map(k=>({v:k,l:`${TI[k]} ${k}`}))}/>
            </div>
            <Inp label="Contenido / Descripción" value={f.content} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,content:v}))} multi rows={3}/>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="XP" value={String(f.xp)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,xp:v}))} type="number"/>
              <Inp label="Duración" value={f.duration} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,duration:v}))} placeholder="20 min"/>
              <Inp label="Orden" value={String(f.order)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,order:v}))} type="number"/>
            </div>
            <Toggle on={f.active} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,active:v}))} label="Lección activa"/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}><Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(null)}>Cancelar</Btn><Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>{m==="new"?"Crear":"Guardar"}</Btn></div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {sm?(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8}}>
          {filtered.map(l=>{
            const sub=db.subjects.find(s=>s.id===l.subjectId);const tc=TC[l.type]||C.p;
            return(
              <Card key={l.id} sx={{padding:14,opacity:l.active?1:.6}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,marginBottom:8}}>
                  <div style={{width:38,height:38,borderRadius:10,background:`${tc}14`,border:`1px solid ${tc}22`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:17,flexShrink:0}}>{TI[l.type]||"📄"}</div>
                  <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}><div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700}}>{l.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{l.levelCode} · {l.duration} · ⭐{l.xp}XP</div></div>
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:8}}>
                  <Bdg clr={tc} sm>{l.type}</Bdg>
                  {sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon}</Bdg>}
                  <Bdg clr={l.active?C.lime:C.tf} sm>{l.active?"Activa":"Off"}</Bdg>
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}>
                  <Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...l,subjectId:String(l.subjectId)});setM(l);}}>✏️</Btn>
                  <Btn xs outline clr={l.active?C.gold:C.lime} onClick={()=>tog(l.id)}>{l.active?"⏸":"▶"}</Btn>
                  <Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(l.id)}>🗑️</Btn>
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      ):(
        <Card sx={{overflow:"hidden"}}>
          <div style={{overflowX:"auto"}}>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Lección</th><th>Nivel</th><th>Materia</th><th>Tipo</th><th>XP</th><th>Duración</th><th>Estado</th><th>Acciones</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {filtered.map(l=>{
                  const sub=db.subjects.find(s=>s.id===l.subjectId);const tc=TC[l.type]||C.p;
                  return(
                    <tr key={l.id} style={{opacity:l.active?1:.55}}>
                      <td><div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>{l.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,maxWidth:200,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{l.content}</div></td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={C.p3} sm>{l.levelCode}</Bdg></td>
                      <td>{sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon} {sub.name}</Bdg>}</td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={tc} sm>{TI[l.type]} {l.type}</Bdg></td>
                      <td style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.gold}}>+{l.xp}</td>
                      <td style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{l.duration}</td>
                      <td><Bdg clr={l.active?C.lime:C.tf} sm>{l.active?"Activa":"Off"}</Bdg></td>
                      <td>
                        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5}}>
                          <Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...l,subjectId:String(l.subjectId)});setM(l);}}>✏️</Btn>
                          <Btn xs outline clr={l.active?C.gold:C.lime} onClick={()=>tog(l.id)}>{l.active?"⏸":"▶"}</Btn>
                          <Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(l.id)}>🗑️</Btn>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN AI GENERATOR ════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminAI({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[type,setType]=useState("lesson");const[level,setLevel]=useState("B1");const[topic,setTopic]=useState("");
  const[load,setLoad]=useState(false);const[res,setRes]=useState(null);const[hist,setHist]=useState([]);
  const TYPES=[{v:"lesson",l:"📝 Lección",ic:"📝"},{v:"vocabulary",l:"📖 Vocabulario",ic:"📖"},{v:"quiz",l:"🎯 Quiz",ic:"🎯"},{v:"conversation",l:"💬 Conversación",ic:"💬"}];

  const gen=async()=>{
    if(!topic.trim())return;setLoad(true);setRes(null);
    const sys=`You are an expert English curriculum designer for Colombian students. Generate educational content in JSON only, no markdown fences. Respond ONLY with a valid JSON object.`;
    const prompt=`Generate a ${type} about "${topic}" for level ${level} English learners (Colombian Spanish speakers).

Return ONLY this JSON structure:
{
  "title": "lesson title",
  "description": "brief description in Spanish",
  "level": "${level}",
  "type": "${type}",
  "tip": "pedagogical tip in Spanish",
  "vocabulary": [{"word":"","spanish":"","definition":"","example":""}],
  "exercises": [{"question":"","options":["","","",""],"correct":0,"explanation":""}]
}`;
    const r=await callAI([{role:"user",content:prompt}],sys,1000);
    try{
      const parsed=JSON.parse(r.replace(/```json|```/g,"").trim());
      setRes(parsed);
      setHist(h=>[{level,type,title:parsed.title},...h].slice(0,10));
    }catch{setRes({title:`Lección: ${topic}`,description:"Contenido generado con IA.",level,type,tip:"Practica con tus estudiantes.",vocabulary:[{word:topic,spanish:"",definition:"Palabra o tema solicitado.",example:`Use ${topic} in a sentence.`}],exercises:[]});}
    setLoad(false);
  };

  const publish=()=>{
    if(!res)return;
    const id=Math.max(0,...db.lessons.map(l=>l.id))+1;
    const subj=db.subjects.find(s=>s.levels?.includes(level))||db.subjects[0];
    upd(p=>({...p,lessons:[...p.lessons,{id,title:res.title,content:res.description||"",levelCode:level,subjectId:subj?.id||1,type:res.type||type,xp:100,duration:"25 min",order:p.lessons.length+1,active:true,materials:[],aiGen:true}]}));
    alert(`✅ "${res.title}" publicada como lección!`);setRes(null);setTopic("");
  };

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <Card sx={{padding:22,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p}16,${C.teal}08)`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}30`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:13,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:18}}>
          <div className="float" style={{width:52,height:52,borderRadius:14,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:24,boxShadow:`0 0 24px ${C.p}60`,flexShrink:0}}>✨</div>
          <div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:19,fontWeight:900}}>Generador de Contenido IA</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>Powered by Claude AI · Crea lecciones, quizzes y vocabulario al instante</div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:12}}>
          {TYPES.map(t=>(
            <button key={t.v} onClick={()=>setType(t.v)}
              style={{padding:"7px 14px",borderRadius:10,border:`1.5px solid ${type===t.v?C.p:C.b}`,background:type===t.v?`${C.p}18`:"transparent",color:type===t.v?C.p2:C.tm,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"all .18s"}}>{t.l}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          <select value={level} onChange={e=>setLevel(e.target.value)}
            style={{background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,padding:"9px 13px",color:C.t,fontSize:12,cursor:"pointer",outline:"none"}}>

          {["A1","A2","B1","B2","C1","C2"].map(l=><option key={l} value={l}>{l}</option>)}
          </select>
          <input value={topic} onChange={e=>setTopic(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e=>e.key==="Enter"&&gen()}
            placeholder="Tema: Restaurantes, Aeropuertos, Tecnología..."
            style={{flex:1,minWidth:160,background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,padding:"9px 13px",color:C.t,fontSize:12,outline:"none",transition:"border-color .2s"}}
            onFocus={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=C.p} onBlur={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=C.b}/>
          <Btn onClick={gen} disabled={!topic.trim()||load} clr={C.p}>{load?<><Spin/>&nbsp;Generando...</>:"✨ Generar"}</Btn>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {res&&!load&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:24}} className="afu">
          <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:18,flexWrap:"wrap",gap:12}}>
            <div><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:3}}>{res.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,fontStyle:"italic"}}>{res.description}</div></div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8}}><Btn sm outline onClick={()=>setRes(null)}>Descartar</Btn><Btn sm onClick={publish} clr={C.lime}>📤 Publicar</Btn></div>
          </div>
          {res.tip&&<div style={{background:`${C.p}10`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}22`,borderRadius:11,padding:13,marginBottom:16,fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>💡 {res.tip}</div>}
          {res.vocabulary?.length>0&&(
            <div style={{marginBottom:18}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8,marginBottom:10}}>📖 Vocabulario ({res.vocabulary.length})</div>
              <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:8}}>
                {res.vocabulary.map((v,i)=>(
                  <div key={i}style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:"11px 13px",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"baseline",marginBottom:3}}><span style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,color:C.teal}}>{v.word}</span><span style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>({v.spanish})</span></div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginBottom:3}}>{v.definition}</div>
                    <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,fontStyle:"italic"}}>"{v.example}"</div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          {res.exercises?.length>0&&(
            <div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8,marginBottom:10}}>📝 Ejercicios ({res.exercises.length})</div>
              {res.exercises.map((ex,i)=>(
                <div key={i}style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:14,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,marginBottom:8}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:9}}>{i+1}. {ex.question}</div>
                  <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:5,marginBottom:7}}>
                    {ex.options?.map((o,j)=><div key={j}style={{padding:"6px 10px",borderRadius:7,background:j===ex.correct?`${C.lime}14`:"transparent",border:`1px solid ${j===ex.correct?C.lime:C.b}`,fontSize:11,color:j===ex.correct?C.lime:C.tm}}>{String.fromCharCode(65+j)}. {o}{j===ex.correct&&" ✓"}</div>)}
                  </div>
                  {ex.explanation&&<div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,fontStyle:"italic"}}>💡 {ex.explanation}</div>}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </Card>
      )}

      {hist.length>0&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:16}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:10}}>📚 Generadas recientemente</div>
          {hist.map((h,i)=>(
            <div key={i}style={{display:"flex",gap:7,alignItems:"center",padding:"7px 0",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <Bdg clr={C.p} sm>{h.level}</Bdg><Bdg clr={C.teal} sm>{h.type}</Bdg>
              <span style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,flex:1}}>{h.title}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN SUBJECTS ════════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminSubjects({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();
  const blank={name:"",code:"",icon:"📚",color:"#7850ff",desc:"",levels:["A1","A2","B1"],active:true};
  const[m,setM]=useState(null);const[f,setF]=useState(blank);
  const ALL=["A1","A2","B1","B2","C1","C2"];
  const save=()=>{
    if(!f.name)return;
    upd(p=>{if(m==="new"){const id=Math.max(0,...p.subjects.map(s=>s.id))+1;return{...p,subjects:[...p.subjects,{...f,id}]};}
    return{...p,subjects:p.subjects.map(s=>s.id===m.id?{...f,id:s.id}:s)};});setM(null);
  };
  const del=(id)=>{if(window.confirm("¿Eliminar?"))upd(p=>({...p,subjects:p.subjects.filter(s=>s.id!==id)}));};
  const tog=(id)=>upd(p=>({...p,subjects:p.subjects.map(s=>s.id===id?{...s,active:!s.active}:s)}));
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between"}}><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700}}>Materias ({db.subjects.length})</div><Btn onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM("new");}} clr={C.lime}>+ Nueva</Btn></div>
      {m&&(
        <Modal title={m==="new"?"Nueva Materia":"Editar Materia"} onClose={()=>setM(null)}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}><Inp label="Nombre" value={f.name} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,name:v}))} req/><Inp label="Código" value={f.code} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,code:v}))}/></div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}><Inp label="Emoji" value={f.icon} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,icon:v}))} placeholder="📚"/><div><label style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8,display:"block",marginBottom:5}}>Color</label><input type="color" value={f.color} onChange={e=>setF(x=>({...x,color:e.target.value}))} style={{width:"100%",height:40,borderRadius:9,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,background:"transparent",cursor:"pointer",padding:4}}/></div></div>
            <Inp label="Descripción" value={f.desc} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,desc:v}))} multi rows={2}/>
            <div><label style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.8,display:"block",marginBottom:8}}>Niveles</label><div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>{ALL.map(l=>{const s=f.levels?.includes(l);return <button key={l} onClick={()=>setF(x=>({...x,levels:s?x.levels.filter(v=>v!==l):[...(x.levels||[]),l]}))} style={{padding:"5px 13px",borderRadius:7,border:`1.5px solid ${s?C.p:C.b}`,background:s?`${C.p}18`:"transparent",color:s?C.p2:C.tm,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>{l}</button>;})} </div></div>
            <Toggle on={f.active} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,active:v}))} label="Activa"/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}><Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(null)}>Cancelar</Btn><Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>{m==="new"?"Crear":"Guardar"}</Btn></div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(auto-fill,minmax(240px,1fr))",gap:12}}>
        {db.subjects.map(s=>(
          <Card key={s.id}sx={{padding:20}}>
            <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:12}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:11,alignItems:"center"}}>
                <div style={{width:44,height:44,borderRadius:12,background:`${s.color}16`,border:`1px solid ${s.color}30`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:20}}>{s.icon}</div>
                <div><div style={{fontSize:14,fontWeight:700}}>{s.name}</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{s.code}</div></div>
              </div>
              <Bdg clr={s.active?C.lime:C.tf}>{s.active?"Activa":"Off"}</Bdg>
            </div>
            <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginBottom:10,fontStyle:"italic"}}>{s.desc}</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:12}}>{s.levels?.map(l=><Bdg key={l} clr={s.color} sm>{l}</Bdg>)}</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}><Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...s});setM(s);}}>✏️</Btn><Btn xs outline clr={s.active?C.gold:C.lime} onClick={()=>tog(s.id)}>{s.active?"⏸":"▶"}</Btn><Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(s.id)}>🗑️</Btn></div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN MATERIALS ═══════════════════════════════════════════
function AdminMaterials({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();
  const blank={title:"",type:"pdf",url:"",size:0,sizeUnit:"MB",lessonId:"",subjectId:String(db.subjects[0]?.id||1),public:true};
  const[m,setM]=useState(false);const[f,setF]=useState(blank);
  const TI={pdf:"📄",doc:"📝",audio:"🎵",video:"🎬",image:"🖼️",link:"🔗"};
  const TC={pdf:C.red,doc:C.teal,audio:C.lime,video:C.p3,image:C.gold,link:C.amber};
  const save=()=>{
    if(!f.title)return;
    const id=Date.now();
    upd(p=>({...p,materials:[...p.materials,{...f,id,lessonId:f.lessonId?parseInt(f.lessonId):null,subjectId:parseInt(f.subjectId),uploadedBy:1,ownerId:1,date:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],downloads:0}]}));setM(false);setF(blank);
  };
  const del=(id)=>{if(window.confirm("¿Eliminar?"))upd(p=>({...p,materials:p.materials.filter(x=>x.id!==id)}));};
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between"}}><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700}}>Materiales ({db.materials.length})</div><Btn onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM(true);}} clr={C.lime}>+ Subir</Btn></div>
      {m&&(
        <Modal title="Subir Material" onClose={()=>setM(false)} w={500}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <Inp label="Título" value={f.title} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,title:v}))} req/>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Sel label="Tipo" value={f.type} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,type:v}))} opts={Object.keys(TI).map(k=>({v:k,l:`${TI[k]} ${k.toUpperCase()}`}))}/>
              <Sel label="Materia" value={f.subjectId} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,subjectId:v}))} opts={db.subjects.map(s=>({v:String(s.id),l:`${s.icon} ${s.name}`}))}/>
            </div>
            <Sel label="Lección (opcional)" value={String(f.lessonId)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,lessonId:v}))} opts={[{v:"",l:"— Sin lección —"},...db.lessons.filter(l=>l.subjectId===parseInt(f.subjectId)).map(l=>({v:String(l.id),l:l.title}))]}/>
            <Inp label="URL (Google Drive u otro)" value={f.url} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,url:v}))} placeholder="https://drive.google.com/..." hint="Drive → Compartir → 'Cualquiera con el enlace'"/>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>
              <Inp label="Tamaño" value={String(f.size)} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,size:parseFloat(v)||0}))} type="number"/>
              <Sel label="Unidad" value={f.sizeUnit} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,sizeUnit:v}))} opts={["KB","MB","GB"].map(u=>({v:u,l:u}))}/>
            </div>
            <Toggle on={f.public} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,public:v}))} label="Visible para todos los estudiantes"/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}><Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(false)}>Cancelar</Btn><Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>📤 Subir</Btn></div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(auto-fill,minmax(200px,1fr))",gap:12}}>
        {db.materials.map(mat=>{
          const sub=db.subjects.find(s=>s.id===mat.subjectId);const tc=TC[mat.type]||C.p;
          return(
            <Card key={mat.id}sx={{padding:16}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,marginBottom:10}}>
                <div style={{width:38,height:38,borderRadius:10,background:`${tc}12`,border:`1px solid ${tc}22`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:18,flexShrink:0}}>{TI[mat.type]||"📎"}</div>
                <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}><div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{mat.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{mat.size}{mat.sizeUnit} · {mat.date}</div></div>
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:9}}>
                <Bdg clr={tc} sm>{mat.type.toUpperCase()}</Bdg>
                {sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon}</Bdg>}
                <Bdg clr={mat.public?C.lime:C.tf} sm>{mat.public?"Público":"Privado"}</Bdg>
              </div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:9}}>⬇️ {mat.downloads} descargas</div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}>
                {mat.url&&<a href={mat.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{flex:1,display:"block",textAlign:"center",padding:"6px",borderRadius:7,background:`${C.teal}12`,border:`1px solid ${C.teal}28`,color:C.teal,fontSize:10,fontWeight:700}}>🔗 Ver</a>}
                <Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(mat.id)}>🗑️</Btn>
              </div>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN ANNOUNCEMENTS ═══════════════════════════════════════
function AdminAnnouncements({db,upd}){
  const C=useC();
  const blank={title:"",body:"",pinned:false,author:"Fundación Study",audience:"all"};
  const[m,setM]=useState(null);const[f,setF]=useState(blank);
  const save=()=>{
    if(!f.title)return;
    upd(p=>{if(m==="new"){const id=Math.max(0,...p.announcements.map(a=>a.id))+1;return{...p,announcements:[{...f,id,date:new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]},...p.announcements]};}
    return{...p,announcements:p.announcements.map(a=>a.id===m.id?{...f,id:a.id,date:a.date}:a)};});setM(null);
  };
  const del=(id)=>{if(window.confirm("¿Eliminar?"))upd(p=>({...p,announcements:p.announcements.filter(a=>a.id!==id)}));};
  const pin=(id)=>upd(p=>({...p,announcements:p.announcements.map(a=>a.id===id?{...a,pinned:!a.pinned}:a)}));
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between"}}><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:16,fontWeight:700}}>Anuncios ({db.announcements.length})</div><Btn onClick={()=>{setF(blank);setM("new");}} clr={C.lime}>+ Nuevo</Btn></div>
      {m&&(
        <Modal title={m==="new"?"Nuevo Anuncio":"Editar Anuncio"} onClose={()=>setM(null)} w={540}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
            <Inp label="Título" value={f.title} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,title:v}))} req/>
            <Inp label="Mensaje" value={f.body} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,body:v}))} multi rows={4}/>
            <Sel label="Audiencia" value={f.audience} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,audience:v}))} opts={[{v:"all",l:"Todos"},{v:"student",l:"Solo estudiantes"},{v:"professor",l:"Solo profesores"}]}/>
            <Toggle on={f.pinned} onChange={v=>setF(x=>({...x,pinned:v}))} label="📌 Fijar anuncio (aparece destacado)"/>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,justifyContent:"flex-end"}}><Btn outline onClick={()=>setM(null)}>Cancelar</Btn><Btn onClick={save} clr={C.lime}>{m==="new"?"Publicar":"Guardar"}</Btn></div>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
      <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:9}}>
        {db.announcements.map(a=>(
          <Card key={a.id}sx={{padding:18,border:`1px solid ${a.pinned?C.p+"38":C.b}`}}>
            <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",gap:12,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:160}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:5,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>{a.pinned&&<span>📌</span>}<span style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700}}>{a.title}</span>{a.pinned&&<Bdg clr={C.p}>Fijado</Bdg>}<Bdg clr={C.tf} sm>{a.audience}</Bdg></div>
                <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.6,marginBottom:5}}>{a.body}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{a.author} · {a.date}</div>
              </div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,height:"fit-content",flexShrink:0}}>
                <Btn xs outline clr={a.pinned?C.gold:C.tf} onClick={()=>pin(a.id)}>📌</Btn>
                <Btn xs outline onClick={()=>{setF({...a});setM(a);}}>✏️</Btn>
                <Btn xs outline clr={C.red} onClick={()=>del(a.id)}>🗑️</Btn>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

          </div>

            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>📊 Videos con Mejor Rendimiento</div>
            {mockVideos.sort((a,b)=>b.likes-a.likes).map((v,i)=>(
              <div key={v.id} style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,padding:"8px 0",borderBottom:i<mockVideos.length-1?`1px solid ${C.b}`:"none"}}>
                <span style={{color:C.tf,fontSize:11,width:18,textAlign:"center"}}>{i+1}</span>
                <span style={{fontSize:18}}>{v.thumb}</span>
                <div style={{flex:1}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600}}>{v.title}</div>
                  <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:10}}>{v.views} vistas · {v.dur}</div>
                </div>
                <span style={{color:C.lime,fontWeight:700,fontSize:11}}>👍 {v.likes}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
      {vtab==="connect"&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:24}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:800,marginBottom:6}}>🔗 Conectar Canal de YouTube</div>
          <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:12,marginBottom:20}}>Integra tu canal con la API de YouTube Data v3 para gestionar videos y transmisiones en vivo.</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:10}}>
            {[["🔑 API Key",YT_API_KEY,"text"],["📺 Channel ID",YT_CHANNEL,"text"]].map(([l,v,t])=>(
              <div key={l}>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,marginBottom:4}}>{l}</div>
                <input defaultValue={v} type={t} style={{width:"100%",padding:"10px 14px",background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:10,color:C.t,fontSize:12,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",boxSizing:"border-box"}}/>
              </div>
            ))}
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,marginTop:4}}>
              <Btn clr={C.red}>Conectar Canal</Btn>
              <Btn clr={C.teal} outlined>Verificar API</Btn>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ DEV SDK VIEW ══════════════════════════════════════════════
function DevSDK({db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[stab,setStab]=useState("quickstart");
  const[consoleInput,setConsoleInput]=useState('{"action":"getUser","id":1}');
  const[consoleOut,setConsoleOut]=useState(null);
  const[loading,setLoading]=useState(false);

  const runConsole=async()=>{
    setLoading(true);setConsoleOut(null);
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,900));
    try{
      const parsed=JSON.parse(consoleInput);
      const mock={getUser:{id:1,name:"Ana García",role:"student",plan:"h_pro",xp:3420},
        getLessons:{lessons:[{id:1,title:"Present Perfect",level:"B1"},{id:2,title:"Conditional",level:"B2"}],total:12},
        getStats:{users:4,lessons:12,sessions:8,revenue:1289700}};
      setConsoleOut(JSON.stringify(mock[parsed.action]||{error:"Method not found",available:Object.keys(mock)},null,2));
    }catch{setConsoleOut('{"error":"JSON inválido"}');}
    setLoading(false);
  };

  const examples={
    js:`// Speak Fluently SDK — JavaScript
// npm install @speakfluently/sdk
const SpeakFluently = require('@speakfluently/sdk');

const sf = new SpeakFluently({
  apiKey: '${API_BASE}',
  version: 'v1'
});

  userId: 'user_123',
  period: '30d'
});

// Iniciar sesión AI Tutor

  studentId: 'user_123',
  language: 'en',
  level: 'B2',
  mode: 'conversation'
});

console.log(session.id, session.token);`,
    python:`# Speak Fluently SDK — Python
# pip install speakfluently
# import speakfluently as sf

# Instalacion: pip install speakfluently-sdk

    api_key="your_key_here",
    base_url="${API_BASE}"
)

    level="B1",
    subject="grammar",
    active=True
)

# Procesar pago Wompi

    amount_cents=8990000,
    currency="COP",
    customer_email="student@example.com",
    reference=f"SF-{uuid4()}"
)
print(payment.checkout_url)`,
    curl:`# Speak Fluently REST API — cURL

# Autenticación
curl -X POST ${API_BASE}/auth/login \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -d '{"email":"student@sf.co","password":"pass"}'

# Obtener perfil de usuario
curl ${API_BASE}/users/me \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer {JWT_TOKEN}"

# Crear sesión AI Tutor
curl -X POST ${API_BASE}/ai/sessions \\
  -H "Authorization: Bearer {JWT_TOKEN}" \\
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \\
  -d '{"level":"B2","mode":"conversation","lang":"en"}'`,
    php:`<?php
// Speak Fluently SDK — PHP
require 'vendor/autoload.php';

use SpeakFluently\\Client;

$sf = new Client([
    'api_key' => 'your_key_here',
    'base_url' => '${API_BASE}'
]);

// Obtener estadísticas del profesor

    'professorId' => 'prof_456',
    'period' => 'monthly'
]);

// Emitir certificado

    'studentId' => 'user_123',
    'courseId' => 'eng_b2',
    'grade' => 92.5
]);

echo $cert->downloadUrl;`
  };

  const apiEndpoints=[
    {method:"POST",path:"/auth/login",desc:"Autenticar usuario, retorna JWT"},
    {method:"GET",path:"/users/me",desc:"Perfil del usuario autenticado"},
    {method:"GET",path:"/lessons",desc:"Listar lecciones con filtros"},
    {method:"POST",path:"/ai/sessions",desc:"Crear sesión AI Tutor"},
    {method:"GET",path:"/progress/:userId",desc:"Progreso completo del estudiante"},
    {method:"POST",path:"/payments/init",desc:"Iniciar transacción Wompi"},
    {method:"GET",path:"/certificates/:id",desc:"Descargar certificado PDF"},
    {method:"POST",path:"/youtube/stream",desc:"Iniciar transmisión en vivo"},
  ];

  const MC={GET:C.lime,POST:C.teal,PUT:C.gold,DELETE:C.red};

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,paddingBottom:2}}>
        {[["quickstart","🚀 Inicio Rápido"],["code","💻 Ejemplos"],["console","⚡ Consola API"],["reference","📋 Referencia"]].map(([id,l])=>(
          <button key={id} onClick={()=>setStab(id)} style={{padding:"8px 14px",border:"none",background:"transparent",borderBottom:`2px solid ${stab===id?C.p:"transparent"}`,color:stab===id?C.p:C.tm,fontWeight:700,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"all .15s"}}>{l}</button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {stab==="quickstart"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
          <Card sx={{padding:20,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p}10,${C.teal}08)`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}22`}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:800,marginBottom:6}}>🚀 Speak Fluently Developer Platform</div>
            <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:12,lineHeight:1.7}}>Integra capacidades de aprendizaje de idiomas en tus aplicaciones. API RESTful con autenticación JWT, webhooks en tiempo real, SDK para JS/Python/PHP y más.</div>
          </Card>
          {[["1","Instalar SDK","npm install @speakfluently/sdk","bash"],["2","Autenticar","Obtén tu API key en la sección de Credenciales del Dev Dashboard","info"],["3","Primera llamada",`const sf = new SpeakFluently({ apiKey: 'your_key' });\nawait sf.lessons.list({ level: 'B1' });`,"js"],["4","Explorar","Usa la Consola API para probar endpoints en tiempo real","info"]].map(([n,title,content,type])=>(

                {type==="info"?<div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:12}}>{content}</div>:<pre style={{background:"#020208",border:`1px solid ${C.p}22`,borderRadius:10,padding:"10px 14px",fontSize:10,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:C.lime,margin:0,overflowX:"auto"}}>{content}</pre>}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {stab==="code"&&(

            </div>
          ))}

      )}

      {stab==="console"&&(

          <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:10}}>⚡ Consola API Interactiva</div>

          ))}

// ══════════ DEV LOGS VIEW ═════════════════════════════════════════════
function DevLogsView(){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[filter,setFilter]=useState("ALL");
  const[search,setSearch]=useState("");
  const[tick,setTick]=useState(0);
  useEffect(()=>{const t=setInterval(()=>setTick(x=>x+1),4000);return()=>clearInterval(t);},[]);
  const allLogs=[
    {t:"14:32:01.421",l:"INFO",s:"AUTH",m:"Login exitoso — fundacionstudy@gmail.com","id":1},
    {t:"14:31:58.002",l:"INFO",s:"AI",m:"Claude API call — tokens_used=312 model=claude-sonnet-4","id":2},
    {t:"14:31:44.810",l:"INFO",s:"LESSON",m:"Lección completada — user=2 lesson_id=3 xp=+70","id":3},
    {t:"14:31:22.344",l:"WARN",s:"DB",m:"Consulta lenta 436ms — table=progress idx=missing","id":4},
    {t:"14:30:55.188",l:"INFO",s:"WOMPI",m:"Webhook recibido — TXN-001 APPROVED $89,900 COP","id":5},
    {t:"14:30:12.900",l:"ERROR",s:"WEBHOOK",m:"Timeout en reintento — TXN-003 attempt=3 retry_in=30s","id":6},
    {t:"14:29:44.200",l:"INFO",s:"DNS",m:"Subdominio resuelto — ana.speakfluently.co → 45.12.34.56","id":7},
    {t:"14:29:00.001",l:"INFO",s:"PWA",m:"Service Worker actualizado — v7.0.0 cache_cleared=true","id":8},
    {t:"14:28:30.500",l:"WARN",s:"YT",m:"YouTube API quota 80% utilizado — 8000/10000 units","id":9},
    {t:"14:27:10.033",l:"INFO",s:"CERT",m:"Certificado emitido — user=2 course=english_b2 grade=94.5","id":10},
    {t:"14:26:44.710",l:"ERROR",s:"EMAIL",m:"Fallo envío SendGrid — code=403 invalid_api_key","id":11},
    {t:"14:25:20.112",l:"INFO",s:"HOSTING",m:"Backup completado — constructoraandina.speakfluently.co 2.3GB","id":12},
  ];
  const LC={INFO:C.lime,WARN:C.gold,ERROR:C.red};
  const filtered=allLogs.filter(l=>(filter==="ALL"||l.l===filter)&&(!search||l.m.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())||l.s.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())));
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <StatCard label="Total" val={allLogs.length} icon="📋" clr={C.p}/>
        <StatCard label="Errores" val={allLogs.filter(l=>l.l==="ERROR").length} icon="🔴" clr={C.red}/>
        <StatCard label="Warnings" val={allLogs.filter(l=>l.l==="WARN").length} icon="🟡" clr={C.gold}/>
        <StatCard label="Info" val={allLogs.filter(l=>l.l==="INFO").length} icon="🟢" clr={C.lime}/>
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap",alignItems:"center"}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:4}}>
          {["ALL","INFO","WARN","ERROR"].map(f=>(
            <button key={f} onClick={()=>setFilter(f)} style={{padding:"6px 12px",borderRadius:8,border:`1px solid ${filter===f?LC[f]||C.p:C.b}`,background:filter===f?`${LC[f]||C.p}18`:"transparent",color:filter===f?LC[f]||C.p:C.tm,fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{f}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <input value={search} onChange={e=>setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar logs..." style={{flex:1,padding:"7px 12px",background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:8,color:C.t,fontSize:11,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}/>
        <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.lime,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:5}}><span className="pulse"style={{width:6,height:6,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.lime,display:"inline-block"}}/>LIVE · {tick} ticks</div>
      </div>
      <div style={{background:"#010108",border:`1px solid ${C.p}18`,borderRadius:14,overflow:"hidden"}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,padding:"8px 14px",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.p}12`,background:`${C.p}08`}}>
          {["TIMESTAMP","LEVEL","SERVICE","MENSAJE"].map(h=>(
            <div key={h} style={{fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,flex:h==="MENSAJE"?3:h==="TIMESTAMP"?1.2:0.7}}>{h}</div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div style={{height:400,overflowY:"auto"}} className="noscroll">
          {filtered.map(log=>(
            <div key={log.id} style={{display:"flex",gap:8,padding:"8px 14px",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.p}08`,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:10,alignItems:"flex-start"}}
              onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background=`${C.p}05`}
              onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.background="transparent"}>
              <div style={{color:"#2a2a55",flex:1.2,minWidth:0}}>{log.t}</div>
              <div style={{color:LC[log.l],fontWeight:700,flex:.7,minWidth:0}}>{log.l}</div>
              <div style={{color:`${C.p}cc`,flex:.7,minWidth:0}}>{log.s}</div>
              <div style={{color:"#7070a0",flex:3,minWidth:0,lineHeight:1.5}}>{log.m}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ DEV WOMPI SANDBOX ═════════════════════════════════════════
function DevWompiSandbox(){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[wtab,setWtab]=useState("widget");
  const[amt,setAmt]=useState("89900");
  const[ref,setRef]=useState(`SF-test-${Date.now()}`);
  const[sig,setSig]=useState("");
  const[sigLoading,setSigLoading]=useState(false);
  const[webhookLog,setWebhookLog]=useState([]);

  const genSig=async()=>{
    setSigLoading(true);
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,600));
    const raw=`${ref}${parseInt(amt)*100}COP${WOMPI_SK}`;
    let hash=0;for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){const c=raw.charCodeAt(i);hash=((hash<<5)-hash)+c;hash|=0;}
    const fakeHash=Math.abs(hash).toString(16).padStart(64,"a");
    setSig(fakeHash);setSigLoading(false);
  };

  const fireWebhook=async(status)=>{
    const ev={ts:new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),ref,status,amount:`$${parseInt(amt).toLocaleString("es-CO")} COP`,id:`WH-${Date.now()}`};
    setWebhookLog(p=>[ev,...p].slice(0,8));
  };

  const testCards=[
    {num:"4242 4242 4242 4242",red:"Cualquiera",cvv:"Cualquiera",res:"✅ APROBADA"},
    {num:"4111 1111 1111 1111",red:"Cualquiera",cvv:"Cualquiera",res:"❌ RECHAZADA"},
    {num:"5555 5555 5555 4444",red:"12/26",cvv:"123",res:"✅ APROBADA"},
    {num:"4000 0000 0000 0002",red:"Cualquiera",cvv:"Cualquiera",res:"⏳ PENDIENTE"},
  ];

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.gold}12,${C.p}08)`,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}22`,borderRadius:14,padding:14,fontSize:11,color:C.gold}}>
        ⚠️ Modo Sandbox activo. Las transacciones son de prueba y no generan cobros reales. PK: <code style={{fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace"}}>{WOMPI_PK.slice(0,30)}...</code>
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,paddingBottom:2}}>
        {[["widget","💳 Widget"],["sig","🔏 Firma SHA256"],["webhooks","🪝 Webhooks"],["cards","🃏 Tarjetas Test"]].map(([id,l])=>(
          <button key={id} onClick={()=>setWtab(id)} style={{padding:"8px 14px",border:"none",background:"transparent",borderBottom:`2px solid ${wtab===id?C.gold:"transparent"}`,color:wtab===id?C.gold:C.tm,fontWeight:700,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"all .15s"}}>{l}</button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {wtab==="widget"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
          <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:12}}>💳 Checkout Wompi</div>
            <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:10,marginBottom:14}}>
              <div>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:4}}>Monto (COP)</div>
                <input value={amt} onChange={e=>setAmt(e.target.value)} style={{width:"100%",padding:"9px 12px",background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,color:C.t,fontSize:13,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",boxSizing:"border-box"}}/>
              </div>
              <div>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:4}}>Referencia</div>
                <input value={ref} onChange={e=>setRef(e.target.value)} style={{width:"100%",padding:"9px 12px",background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,color:C.t,fontSize:11,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",boxSizing:"border-box"}}/>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style={{background:"#020208",border:`1px solid ${C.gold}22`,borderRadius:12,padding:16,marginBottom:14}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:8,fontWeight:700}}>PREVIEW WIDGET</div>
              <div style={{textAlign:"center",padding:"20px 0"}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:24,marginBottom:8}}>💳</div>
                <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:14}}>Pagar ${parseInt(amt||0).toLocaleString("es-CO")} COP</div>
                <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:10,marginTop:4}}>Ref: {ref}</div>
                <button style={{marginTop:12,padding:"10px 28px",borderRadius:10,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.gold},#ffaa00)`,border:"none",color:"#000",fontWeight:800,fontSize:12,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>Pagar con Wompi</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Btn clr={C.gold} onClick={()=>alert("En producción, esto abre el checkout real de Wompi")}>Probar Widget</Btn>
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}

      {wtab==="sig"&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:20}}>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:6}}>🔏 Generador de Firma de Integridad</div>
          <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:11,marginBottom:14}}>Genera el hash SHA256 requerido por Wompi para validar transacciones.</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8}}>
            {[["Referencia",ref,setRef,"text"],["Monto (COP)",amt,setAmt,"number"]].map(([l,v,fn,t])=>(
              <div key={l}><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:3}}>{l}</div>
              <input value={v} onChange={e=>fn(e.target.value)} type={t} style={{width:"100%",padding:"9px 12px",background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,color:C.t,fontSize:12,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",boxSizing:"border-box"}}/></div>
            ))}
            <div><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:3}}>String a firmar</div>
            <div style={{background:"#010108",borderRadius:8,padding:"9px 12px",fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:10,color:"#6060a0",border:`1px solid ${C.p}12`}}>{ref}{parseInt(amt||0)*100}COP{WOMPI_SK.slice(0,15)}...</div></div>
            <Btn clr={C.gold} onClick={genSig}>{sigLoading?"⏳ Generando...":"🔏 Generar Firma"}</Btn>
            {sig&&<div><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginBottom:3}}>Firma SHA256 (simulada)</div>
            <div style={{background:"#010108",borderRadius:8,padding:"9px 12px",fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:10,color:C.lime,border:`1px solid ${C.lime}18`,wordBreak:"break-all"}}>{sig}</div></div>}
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {wtab==="webhooks"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
          <Card sx={{padding:18}}>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:10}}>🪝 Simulador de Webhooks</div>
            <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              {[["APPROVED",C.lime],["DECLINED",C.red],["PENDING",C.gold],["VOIDED",C.tf]].map(([st,col])=>(
                <button key={st} onClick={()=>fireWebhook(st)} style={{padding:"9px 16px",borderRadius:9,background:`${col}15`,border:`1px solid ${col}28`,color:col,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>{st}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </Card>
          <div style={{background:"#010108",border:`1px solid ${C.p}18`,borderRadius:12,padding:14,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",minHeight:160}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:8,fontWeight:700}}>WEBHOOK LOG</div>
            {webhookLog.length===0&&<div style={{color:"#2a2a50",fontSize:11}}>// Dispara un evento para ver logs aquí...</div>}
            {webhookLog.map((ev,i)=>(
              <div key={i} style={{fontSize:10,marginBottom:6,lineHeight:1.8}}>
                <span style={{color:"#252550"}}>[{ev.ts}]</span> <span style={{color:{APPROVED:C.lime,DECLINED:C.red,PENDING:C.gold,VOIDED:C.tf}[ev.status]||C.p,fontWeight:700}}>{ev.status}</span> <span style={{color:"#6060a0"}}>{ev.ref}</span> <span style={{color:C.teal}}>{ev.amount}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {wtab==="cards"&&(
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:10}}>
          <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:11,marginBottom:4}}>Tarjetas de prueba para el sandbox de Wompi. Usa cualquier fecha futura y CVV válido.</div>
          {testCards.map((c,i)=>(
            <div key={i} style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:12,padding:"12px 16px",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:14,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
              <span style={{fontSize:22}}>💳</span>
              <div style={{flex:1}}>
                <code style={{fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",fontSize:12,color:C.teal,display:"block"}}>{c.num}</code>
                <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:10,marginTop:3}}>Exp: {c.red} · CVV: {c.cvv}</div>
              </div>
              <span style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700}}>{c.res}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ COURSES VIEW ══════════════════════════════════════════════
function CoursesView({user,db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[fSub,setFSub]=useState("all");const[fLvl,setFLvl]=useState("all");
  const prog=db.progress[user.id]||{};
  const done=Object.values(prog).flat();

  const complete=(l)=>{
    upd(p=>{
      const up={...p.progress};const uid=user.id;
      if(!up[uid])up[uid]={};
      if(!up[uid][l.levelCode])up[uid][l.levelCode]=[];
      if(!up[uid][l.levelCode].includes(l.id)){
        up[uid][l.levelCode]=[...up[uid][l.levelCode],l.id];
        return{...p,progress:up,users:p.users.map(u=>u.id===uid?{...u,xp:u.xp+l.xp,streak:u.streak+1}:u)};
      }return p;
    });
  };

  const lessons=db.lessons.filter(l=>l.active&&(fSub==="all"||l.subjectId===parseInt(fSub))&&(fLvl==="all"||l.levelCode===fLvl));
  const TC={vocabulary:C.teal,grammar:C.p3,conversation:C.lime,exam:C.red,listening:C.gold,writing:C.amber};
  const TI={vocabulary:"📖",grammar:"✏️",conversation:"💬",exam:"📝",listening:"🎧",writing:"✍️"};

  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7,flexWrap:"wrap",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center"}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          <select value={fSub} onChange={e=>setFSub(e.target.value)} style={{background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,padding:"8px 12px",color:C.t,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer"}}>
            <option value="all">Todas las materias</option>
            {db.subjects.map(s=><option key={s.id} value={String(s.id)}>{s.icon} {s.name}</option>)}
          </select>
          <select value={fLvl} onChange={e=>setFLvl(e.target.value)} style={{background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,padding:"8px 12px",color:C.t,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer"}}>
            <option value="all">Todos los niveles</option>
            {["A1","A2","B1","B2","C1","C2"].map(l=><option key={l} value={l}>{l}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>
        <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>{done.length}/{db.lessons.filter(l=>l.active).length} completadas · <span style={{color:C.gold,fontWeight:700}}>⭐{user.xp}</span></div>
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(3,1fr)",gap:12}}>
        {lessons.map(l=>{
          const isDone=done.includes(l.id);
          const sub=db.subjects.find(s=>s.id===l.subjectId);
          const tc=TC[l.type]||C.p;
          return(
            <Card key={l.id}sx={{padding:18,cursor:"pointer",border:`1px solid ${isDone?C.lime+"45":C.b}`,transition:"all .2s"}}
              onClick={()=>!isDone&&complete(l)}
              onMouseEnter={e=>{if(!isDone){e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=tc;e.currentTarget.style.transform="translateY(-2px)";}}}
              onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=isDone?C.lime+"45":C.b;e.currentTarget.style.transform="translateY(0)";}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:10}}>
                <div style={{width:38,height:38,borderRadius:10,background:isDone?`${C.lime}16`:`${tc}12`,border:`1px solid ${isDone?C.lime+"40":tc+"25"}`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:17}}>{isDone?"✅":TI[l.type]||"📄"}</div>
                <Bdg clr={isDone?C.lime:tc} sm>{isDone?"done":l.type}</Bdg>
              </div>
              <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:5,lineHeight:1.3}}>{l.title}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.5,marginBottom:10,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",display:"-webkit-box",WebkitLineClamp:2,WebkitBoxOrient:"vertical"}}>{l.content}</div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center"}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5}}>{sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon}</Bdg>}<Bdg clr={C.p3} sm>{l.levelCode}</Bdg></div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}><span>⏱{l.duration}</span><span style={{color:C.gold,fontWeight:700}}>+{l.xp}XP</span></div>
              </div>
              {!isDone&&<div style={{marginTop:12,padding:"8px",borderRadius:8,background:`${tc}10`,border:`1px solid ${tc}22`,fontSize:11,color:tc,textAlign:"center",fontWeight:700}}>Marcar como completada →</div>}
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ LIVE SESSION CARD ════════════════════════════════════════
function LiveSessionCard({s,db,user,enroll}){
    const C2=useC();
    const sub=db.subjects.find(x=>x.id===s.subjectId);
    const en=s.enrolled.includes(user.id);
    const col={live:C2.red,upcoming:C2.teal,completed:C2.lime,cancelled:C2.tf}[s.status]||C2.p;
    const liveCol=s.status==="live"?C2.red:C2.teal;
    return(
      <Card sx={{padding:18,border:`1px solid ${s.status==="live"?C2.red+"55":C2.b}`,boxShadow:s.status==="live"?`0 0 28px ${C2.red}18`:""}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:10,gap:8}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:9,flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
            <div style={{width:44,height:44,borderRadius:12,background:`${liveCol}16`,border:`1px solid ${liveCol}28`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:20,flexShrink:0}}>📹</div>
            <div style={{minWidth:0}}><div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:2,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{s.title}</div><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C2.tm}}>{s.professorName} · {s.date} {s.time}</div></div>
          </div>
          <Bdg clr={col} dot={s.status==="live"}>{s.status==="live"?"VIVO":s.status}</Bdg>
        </div>
        {s.description&&<div style={{fontSize:12,color:C2.tm,lineHeight:1.6,marginBottom:12}}>{s.description}</div>}
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,flexWrap:"wrap",marginBottom:12}}>
          {sub&&<Bdg clr={sub.color} sm>{sub.icon} {sub.name}</Bdg>}
          <Bdg clr={C2.p3} sm>{s.levelCode}</Bdg>
          <span style={{fontSize:10,color:C2.tf}}>⏱{s.duration}min · 👥{s.enrolled.length}/{s.maxStudents}</span>
          {s.price>0&&<Bdg clr={C2.gold} sm>${s.price.toLocaleString("es-CO")}</Bdg>}
        </div>
        {s.status!=="completed"
          ?en
            ?<a href={s.link||"#"} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{display:"block",textAlign:"center",padding:"11px",borderRadius:11,background:s.status==="live"?C2.red:C2.p,color:"#fff",fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,boxShadow:`0 4px 18px ${s.status==="live"?C2.red:C2.p}50`}}>{s.status==="live"?"📹 Entrar ahora":"📅 Ya inscrito"}</a>
            :<Btn full onClick={()=>enroll(s.id)} clr={C2.p}>✅ Inscribirme{s.price>0?` — $${s.price.toLocaleString("es-CO")}/sesión`:""}</Btn>
          :s.recording
            ?<a href={s.recording} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" style={{display:"block",textAlign:"center",padding:"10px",borderRadius:10,background:C2.g3,color:C2.tm,fontWeight:600,fontSize:12,border:`1px solid ${C2.b}`}}>🎬 Ver grabación</a>
            :<div style={{textAlign:"center",color:C2.tf,fontSize:11,padding:10}}>Grabación no disponible</div>
        }
      </Card>
    );
}

// ══════════ LIVE VIEW ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function LiveView({user,db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const enroll=(sid)=>upd(p=>({...p,sessions:p.sessions.map(s=>s.id===sid&&!s.enrolled.includes(user.id)?{...s,enrolled:[...s.enrolled,user.id]}:s)}));
  // LiveSessionCard is defined externally
  const live=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.status==="live");
  const upcoming=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.status==="upcoming");
  const done=db.sessions.filter(s=>s.status==="completed");
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      {live.length>0&&<div><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.red,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,marginBottom:10,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8}}><span className="pulse"style={{width:7,height:7,borderRadius:"50%",background:C.red,display:"inline-block"}}/>En Vivo Ahora</div><div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>{live.map(s=><LiveSessionCard key={s.id} s={s} db={db} user={user} enroll={enroll}/>)}</div></div>}
      {upcoming.length>0&&<div><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.teal,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,marginBottom:10}}>📅 Próximas Sesiones</div><div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>{upcoming.map(s=><LiveSessionCard key={s.id} s={s} db={db} user={user} enroll={enroll}/>)}</div></div>}
      {done.length>0&&<div><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,marginBottom:10}}>✅ Sesiones Pasadas</div><div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:12}}>{done.map(s=><LiveSessionCard key={s.id} s={s} db={db} user={user} enroll={enroll}/>)}</div></div>}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ PRONUNCIATION VIEW ════════════════════════════════════════
function PronView(){
  const C=useC();
  const[word,setWord]=useState("");const[res,setRes]=useState(null);const[load,setLoad]=useState(false);
  const analyze=async()=>{
    if(!word.trim())return;setLoad(true);setRes(null);
    const r=await callAI([{role:"user",content:`Analyze the pronunciation of "${word}" for a Colombian Spanish speaker learning English. Respond ONLY with valid JSON: {"ipa":"","syllables":"","stress":"","tips":["",""],"common_mistakes":[""],"similar_spanish":"","audio_tip":""}`}],"",500);
    try{setRes(JSON.parse(r.replace(/```json|```/g,"").trim()));}catch{setRes({ipa:"Error",syllables:word,stress:"",tips:["Intenta de nuevo"],common_mistakes:[],similar_spanish:"",audio_tip:""});}
    setLoad(false);
  };
  const HARD=["through","thought","world","squirrel","rural","sixth","clothes","months","strengths"];
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16,maxWidth:640}}>
      <Card sx={{padding:22,border:`1px solid ${C.red}28`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:11,alignItems:"center",marginBottom:18}}>
          <div className="float"style={{width:48,height:48,borderRadius:13,background:`${C.red}18`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:22}}>🎙️</div>
          <div><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:17,fontWeight:700}}>Analizador de Pronunciación IA</div><div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>Fonética, sílabas y tips para colombianos</div></div>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8}}>
          <input value={word} onChange={e=>setWord(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={e=>e.key==="Enter"&&analyze()} placeholder="Escribe una palabra en inglés..."
            style={{flex:1,background:C.g2,border:`1.5px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:10,padding:"10px 14px",color:C.t,fontSize:13,outline:"none",transition:"border-color .2s"}}
            onFocus={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=C.red} onBlur={e=>e.target.style.borderColor=C.b}/>
          <Btn onClick={analyze} disabled={!word.trim()||load} clr={C.red}>{load?<Spin/>:"🎙️ Analizar"}</Btn>
        </div>
        <div style={{marginTop:12}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:8,fontWeight:700,letterSpacing:1.5,textTransform:"uppercase"}}>Difíciles para colombianos:</div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
            {HARD.map(w=><button key={w} onClick={()=>setWord(w)} style={{padding:"4px 11px",borderRadius:8,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,background:C.g3,color:C.tm,fontSize:11,cursor:"pointer",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif",transition:"all .15s"}} onMouseEnter={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.red;e.currentTarget.style.color=C.t;}} onMouseLeave={e=>{e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.b;e.currentTarget.style.color=C.tm;}}>{w}</button>)}
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
      {res&&!load&&(
        <Card sx={{padding:22}} className="afu">
          <div style={{textAlign:"center",marginBottom:20,padding:20,background:`${C.red}08`,borderRadius:14,border:`1px solid ${C.red}20`}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:13,color:C.tf,marginBottom:6,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:2,fontWeight:700}}>IPA · Transcripción fonética</div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:40,fontWeight:900,color:C.red,marginBottom:4}}>/{res.ipa}/</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:16,color:C.tm,fontWeight:600,marginBottom:4}}>{res.syllables}</div>
            {res.stress&&<div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tf}}>Acento: {res.stress}</div>}
          </div>
          {res.similar_spanish&&<div style={{background:`${C.teal}0a`,borderRadius:11,padding:12,marginBottom:14,border:`1px solid ${C.teal}20`}}><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.teal,marginBottom:4}}>🇨🇴 Parecido en español</div><div style={{fontSize:13,color:C.tm}}>{res.similar_spanish}</div></div>}
          {res.audio_tip&&<div style={{background:`${C.gold}0a`,borderRadius:11,padding:12,marginBottom:14,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}20`}}><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.gold,marginBottom:4}}>🎵 Tip de audio</div><div style={{fontSize:13,color:C.tm}}>{res.audio_tip}</div></div>}
          {res.tips?.length>0&&<div style={{marginBottom:14}}><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:8}}>💡 Tips para colombianos</div>{res.tips.map((t,i)=><div key={i}style={{display:"flex",gap:8,fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginBottom:6,background:C.g3,borderRadius:8,padding:"8px 12px",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}><span style={{color:C.lime,flexShrink:0}}>✓</span>{t}</div>)}</div>}
          {res.common_mistakes?.length>0&&<div><div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5,marginBottom:8}}>⚠️ Errores comunes</div>{res.common_mistakes.map((e,i)=><div key={i}style={{display:"flex",gap:8,fontSize:12,color:C.tm,marginBottom:5}}><span style={{color:C.red,flexShrink:0}}>✗</span>{e}</div>)}</div>}
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ACHIEVEMENTS ══════════════════════════════════════════════
function AchievementsView({user}){
  const C=useC();
  const ALL=[{id:"first",ic:"🌱",n:"Primer Paso",d:"Completa tu primera lección",req:user.xp>=50,xp:50},{id:"streak7",ic:"🔥",n:"7 días seguidos",d:"Racha de 7 días",req:user.streak>=7,xp:100},{id:"xp500",ic:"⭐",n:"500 XP",d:"Alcanza 500 XP",req:user.xp>=500,xp:200},{id:"hosting",ic:"☁️",n:"En la Nube",d:"Activa tu hosting",req:user.plan!=="h_free",xp:150},{id:"api",ic:"🔑",n:"Dev Mode",d:"Genera tu API Key",req:!!user.apiKey,xp:200},{id:"streak30",ic:"⚡",n:"30 días",d:"Racha de 30 días",req:user.streak>=30,xp:500},{id:"xp1000",ic:"💎",n:"1000 XP",d:"Alcanza 1000 XP",req:user.xp>=1000,xp:300},{id:"c1",ic:"🏆",n:"Nivel C1",d:"Alcanza nivel C1",req:user.level==="C1"||user.level==="C2",xp:1000}];
  const earned=ALL.filter(a=>a.req);const pct=Math.round(earned.length/ALL.length*100);
  return(
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16}}>
      <Card sx={{padding:22,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.gold}18,${C.p}0a)`,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}28`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:12}}>
          <div><div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:20,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:3}}>🏆 Mis Logros</div><div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.tm}}>{earned.length}/{ALL.length} desbloqueados · {user.xp.toLocaleString()} XP total</div></div>
          <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:36,fontWeight:900,color:C.gold}}>{pct}%</div>
        </div>
        <div style={{marginTop:14,height:6,background:C.b,borderRadius:99}}><div style={{height:"100%",width:`${pct}%`,background:`linear-gradient(90deg,${C.gold},${C.lime})`,borderRadius:99,transition:"width 1.2s"}}/></div>
      </Card>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:"repeat(auto-fill,minmax(180px,1fr))",gap:12}}>
        {ALL.map(a=>(
          <Card key={a.id}sx={{padding:18,textAlign:"center",opacity:a.req?1:.45,border:`1px solid ${a.req?C.gold+"45":C.b}`,background:a.req?`${C.gold}06`:C.card,transition:"all .3s"}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:36,marginBottom:9,filter:a.req?"none":"grayscale(1)"}}>{a.ic}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:13,fontWeight:700,marginBottom:4,color:a.req?C.t:C.tf}}>{a.n}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm,marginBottom:10,lineHeight:1.4}}>{a.d}</div>
            <Bdg clr={a.req?C.gold:C.tf}>+{a.xp} XP</Bdg>
            {a.req&&<div style={{marginTop:7,fontSize:10,color:C.lime,fontWeight:700}}>✅ Desbloqueado</div>}
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ PWA INSTALL HOOK ══════════════════════════════════════════
function usePWAInstall(){
  const[prompt,setPrompt]=useState(null);
  const[installed,setInstalled]=useState(false);
  useEffect(()=>{
    const h=(e)=>{e.preventDefault();setPrompt(e);};
    window.addEventListener("beforeinstallprompt",h);
    window.addEventListener("appinstalled",()=>setInstalled(true));
    if(window.matchMedia?.("(display-mode: standalone)").matches)setInstalled(true);
    return()=>window.removeEventListener("beforeinstallprompt",h);
  },[]);
  const install=async()=>{if(!prompt)return;prompt.prompt();const{outcome}=await prompt.userChoice;if(outcome==="accepted")setInstalled(true);setPrompt(null);};
  return{canInstall:!!prompt,installed,install};
}
