// ══════════ QUIZ VIEW ════════════════════════════════════════════════
function QuizView({user,db,upd}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[phase,setPhase]=useState("select"); // select | quiz | result
  const[selectedLevel,setSelectedLevel]=useState(user.level||"A1");
  const[qIdx,setQIdx]=useState(0);
  const[answers,setAnswers]=useState({});
  const[chosen,setChosen]=useState(null);
  const[showExp,setShowExp]=useState(false);
  const[score,setScore]=useState(0);
  const[busy,setBusy]=useState(false);
  const[aiFeedback,setAiFeedback]=useState("");
  const[timer,setTimer]=useState(30);
  const timerRef=useRef(null);

  const questions=QUIZ_DATA[selectedLevel]||QUIZ_DATA.A1;
  const currentQ=questions[qIdx];
  const total=questions.length;
  const isLast=qIdx===total-1;

  useEffect(()=>{
    if(phase==="quiz"){
      setTimer(30);clearInterval(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current=setInterval(()=>{
        setTimer(t=>{
          if(t<=1){clearInterval(timerRef.current);handleSelect(-1);return 0;}
          return t-1;
        });
      },1000);
      return()=>clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    }
  },[qIdx,phase]);

  const handleSelect=(opt)=>{
    if(chosen!==null)return;
    clearInterval(timerRef.current);
    setChosen(opt);
    setShowExp(true);
    const correct=opt===currentQ.ans;
    if(correct)setScore(s=>s+1);
    setAnswers(a=>({...a,[currentQ.id]:opt}));
  };

  const handleNext=()=>{
    if(isLast){
      finishQuiz();
    } else {
      setQIdx(i=>i+1);setChosen(null);setShowExp(false);
    }
  };

  const finishQuiz=async()=>{
    setPhase("result");
    const pct=Math.round((score+(answers[currentQ.id]===currentQ.ans&&chosen===null?0:chosen===currentQ.ans?1:0))/total*100);
    const xpEarned=Math.round(pct*1.5);
    setBusy(true);
    const prompt=`El estudiante de nivel ${selectedLevel} completó un quiz de inglés con ${score} de ${total} respuestas correctas (${pct}%). Da retroalimentación en español: 2 frases de análisis y 1 consejo específico de mejora para este nivel. Sé conciso y motivador.`;
    const fb=await callAI([{role:"user",content:prompt}],"Eres un tutor de inglés experto. Responde siempre en español de Colombia.",300);
    setAiFeedback(fb);setBusy(false);
    if(upd)upd(p=>({...p,users:p.users.map(u=>u.id===user.id?{...u,xp:(u.xp||0)+xpEarned}:u)}));
  };

  const restart=()=>{setPhase("select");setQIdx(0);setAnswers({});setChosen(null);setShowExp(false);setScore(0);setAiFeedback("");};

  const RARITY_C={common:C.teal,rare:C.p,epic:C.gold,legendary:C.red};
  const levelColors={A1:C.teal,A2:C.lime,B1:C.gold,B2:C.amber,C1:C.p3,C2:C.red};

  if(phase==="select") return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?20:26,fontWeight:900}}>🎯 Quizzes de Inglés</div>
      <div style={{color:C.tm,fontSize:12}}>Pon a prueba tu inglés y recibe retroalimentación personalizada con IA</div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(4,1fr)",gap:12}}>
        {Object.keys(QUIZ_DATA).map(lv=>(
          <div key={lv} onClick={()=>setSelectedLevel(lv)} style={{padding:"20px 16px",borderRadius:16,border:`2px solid ${selectedLevel===lv?levelColors[lv]:C.b}`,background:selectedLevel===lv?`${levelColors[lv]}14`:C.g2,cursor:"pointer",textAlign:"center",transition:"all .2s"}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:28,marginBottom:6}}>{lv==="A1"?"🌱":lv==="A2"?"📗":lv==="B1"?"⭐":lv==="B2"?"🔥":"💎"}</div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontWeight:800,fontSize:16,color:levelColors[lv]}}>{lv}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginTop:3}}>{lv==="A1"?"Principiante":lv==="A2"?"Elemental":lv==="B1"?"Intermedio":"Inter. Alto"}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tm,marginTop:4}}>{(QUIZ_DATA[lv]||[]).length} preguntas</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          {[["⏱️","30 segundos por pregunta"],["🤖","Retroalimentación con IA al final"],["✨","Gana XP por tus respuestas correctas"],["📊","Tracking de progreso automático"]].map(([ic,t])=>(
            <div key={t} style={{display:"flex",gap:7,alignItems:"center",minWidth:160}}>
              <span style={{fontSize:16}}>{ic}</span>
              <span style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>{t}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      <Btn clr={C.p} onClick={()=>{setPhase("quiz");setQIdx(0);setScore(0);setAnswers({});}} full>
        Iniciar Quiz {selectedLevel} →
      </Btn>
    </div>
  );

  if(phase==="quiz") return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:16,maxWidth:640,margin:"0 auto"}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center"}}>
        <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,color:C.tm}}>Pregunta {qIdx+1} de {total}</div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700,color:timer<=10?C.red:C.lime}}>⏱ {timer}s</div>
          <div style={{background:levelColors[selectedLevel],color:"#000",fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,padding:"3px 10px",borderRadius:99}}>{selectedLevel}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{height:4,borderRadius:99,background:C.b,overflow:"hidden"}}>
        <div style={{height:"100%",borderRadius:99,background:C.p,width:`${((qIdx)/total)*100}%`,transition:"width .4s"}}/>
      </div>
      <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:18,padding:sm?"18px":"26px",border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`}}>
        <div style={{fontSize:sm?14:17,fontWeight:700,lineHeight:1.5,marginBottom:20,color:C.t}}>{currentQ.q}</div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:10}}>
          {currentQ.opts.map((opt,i)=>{
            let bg=C.g3,border=C.b,col=C.t;
            if(chosen!==null){
              if(i===currentQ.ans){bg=`${C.lime}15`;border=C.lime;col=C.lime;}
              else if(i===chosen&&i!==currentQ.ans){bg=`${C.red}15`;border=C.red;col=C.red;}
            } else if(chosen===null){bg=C.g3;border=C.b;}
            return (
              <div key={i} onClick={()=>handleSelect(i)} style={{padding:"14px 16px",borderRadius:12,border:`2px solid ${border}`,background:bg,color:col,cursor:chosen!==null?"default":"pointer",fontSize:13,fontWeight:500,display:"flex",gap:12,alignItems:"center",transition:"all .15s"}}>
                <div style={{width:24,height:24,borderRadius:"50%",border:`2px solid ${border}`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:11,fontWeight:800,flexShrink:0}}>
                  {chosen!==null&&i===currentQ.ans?"✓":chosen!==null&&i===chosen&&i!==currentQ.ans?"✗":String.fromCharCode(65+i)}
                </div>
                {opt}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        {showExp&&(
          <div style={{marginTop:16,padding:"12px 16px",borderRadius:12,background:`${C.p}10`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}28`,fontSize:12,color:C.p3,lineHeight:1.6}}>
            💡 {currentQ.exp}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      {chosen!==null&&(
        <Btn full clr={C.p} onClick={handleNext}>{isLast?"Ver Resultados →":"Siguiente →"}</Btn>
      )}
      <button onClick={restart} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.tf,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,textAlign:"center"}}>Abandonar quiz</button>
    </div>
  );

  const finalScore=Object.values(answers).filter((v,i)=>v===questions[i]?.ans).length;
  const pct=Math.round(finalScore/total*100);
  const xp=Math.round(pct*1.5);
  const grade=pct>=90?"A+":pct>=80?"A":pct>=70?"B":pct>=60?"C":"D";
  const gradeColor=pct>=70?C.lime:pct>=50?C.gold:C.red;

  return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18,maxWidth:640,margin:"0 auto"}}>
      <div style={{background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p}20,${C.teal}12)`,borderRadius:20,padding:sm?"24px":"36px",textAlign:"center",border:`1px solid ${C.p}30`}}>
        <div style={{fontSize:48,marginBottom:10}}>{pct>=80?"🏆":pct>=60?"🎯":"📖"}</div>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?22:30,fontWeight:900,marginBottom:4}}>
          {pct>=80?"¡Excelente!":pct>=60?"¡Buen trabajo!":"Sigue practicando"}
        </div>
        <div style={{fontSize:48,fontWeight:900,color:gradeColor,fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif"}}>{grade}</div>
        <div style={{fontSize:14,color:C.tm,marginTop:4}}>{finalScore} de {total} correctas · {pct}%</div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:16,justifyContent:"center",marginTop:16,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          {[["✨",`+${xp} XP ganados`],[`${finalScore}/${total}`,"Correctas"],[`${pct}%`,"Puntaje"]].map(([v,l])=>(
            <div key={l} style={{textAlign:"center"}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:18,fontWeight:800,color:C.p2}}>{v}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{l}</div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      {busy&&<div style={{textAlign:"center",color:C.tm,fontSize:12,padding:16}}>🤖 Analizando tus respuestas con IA...</div>}
      {aiFeedback&&(
        <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.p}20`}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.p,marginBottom:8}}>🤖 ANÁLISIS DE TU TUTOR IA</div>
          <div style={{fontSize:13,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.7}}>{aiFeedback}</div>
        </div>
      )}
      <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:6}}>
        {questions.map((q,i)=>{
          const ans=answers[q.id];const correct=ans===q.ans;
          return (
            <div key={q.id} style={{display:"flex",gap:10,padding:"10px 14px",borderRadius:10,background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${correct?C.lime:C.red}20`,alignItems:"flex-start"}}>
              <span style={{fontSize:16,flexShrink:0}}>{correct?"✅":"❌"}</span>
              <div style={{flex:1}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,color:C.t}}>{q.q}</div>
                {!correct&&<div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.lime,marginTop:2}}>Correcto: {q.opts[q.ans]}</div>}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10}}>
        <Btn full clr={C.p} onClick={restart}>Nuevo Quiz</Btn>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ CERTIFICATE VIEW ══════════════════════════════════════════
function CertificateView({user,db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const canvasRef=useRef(null);
  const[selLevel,setSelLevel]=useState(user.level||"A1");
  const[selLang,setSelLang]=useState("english");
  const[generated,setGenerated]=useState(false);
  const[busy,setBusy]=useState(false);
  const[shareMsg,setShareMsg]=useState("");

  const CERT_COLORS={A1:"#22d3ee",A2:"#aaff4d",B1:"#ffd04d",B2:"#ff9a3c",C1:"#c4b0ff",C2:"#ff4d7a"};
  const LEVEL_NAMES={A1:"Principiante",A2:"Elemental",B1:"Intermedio",B2:"Intermedio Alto",C1:"Avanzado",C2:"Maestría"};
  const availLevels=["A1","A2","B1","B2"].filter(lv=>{
    const lvOrder={"A1":1,"A2":2,"B1":3,"B2":4,"C1":5,"C2":6};
    return (lvOrder[lv]||0)<=(lvOrder[user.level]||2);
  });

  const generateCert=()=>{
    setBusy(true);
    setTimeout(()=>{
      const canvas=canvasRef.current;if(!canvas)return;
      const ctx=canvas.getContext("2d");
      const W=800,H=560;canvas.width=W;canvas.height=H;
      // Background gradient
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,W,H);
      g.addColorStop(0,"#02020d");g.addColorStop(0.5,"#04041a");g.addColorStop(1,"#02020d");
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
      // Border
      ctx.strokeStyle=CERT_COLORS[selLevel];ctx.lineWidth=3;
      ctx.strokeRect(20,20,W-40,H-40);
      ctx.strokeStyle=`${CERT_COLORS[selLevel]}40`;ctx.lineWidth=1;
      ctx.strokeRect(28,28,W-56,H-56);
      // Logo/Icon
      ctx.font="bold 48px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillStyle=CERT_COLORS[selLevel];
      ctx.fillText("✨",W/2,95);
      // Title
      ctx.font="bold 32px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="#f0ecff";
      ctx.fillText("CERTIFICADO DE DOMINIO DEL IDIOMA",W/2,140);
      ctx.font="16px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.55)";
      ctx.fillText("Speak Fluently Academy · Colombia",W/2,165);
      // Divider
      ctx.strokeStyle=CERT_COLORS[selLevel];ctx.lineWidth=1;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(100,182);ctx.lineTo(W-100,182);ctx.stroke();
      // Main text
      ctx.font="16px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.65)";
      ctx.fillText("Se certifica que",W/2,215);
      ctx.font="bold 34px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="#fff";
      ctx.fillText(user.name,W/2,260);
      ctx.font="14px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.6)";
      ctx.fillText("ha completado satisfactoriamente el programa de",W/2,292);
      // Level
      ctx.font="bold 28px Arial";ctx.fillStyle=CERT_COLORS[selLevel];
      ctx.fillText(`INGLES NIVEL ${selLevel} — CEFR`,W/2,332);
      ctx.font="13px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.5)";
      ctx.fillText(`${LEVEL_NAMES[selLevel]} · Common European Framework of Reference`,W/2,355);
      // Date
      const date=new Date().toLocaleDateString("es-CO",{year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric"});
      ctx.fillText(`Expedido el ${date}`,W/2,388);
      // Signatures
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(150,440);ctx.lineTo(330,440);ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.25)";ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(470,440);ctx.lineTo(650,440);ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.25)";ctx.stroke();
      ctx.font="bold 12px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.5)";
      ctx.fillText("DIRECTORA ACADÉMICA",240,460);
      ctx.fillText("COORD. CERTIFICACIONES",560,460);
      ctx.font="11px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.3)";
      ctx.fillText("Fundación Study",240,475);
      ctx.fillText("Speak Fluently Academy",560,475);
      // Certificate ID
      const certId=`SF-CERT-${selLevel}-${user.id}-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
      ctx.font="10px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(240,236,255,0.2)";
      ctx.fillText(`ID: ${certId}`,W/2,510);
      ctx.fillText("speakfluently.co · academia@speakfluently.co · NIT: Fundacion Study",W/2,525);
      setGenerated(true);setBusy(false);
    },800);
  };

  const downloadCert=()=>{
    const canvas=canvasRef.current;if(!canvas)return;
    const link=document.createElement("a");
    link.download=`Certificado_SpeakFluently_${selLevel}_${user.name.replace(/ /g,"_")}.png`;
    link.href=canvas.toDataURL("image/png");link.click();
  };

  const shareCert=()=>{
    setShareMsg(`¡Obtuve mi certificado CEFR ${selLevel} en Speak Fluently Academy! 🎓🇬🇧 #SpeakFluently #CEFR #${selLevel} #AprendizajeDeIdiomas`);
  };

  return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?20:26,fontWeight:900}}>📜 Certificados CEFR</div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"1fr 1fr",gap:16}}>
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:12}}>
          <Sel label="Nivel del certificado" value={selLevel} onChange={setSelLevel} opts={availLevels.map(lv=>({v:lv,l:`${lv} — ${LEVEL_NAMES[lv]}`}))}/>
          <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:12,padding:14,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,fontSize:12}}>
            <div style={{fontWeight:700,marginBottom:8,color:C.p}}>📋 Incluye</div>
            {["Nombre completo del estudiante","Nivel CEFR oficial (A1-C2)","Fecha de expedición","Firmas institucionales","ID único de verificación","Logo Speak Fluently Academy"].map(f=>(
              <div key={f} style={{display:"flex",gap:7,marginBottom:5,alignItems:"center"}}>
                <span style={{color:C.lime,fontSize:14}}>✓</span>
                <span style={{color:C.tm}}>{f}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div style={{background:`${C.gold}10`,borderRadius:12,padding:14,border:`1px solid ${C.gold}25`,fontSize:11,color:C.gold}}>
            ⚠️ Los certificados son válidos para portafolios personales y CVs. Para acreditación oficial DIAN/MEN, contacta fundacionstudy@gmail.com
          </div>
          <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
            <Btn clr={C.p} onClick={generateCert} disabled={busy} full>{busy?"⏳ Generando...":"🖨️ Generar Certificado"}</Btn>
            {generated&&<Btn outline onClick={downloadCert} full>⬇️ Descargar PNG</Btn>}
            {generated&&<Btn ghost onClick={shareCert} full>📲 Compartir</Btn>}
          </div>
          {shareMsg&&(
            <div style={{background:C.g3,borderRadius:10,padding:12,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,fontSize:11,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.6}}>
              <div style={{fontWeight:700,color:C.p,marginBottom:4}}>Copia este mensaje:</div>
              {shareMsg}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:10}}>
          <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:C.tf,textTransform:"uppercase",letterSpacing:1.5}}>Vista previa</div>
          <div style={{borderRadius:14,overflow:"hidden",border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,background:"#010108"}}>
            <canvas ref={canvasRef} style={{width:"100%",display:"block"}}/>
            {!generated&&(
              <div style={{padding:"40px 20px",textAlign:"center",color:C.tf,fontSize:12}}>
                Haz clic en Generar Certificado para ver la vista previa
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:800,marginBottom:10}}>Historial de certificados</div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8}}>
          {(db.payments||[]).filter(p=>p.userId===user.id&&p.type==="plan").slice(0,3).map((p,i)=>(
            <div key={i} style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",padding:"12px 16px",background:C.g2,borderRadius:10,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}>
                <span style={{fontSize:20}}>📜</span>
                <div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>Certificado Nivel {user.level}</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{p.date}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span style={{background:`${C.lime}15`,color:C.lime,fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,padding:"3px 10px",borderRadius:99}}>EMITIDO</span>
            </div>
          ))}
          {(db.payments||[]).filter(p=>p.userId===user.id&&p.type==="plan").length===0&&(
            <div style={{textAlign:"center",color:C.tf,fontSize:12,padding:24}}>No hay certificados aún. Completa un nivel para obtener el tuyo.</div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ PROGRESS VIEW (WITH RECHARTS) ════════════════════════════
function ProgressView({user,db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[period,setPeriod]=useState("30d");

  const xpData=[
    {day:"1 Mar",xp:820,sessions:2},{day:"5 Mar",xp:970,sessions:3},
    {day:"8 Mar",xp:1050,sessions:1},{day:"10 Mar",xp:1180,sessions:4},
    {day:"12 Mar",xp:1340,sessions:2},{day:"14 Mar",xp:1420,sessions:3},
    {day:"16 Mar",xp:1590,sessions:5},{day:"18 Mar",xp:1720,sessions:2},
    {day:"20 Mar",xp:1850,sessions:3},{day:"22 Mar",xp:2050,sessions:6},
    {day:"24 Mar",xp:2200,sessions:2},{day:"26 Mar",xp:2380,sessions:4},
    {day:"28 Mar",xp:2520,sessions:3},{day:"30 Mar",xp:2700,sessions:5},
  ];

  const subjectData=[
    {name:"Inglés Gral",value:45,fill:C.p},{name:"Business",value:25,fill:C.teal},
    {name:"IELTS",value:15,fill:C.gold},{name:"Pronunciación",value:15,fill:C.red},
  ];

  const weeklyData=[
    {day:"Lun",min:25,sessions:1},{day:"Mar",min:40,sessions:2},{day:"Mie",min:15,sessions:1},
    {day:"Jue",min:55,sessions:3},{day:"Vie",min:30,sessions:2},{day:"Sab",min:60,sessions:4},{day:"Dom",min:10,sessions:1},
  ];

  const levOrder={"A1":1,"A2":2,"B1":3,"B2":4,"C1":5,"C2":6};
  const nextLevel=Object.keys(levOrder).find(k=>levOrder[k]===((levOrder[user.level]||1)+1));
  const curLvData=db.levels?.find(l=>l.code===user.level);
  const nextLvData=db.levels?.find(l=>l.code===nextLevel);
  const xpToNext=nextLvData?(nextLvData.xpReq-user.xp):0;
  const xpPct=nextLvData?Math.min(100,Math.round((user.xp-(curLvData?.xpReq||0))/(nextLvData.xpReq-(curLvData?.xpReq||0))*100)):100;

  const CT={fill:C.t,stroke:C.b};

  return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?20:26,fontWeight:900}}>📈 Mi Progreso</div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(4,1fr)",gap:12}}>
        {[["✨",user.xp.toLocaleString(),"XP Total",C.p],["🔥",user.streak,"Días seguidos",C.amber],["📚",db.lessons?.length||0,"Lecciones",C.teal],["🏆",user.level,"Nivel CEFR",C.gold]].map(([ic,v,l,c])=>(
          <div key={l} style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:"16px",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,textAlign:"center"}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:22}}>{ic}</div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,color:c}}>{v}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{l}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      {nextLevel&&(
        <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",marginBottom:10,flexWrap:"wrap",gap:6}}>
            <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13}}>Progreso hacia {nextLevel}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:12,color:C.p}}>{xpPct}% · {xpToNext.toLocaleString()} XP restantes</div>
          </div>
          <div style={{height:8,borderRadius:99,background:C.b,overflow:"hidden"}}>
            <div style={{height:"100%",borderRadius:99,background:`linear-gradient(90deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,width:`${xpPct}%`,transition:"width 1s"}}/>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"2fr 1fr",gap:16}}>
        <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:12}}>XP acumulado — últimos 30 días</div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={200}>
            <AreaChart data={xpData}>
              <defs>
                <linearGradient id="xpGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                  <stop offset="5%" stopColor={C.p} stopOpacity={0.3}/>
                  <stop offset="95%" stopColor={C.p} stopOpacity={0}/>
                </linearGradient>
              </defs>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke={C.b}/>
              <XAxis dataKey="day" tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:9}} tickLine={false}/>
              <YAxis tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:9}} tickLine={false} axisLine={false}/>
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:8,fontSize:11,color:C.t}} labelStyle={{color:C.p}}/>
              <Area type="monotone" dataKey="xp" stroke={C.p} fill="url(#xpGrad)" strokeWidth={2}/>
            </AreaChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
        <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:12}}>Por materia</div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={200}>
            <PieChart>
              <Pie data={subjectData} cx="50%" cy="50%" innerRadius={50} outerRadius={80} paddingAngle={3} dataKey="value">
                {subjectData.map((entry,i)=><Cell key={i} fill={entry.fill}/>)}
              </Pie>
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:8,fontSize:11,color:C.t}}/>
            </PieChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:4,marginTop:4}}>
            {subjectData.map(d=>(
              <div key={d.name} style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:10,color:C.tm}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,alignItems:"center"}}>
                  <div style={{width:8,height:8,borderRadius:2,background:d.fill}}/>
                  {d.name}
                </div>
                <span style={{fontWeight:700,color:d.fill}}>{d.value}%</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
        <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:12}}>Minutos de estudio esta semana</div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={160}>
          <BarChart data={weeklyData} barSize={sm?20:30}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke={C.b} vertical={false}/>
            <XAxis dataKey="day" tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:10}} tickLine={false}/>
            <YAxis tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:10}} tickLine={false} axisLine={false}/>
            <Tooltip contentStyle={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:8,fontSize:11,color:C.t}}/>
            <Bar dataKey="min" fill={C.p} radius={[5,5,0,0]}/>
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:14,fontWeight:800,marginBottom:10}}>Logros recientes</div>
        <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"repeat(2,1fr)":"repeat(4,1fr)",gap:10}}>
          {ALL_ACHIEVEMENTS.slice(0,4).map(ach=>{
            const rc={common:C.teal,rare:C.p,epic:C.gold,legendary:C.red};
            const earned=user.xp>=(ach.type==="xp"?ach.threshold:100);
            return (
              <div key={ach.id} style={{padding:"14px",borderRadius:12,border:`1px solid ${earned?rc[ach.rarity]:C.b}`,background:earned?`${rc[ach.rarity]}0e`:C.g2,opacity:earned?1:0.5,textAlign:"center"}}>
                <div style={{fontSize:26,marginBottom:4}}>{ach.icon}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,color:earned?rc[ach.rarity]:C.t}}>{ach.title}</div>
                <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf,marginTop:2}}>{ach.desc}</div>
                {earned&&<div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.lime,marginTop:3,fontWeight:700}}>+{ach.xpReward} XP</div>}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ DOCS VIEW — FULL API DOCUMENTATION ════════════════════════
function DocsView({user}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[section,setSection]=useState("overview");
  const[copied,setCopied]=useState("");

  const copy=(txt,id)=>{
    navigator.clipboard?.writeText(txt).catch(()=>{});
    setCopied(id);setTimeout(()=>setCopied(""),2000);
  };

  const MC={GET:C.lime,POST:C.teal,PUT:C.gold,DELETE:C.red,PATCH:C.amber};

  const SECTIONS=[
    {id:"overview",label:"Visión General"},
    {id:"auth",label:"Autenticación"},
    {id:"endpoints",label:"Endpoints"},
    {id:"webhooks",label:"Webhooks"},

  const ERRORS=[
    {code:400,name:"Bad Request",desc:"Parámetros faltantes o inválidos en la petición"},
    {code:401,name:"Unauthorized",desc:"Token JWT ausente, inválido o expirado"},

  const CodeBlock=({code,id,lang="json"})=>(
    <div style={{position:"relative"}}>
      <pre style={{background:"#010108",border:`1px solid ${C.p}18`,borderRadius:10,padding:"12px 14px",fontSize:10,fontFamily:"'JetBrains Mono',monospace",color:lang==="json"?C.teal:C.lime,margin:0,overflowX:"auto",lineHeight:1.7}}>
        {code}
      </pre>
      <button onClick={()=>copy(code,id)} style={{position:"absolute",top:6,right:6,background:`${C.p}20`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}30`,borderRadius:6,color:copied===id?C.lime:C.p,fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer",padding:"3px 8px"}}>
        {copied===id?"✓ Copiado":"Copiar"}
      </button>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",gap:sm?0:20,flexDirection:sm?"column":"row"}}>
      {!sm&&(
        <div style={{width:180,flexShrink:0}}>

function HelpCenter(){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[openFaq,setOpenFaq]=useState(null);
  const[search,setSearch]=useState("");

  const FAQS=[
    {q:"Como instalo la app en mi celular?",a:"Android: Chrome → menu (tres puntos) → Instalar app. iOS Safari: boton Compartir (cuadrado con flecha) → Agregar a pantalla de inicio. La app funciona offline y recibe notificaciones."},
    {q:"Como funciona el pago con Wompi?",a:"Wompi es el procesador de pagos lideres en Colombia. Acepta PSE, Nequi, Bancolombia, Daviplata, tarjetas Visa/Mastercard y Efecty. El pago es 100% seguro, regulado por la Superfinanciera."},
    {q:"Que diferencia hay entre los planes de hosting?",a:"Starter es gratis y tiene 500MB. Academico (49.900/mes) tiene 5GB. Profesional (89.900/mes) tiene 20GB y API key. Academia (189.900/mes) tiene 100GB y hasta 10 usuarios. Cada plan incluye subdominio propio en speakfluently.co."},
    {q:"Como funciona el Tutor IA?",a:"El Tutor IA usa Claude (Anthropic) para darte retroalimentacion personalizada en tiempo real. Puedes practicar conversacion, pedir correcciones gramaticales, prepararte para IELTS o practicar pronunciacion."},
    {q:"Como obtengo mi certificado CEFR?",a:"Ve a Certificados en el menu. Selecciona el nivel que hayas completado y genera tu certificado PDF. El certificado incluye tu nombre, nivel CEFR (A1-C2), fecha y un ID de verificacion unico."},
    {q:"Puedo cambiar de plan en cualquier momento?",a:"Si, puedes hacer upgrade inmediatamente desde Planes y Pagos. Para downgrade, el cambio aplica al inicio del siguiente periodo de facturacion. No hay penalizaciones por cancelar."},
    {q:"Como configuro mi subdominio?",a:"Al adquirir un plan de pago, se crea automaticamente un subdominio {tu-usuario}.speakfluently.co con SSL incluido. Puedes ver y gestionar tus subdominios en Mi Hosting."},
    {q:"Como uso la API desde mi aplicacion?",a:"Genera tu API key en Perfil → API Keys. Usa el endpoint POST /auth/login para obtener un JWT y luego incluye el header Authorization: Bearer {token} en cada peticion. Revisa la documentacion completa en Dev → SDK."},
    {q:"Cuanto tiempo dura el acceso a las clases grabadas?",a:"Con plan Academico o superior, tienes acceso permanente a todas las grabaciones. Con plan Starter, accedes a las grabaciones durante 30 dias desde la sesion."},

  ].filter(f=>!search||f.q.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())||f.a.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()));

  const GUIDES=[
    {icon:"🚀",title:"Primeros pasos",desc:"Guia completa para estudiantes nuevos",time:"5 min"},

  ];

  return (

      <div style={{position:"relative"}}>
        <input value={search} onChange={e=>setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Buscar en la ayuda..." style={{width:"100%",padding:"12px 16px 12px 42px",background:C.g2,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:12,color:C.t,fontSize:13,boxSizing:"border-box"}}/>

      </div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(3,1fr)",gap:10}}>
        {GUIDES.map(g=>(
          <div key={g.title} style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:12,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,cursor:"pointer",transition:"all .15s"}}
            onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.p}
            onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=C.b}>
            <div style={{fontSize:24,marginBottom:6}}>{g.icon}</div>
            <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:12,marginBottom:3}}>{g.title}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,marginBottom:6}}>{g.desc}</div>
            <span style={{fontSize:9,color:C.p}}>{g.time} lectura</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:15,fontWeight:800,marginBottom:12}}>Preguntas Frecuentes</div>
        {FAQS.length===0&&<div style={{color:C.tf,fontSize:12,textAlign:"center",padding:20}}>No se encontraron resultados para tu busqueda.</div>}
        {FAQS.map((f,i)=>(
          <div key={i} style={{marginBottom:8,background:C.g2,borderRadius:12,border:`1px solid ${openFaq===i?C.p:C.b}`,overflow:"hidden",transition:"border .15s"}}>
            <div onClick={()=>setOpenFaq(openFaq===i?null:i)} style={{padding:"14px 16px",cursor:"pointer",display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",gap:10}}>
              <div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,color:C.t}}>{f.q}</div>
              <div style={{fontSize:16,color:C.p,flexShrink:0,transform:openFaq===i?"rotate(180deg)":"",transition:"transform .2s"}}>▾</div>
            </div>
            {openFaq===i&&<div style={{padding:"0 16px 14px",fontSize:12,color:C.tm,lineHeight:1.7,borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}><br/>{f.a}</div>}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:18,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",gap:14,alignItems:"center",flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
        <div style={{fontSize:32}}>💬</div>
        <div style={{flex:1}}>
          <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:3}}>Soporte directo</div>
          <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tm}}>Lunes a Viernes 8am-6pm Colombia</div>
        </div>
        <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,flexWrap:"wrap"}}>
          <Btn sm clr={C.lime} onClick={()=>window.open("https://wa.me/573000000000","_blank")}>📱 WhatsApp</Btn>
          <Btn sm outline onClick={()=>window.open("mailto:fundacionstudy@gmail.com","_blank")}>📧 Email</Btn>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ ADMIN ANALYTICS EXPANDED ═════════════════════════════════
function AdminAnalytics({db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();

  const revenueData=[
    {mes:"Oct",ingresos:820000,usuarios:38},{mes:"Nov",ingresos:1240000,usuarios:52},
    {mes:"Dic",ingresos:1580000,usuarios:61},{mes:"Ene",ingresos:980000,usuarios:45},
    {mes:"Feb",ingresos:1820000,usuarios:73},{mes:"Mar",ingresos:2340000,usuarios:89},
  ];

  const planDist=[
    {name:"Starter",value:45,fill:"#64748b"},{name:"Academico",value:28,fill:"#22d3ee"},
    {name:"Profesional",value:16,fill:"#7850ff"},{name:"Academia",value:7,fill:"#ffd04d"},
    {name:"Profesor Pro",value:4,fill:"#3cffa0"},
  ];

  const weekActivity=[
    {dia:"Lun",sesiones:12,lecciones:48,pagos:3},{dia:"Mar",sesiones:18,lecciones:72,pagos:5},
    {dia:"Mie",sesiones:9,lecciones:36,pagos:2},{dia:"Jue",sesiones:22,lecciones:88,pagos:8},
    {dia:"Vie",sesiones:15,lecciones:60,pagos:6},{dia:"Sab",sesiones:28,lecciones:112,pagos:4},
    {dia:"Dom",sesiones:8,lecciones:32,pagos:1},
  ];

  const totalRevenue=db.payments?.filter(p=>p.status==="APPROVED").reduce((s,p)=>s+p.amount,0)||0;
  const activeUsers=db.users?.filter(u=>u.active).length||0;
  const storageUsed=db.users?.reduce((s,u)=>s+u.storageUsed,0)||0;
  const storageTotal=db.serverStats?.totalStorage||500000;

  return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:18}}>
      <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?20:26,fontWeight:900}}>📊 Analytics & Estadísticas</div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr 1fr":"repeat(4,1fr)",gap:12}}>
        {[["💰",`$${(totalRevenue/1000000).toFixed(2)}M`,"Ingresos COP",C.lime],[`👥`,activeUsers,"Usuarios Activos",C.teal],["📈",`${db.payments?.filter(p=>p.status==="APPROVED").length||0}`,"Pagos Aprobados",C.gold],["☁️",`${Math.round(storageUsed/storageTotal*100)}%`,"Disco Usado",C.p]].map(([ic,v,l,c])=>(
          <div key={l} style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,textAlign:"center"}}>
            <div style={{fontSize:22}}>{ic}</div>
            <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:22,fontWeight:900,color:c}}>{v}</div>
            <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf}}>{l}</div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"grid",gridTemplateColumns:sm?"1fr":"2fr 1fr",gap:16}}>
        <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:12}}>Ingresos y Usuarios — últimos 6 meses</div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={220}>
            <AreaChart data={revenueData}>
              <defs>
                <linearGradient id="revGrad" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                  <stop offset="5%" stopColor={C.lime} stopOpacity={0.25}/>
                  <stop offset="95%" stopColor={C.lime} stopOpacity={0}/>
                </linearGradient>
              </defs>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke={C.b}/>
              <XAxis dataKey="mes" tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:10}} tickLine={false}/>
              <YAxis yAxisId="left" tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:9}} tickLine={false} axisLine={false} tickFormatter={v=>`$${(v/1000000).toFixed(1)}M`}/>
              <YAxis yAxisId="right" orientation="right" tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:9}} tickLine={false} axisLine={false}/>
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:8,fontSize:11,color:C.t}} formatter={(v,n)=>n==="ingresos"?[`$${v.toLocaleString()} COP`,n]:[v,n]}/>
              <Area yAxisId="left" type="monotone" dataKey="ingresos" stroke={C.lime} fill="url(#revGrad)" strokeWidth={2}/>
              <Line yAxisId="right" type="monotone" dataKey="usuarios" stroke={C.teal} strokeWidth={2} dot={false}/>
            </AreaChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>
        <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
          <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:12}}>Distribución de Planes</div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={180}>
            <PieChart>
              <Pie data={planDist} cx="50%" cy="50%" outerRadius={75} paddingAngle={2} dataKey="value">
                {planDist.map((e,i)=><Cell key={i} fill={e.fill}/>)}
              </Pie>
              <Tooltip contentStyle={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:8,fontSize:11}}/>
            </PieChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
          <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:4}}>
            {planDist.map(d=>(
              <div key={d.name} style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",fontSize:10,color:C.tm}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:5,alignItems:"center"}}><div style={{width:8,height:8,borderRadius:2,background:d.fill}}/>{d.name}</div>
                <span style={{fontWeight:700,color:d.fill}}>{d.value}%</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{background:C.g2,borderRadius:14,padding:16,border:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
        <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:13,marginBottom:12}}>Actividad semanal</div>
        <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={180}>
          <BarChart data={weekActivity} barSize={sm?16:22}>
            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke={C.b} vertical={false}/>
            <XAxis dataKey="dia" tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:10}} tickLine={false}/>
            <YAxis tick={{fill:C.tf,fontSize:9}} tickLine={false} axisLine={false}/>
            <Tooltip contentStyle={{background:C.g3,border:`1px solid ${C.b2}`,borderRadius:8,fontSize:11,color:C.t}}/>
            <Bar dataKey="sesiones" name="Sesiones" fill={C.p} radius={[4,4,0,0]}/>
            <Bar dataKey="pagos" name="Pagos" fill={C.lime} radius={[4,4,0,0]}/>
          </BarChart>
        </ResponsiveContainer>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ══════════ NOTIFICATIONS VIEW ════════════════════════════════════════
function NotificationsView({user,db}){
  const C=useC();const{sm}=useRWD();
  const[filter,setFilter]=useState("all");
  const[read,setRead]=useState(new Set());

  const notifs=[
    {id:1,type:"payment",icon:"💳",title:"Pago aprobado",body:"Tu pago de $89,900 COP por Plan Profesional fue aprobado. ¡Bienvenido!",time:"Hace 2h",unread:true},
    {id:2,type:"lesson",icon:"🎓",title:"Lección completada",body:"Completaste 'Present Perfect' +100 XP. Vas muy bien, sigue asi!",time:"Hace 4h",unread:true},
    {id:3,type:"session",icon:"📹",title:"Sesion live en 30 min",body:"Club de Conversacion: Viajes comienza a las 18:00. Tu enlace: meet.google.com/xyz",time:"Hace 5h",unread:true},
    {id:4,type:"achievement",icon:"🏆",title:"Nuevo logro desbloqueado",body:"Obtuviste 'Semana Perfecta' por 7 dias seguidos estudiando. +350 XP",time:"Hace 1d",unread:false},
    {id:5,type:"announce",icon:"📢",title:"Nuevas clases IELTS disponibles",body:"A partir del 10 de marzo sesiones intensivas IELTS. Cupos limitados a 8 estudiantes.",time:"Hace 2d",unread:false},
    {id:6,type:"system",icon:"⚡",title:"Tu plan se renueva pronto",body:"Tu plan Profesional se renueva el 6 de abril. Verifica tu metodo de pago.",time:"Hace 3d",unread:false},
  ];

  const filtered=notifs.filter(n=>filter==="all"||(filter==="unread"&&n.unread&&!read.has(n.id)));
  const unreadCount=notifs.filter(n=>n.unread&&!read.has(n.id)).length;

  const markRead=(id)=>setRead(s=>{const ns=new Set(s);ns.add(id);return ns;});
  const markAllRead=()=>setRead(new Set(notifs.map(n=>n.id)));

  const TYPE_C={payment:C.lime,lesson:C.p,session:C.teal,achievement:C.gold,announce:C.amber,system:C.red};

  return (
    <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:14}}>
      <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:10}}>
        <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontSize:sm?18:22,fontWeight:900}}>🔔 Notificaciones {unreadCount>0&&<span style={{background:C.red,color:"#fff",fontSize:11,padding:"2px 8px",borderRadius:99,marginLeft:6}}>{unreadCount}</span>}</div>
        {unreadCount>0&&<button onClick={markAllRead} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.p,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:11,fontWeight:700}}>Marcar todas como leídas</button>}
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",gap:6}}>
        {[["all","Todas"],["unread","Sin leer"]].map(([v,l])=>(
          <button key={v} onClick={()=>setFilter(v)} style={{padding:"6px 14px",borderRadius:99,border:`1px solid ${filter===v?C.p:C.b}`,background:filter===v?`${C.p}18`:"transparent",color:filter===v?C.p:C.tm,fontSize:11,fontWeight:700,cursor:"pointer"}}>{l}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",gap:8}}>
        {filtered.map(n=>{
          const isRead=read.has(n.id)||!n.unread;
          return (
            <div key={n.id} onClick={()=>markRead(n.id)} style={{display:"flex",gap:12,padding:"14px 16px",background:isRead?C.g2:`${TYPE_C[n.type]||C.p}08`,borderRadius:12,border:`1px solid ${isRead?C.b:`${TYPE_C[n.type]||C.p}25`}`,cursor:"pointer",transition:"all .15s"}}
              onMouseEnter={e=>e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=TYPE_C[n.type]||C.p}
              onMouseLeave={e=>e.currentTarget.style.borderColor=isRead?C.b:`${TYPE_C[n.type]||C.p}25`}>
              <div style={{width:40,height:40,borderRadius:12,background:`${TYPE_C[n.type]||C.p}18`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:18,flexShrink:0}}>
                {n.icon}
              </div>
              <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"flex-start",gap:8}}>
                  <div style={{fontWeight:700,fontSize:12,color:isRead?C.tm:C.t}}>{n.title}</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tf,flexShrink:0}}>{n.time}</div>
                </div>
                <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginTop:3,lineHeight:1.5}}>{n.body}</div>
              </div>
              {!isRead&&<div style={{width:8,height:8,borderRadius:"50%",background:TYPE_C[n.type]||C.p,flexShrink:0,marginTop:4}}/>}
            </div>
          );
        })}
        {filtered.length===0&&<div style={{textAlign:"center",color:C.tf,fontSize:12,padding:32}}>Sin notificaciones {filter==="unread"?"sin leer":""}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

      case "dev-apis": return <AdminAPIs db={db} upd={upd}/>;
      case "dev-webhooks": return <DevWompiSandbox/>;
      case "dev-youtube": return <YouTubeView user={user} db={db}/>;
      case "dev-logs": return <DevLogsView/>;
      case "dev-wompi": return <DevWompiSandbox/>;
      default: return <StudentDash {...p}/>;
    }
  };

  const items=NAV_ITEMS_FULL[user?.role]||NAV_ITEMS_FULL.student;

  if(!user) return (
    <TC.Provider value={C}>
      <AuthPage db={db} upd={upd} onLogin={login} isDark={dark} toggleTheme={()=>setDark(d=>!d)}/>
    </TC.Provider>
  );

  return (
    <TC.Provider value={C}>
      <GCss C={C}/>
      <div style={{position:"fixed",inset:0,pointerEvents:"none",overflow:"hidden",zIndex:0}}>
        <div style={{position:"absolute",top:"-18%",left:"8%",width:520,height:520,borderRadius:"50%",background:`radial-gradient(${C.p}14,transparent 70%)`,filter:"blur(80px)"}}/>
        <div style={{position:"absolute",bottom:"-12%",right:"4%",width:420,height:420,borderRadius:"50%",background:`radial-gradient(${C.teal}10,transparent 70%)`,filter:"blur(80px)"}}/>
      </div>
      <div style={{display:"flex",minHeight:"100vh",position:"relative",zIndex:1}}>
        {!sm&&(
          <div style={{width:224,flexShrink:0,position:"fixed",top:0,left:0,height:"100vh",background:C.g1,borderRight:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",zIndex:100,overflowY:"auto"}} className="noscroll">
            <div style={{padding:"20px 16px 12px",display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:10,borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{width:34,height:34,borderRadius:9,background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p},${C.teal})`,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center",fontSize:16}}>✨</div>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontWeight:900,fontSize:13}}>Speak Fluently</div>
              <TBtn isDark={dark} toggle={()=>setDark(d=>!d)}/>
            </div>
            <div style={{padding:"10px 8px",flex:1}}>
              {items.map(it=>{
                const a=tab===it.id;
                return (
                  <button key={it.id} onClick={()=>setTab(it.id)} style={{display:"flex",gap:9,alignItems:"center",width:"100%",padding:"8px 10px",border:"none",borderRadius:9,background:a?`${C.p}18`:"transparent",color:a?C.p:C.tm,fontSize:11,fontWeight:a?700:500,cursor:"pointer",marginBottom:2,transition:"all .15s",textAlign:"left",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>
                    <span style={{fontSize:15}}>{it.ic}</span>{it.l}
                    {it.id==="notifications"&&<span style={{marginLeft:"auto",background:C.red,color:"#fff",fontSize:9,fontWeight:700,padding:"1px 5px",borderRadius:99}}>3</span>}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <div style={{padding:"10px 8px",borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8,padding:"8px 10px",marginBottom:6}}>
                <AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={28} clr={C.p}/>
                <div style={{flex:1,minWidth:0}}>
                  <div style={{fontSize:10,fontWeight:700,overflow:"hidden",textOverflow:"ellipsis",whiteSpace:"nowrap"}}>{user.name}</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:9,color:C.tf}}>{user.role} · {user.level}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={logout} style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8,padding:"7px 10px",width:"100%",border:"none",background:"transparent",color:C.red,fontSize:11,fontWeight:600,cursor:"pointer",borderRadius:8,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>
                🚪 Cerrar sesión
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {sm&&sideOpen&&(
          <div style={{position:"fixed",inset:0,zIndex:200}}>
            <div onClick={()=>setSide(false)} style={{position:"absolute",inset:0,background:"rgba(0,0,0,.7)"}}/>
            <div style={{position:"absolute",left:0,top:0,bottom:0,width:240,background:C.g1,borderRight:`1px solid ${C.b}`,overflowY:"auto",display:"flex",flexDirection:"column"}} className="noscroll">
              <div style={{padding:"18px 14px",display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontWeight:900,fontSize:13}}>✨ Speak Fluently</div>
                <button onClick={()=>setSide(false)} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.tm,fontSize:20,cursor:"pointer"}}>✕</button>
              </div>
              <div style={{padding:"10px 8px",flex:1}}>
                {items.map(it=>{
                  const a=tab===it.id;
                  return (
                    <button key={it.id} onClick={()=>{setTab(it.id);setSide(false);}} style={{display:"flex",gap:9,alignItems:"center",width:"100%",padding:"10px",border:"none",borderRadius:9,background:a?`${C.p}18`:"transparent",color:a?C.p:C.tm,fontSize:12,fontWeight:a?700:500,cursor:"pointer",marginBottom:3,transition:"all .15s",textAlign:"left",fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>
                      <span style={{fontSize:16}}>{it.ic}</span>{it.l}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
              <div style={{padding:"10px 8px",borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
                <button onClick={logout} style={{display:"flex",alignItems:"center",gap:8,padding:"10px",width:"100%",border:"none",background:"transparent",color:C.red,fontSize:12,fontWeight:600,cursor:"pointer",borderRadius:8,fontFamily:"'Plus Jakarta Sans',sans-serif"}}>
                  🚪 Cerrar sesión
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        <main style={{flex:1,marginLeft:sm?0:224,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",minHeight:"100vh",background:C.bg,transition:"margin .3s"}}>
          {sm?(
            <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",padding:"14px 16px",background:C.g1,borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`,position:"sticky",top:0,zIndex:90}}>
              <button onClick={()=>setSide(true)} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.t,fontSize:22,cursor:"pointer",lineHeight:1}}>☰</button>
              <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontWeight:800,fontSize:13}}>✨ {TAB_TITLE_FULL[tab]||"Speak Fluently"}</div>
              <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
                <TBtn isDark={dark} toggle={()=>setDark(d=>!d)}/>
                <AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={28} clr={C.p}/>
              </div>
            </div>
          ):(
            <div style={{padding:"24px 32px 0",position:"sticky",top:0,background:C.bg,zIndex:90,borderBottom:`1px solid ${C.b}`}}>
              <div style={{display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",paddingBottom:16}}>
                <div>
                  <div style={{fontFamily:"'Syne',sans-serif",fontWeight:900,fontSize:22}}>{TAB_TITLE_FULL[tab]||"Speak Fluently"}</div>
                  <div style={{fontSize:11,color:C.tf,marginTop:2}}>Speak Fluently Academy · {user.name} · {user.role}</div>
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:8,alignItems:"center"}}>
                  <button onClick={()=>setTab("notifications")} style={{background:"none",border:`1px solid ${C.b}`,borderRadius:9,padding:"6px 10px",color:C.t,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:14,position:"relative"}}>
                    🔔<span style={{position:"absolute",top:-4,right:-4,background:C.red,color:"#fff",borderRadius:"50%",fontSize:8,width:14,height:14,display:"flex",alignItems:"center",justifyContent:"center"}}>3</span>
                  </button>
                  <AvatarCircle name={user.name} size={34} clr={C.p}/>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          <div key={tab} className="afu" style={{flex:1,padding:sm?"14px 12px 96px":"20px 32px 60px"}}>
            {canInstall&&!installed&&(
              <div style={{background:`linear-gradient(135deg,${C.p}18,${C.teal}10)`,border:`1px solid ${C.p}28`,borderRadius:14,padding:"12px 16px",marginBottom:16,display:"flex",justifyContent:"space-between",alignItems:"center",flexWrap:"wrap",gap:8}}>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:10,alignItems:"center"}}>
                  <span style={{fontSize:22}}>📱</span>
                  <div><div style={{fontSize:12,fontWeight:700}}>Instala Speak Fluently como app</div><div style={{fontSize:10,color:C.tm}}>Acceso rápido, modo offline y notificaciones</div></div>
                </div>
                <div style={{display:"flex",gap:7}}>
                  <Btn sm onClick={install} clr={C.p}>Instalar App</Btn>
                  <button onClick={()=>window.dispatchEvent(new Event("dismissPWA"))} style={{background:"none",border:"none",color:C.tf,cursor:"pointer",fontSize:16,padding:"0 4px"}}>✕</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            {render()}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
      {sm&&(
        <div style={{position:"fixed",bottom:0,left:0,right:0,background:C.g1,borderTop:`1px solid ${C.b}`,display:"flex",zIndex:150,paddingBottom:"env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0)"}}>
          {(items.slice(0,5)).map(it=>{
            const a=tab===it.id;
            return (
              <button key={it.id} onClick={()=>setTab(it.id)} style={{flex:1,padding:"10px 4px",border:"none",background:"transparent",color:a?C.p:C.tf,display:"flex",flexDirection:"column",alignItems:"center",gap:2,cursor:"pointer",transition:"color .15s"}}>
                <span style={{fontSize:18}}>{it.ic}</span>
                <span style={{fontSize:8,fontWeight:a?700:500,letterSpacing:.3}}>{it.l.length>7?it.l.slice(0,7)+"...":it.l}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </TC.Provider>
  );
}
